## NOTE 275 — public-profile EDIT view breaks two Nov 18 founder rulings — FIXED (fleet/pubprofile-edit-grouping off origin/staging @2b071034, 2026-11-19, Scout NOTE 275) — DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Root cause:** the public-only edit view (`public_profile_edit_screen`) renders the `GET /profile/bullets` payload **verbatim** (no grouping of its own). That payload (`get_bullets` → `person_graph_source.story_bullets` → `render.story_sections`) is the raw OWNER view (`drop_header_fields=False`), so it bypasses the public placement rule the RENDERED profile applies (`public_sections` → `drop_header_fields=True`). So the edit view showed header facts as About Me bullets and left "Interested in" under About Me — while the rendered profile is correct.
- **Defect 1 (header facts in About Me edit view):** FIXED. **Defect 2 (no What I'm Looking For section; "Interested in" under About Me):** FIXED. Both by giving the edit view the same rule.
- **Fix (backend only; the Flutter edit screen is unchanged):** new `person_graph_render.public_edit_sections(graph)` = `story_sections(graph, drop_header_fields=True)` — the public placement rule (drop header facts, relocate "Interested in" to `looking_for`) applied to the PUBLIC bullets, while PRIVATE bullets stay in place (`_public_fact_placement` returns "keep" for them) so the editor's section-PUT save round-trip never drops them. Threaded via a new `public_edit` flag: `get_bullets(db, uid, public_edit=True)` ← the `GET /profile/bullets` route only; `person_graph_source.story_bullets(..., public_edit=True)`. The person-graph **net** (`profile_graph.get_graph`/`public_graph`, the other `get_bullets` consumer) and **Your Story** (`story_sections`, flag off) are untouched.
- **Defect 3 (Career LinkedIn link absent): intentional, confirmed.** The LinkedIn URL is a profile-doc field (`schema.PF_LINKEDIN_URL`, `career_timeline`) surfaced as a public link button, NOT a story/graph bullet, so it is correctly outside the bullet editor (which edits story bullets). No action.
- **Defect 4 (two DEBUG ANRs in the edit text fields): report-only.** The TextField `onChanged` → `_markDirty` does a one-time `setState` only (`if (!_dirty)`), no per-keystroke recompute or section rebuild — the path is clean. The ANRs correlate with adb input injection, not real work (the u2 set_text path never ANR'd); Scout owes a keyboard-typed repro before this grades as a defect.
- **Tests:** `test_person_graph_render.py::test_note275_public_edit_sections_match_render_but_keep_private` (NEW — the live Daniel fixture: header facts gone from About Me, "Interested in" under looking_for, private bullet preserved, Your Story unchanged), `test_profile_bullets.py::test_get_bullets_forwards_public_edit_to_graph_source` (NEW — flag threading), updated one monkeypatch stub. Backend suites green. No Flutter change, no new route (layout matrix unaffected). Inference untouched (100% LiveKit).

## STARTUP AUDIT P2 BATCH — five Phase-2 startup-audit follow-ups — FIXED (fleet/startup-audit-p2 off origin/staging @50b40804, 2026-11-19, Astro heartbeat) — DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Source:** `docs/perf/phase2-startup-audit.md` (branch `fleet/startup-audit-antigravity @0c1e4f3f`); five P2 chunks in order, chunk 6 (doc/spec cleanup + cold-start budget restatement) deliberately skipped — the budget restatement needs a founder decision.
- **1 — Coalesce the double bootstrap (`api_client.dart`):** `ApiClient.bootstrap` now shares one in-flight `POST /bootstrap` across concurrent identical calls (key = full request identity; cleared on settle, so it coalesces, never caches). Kills the two POSTs an optimistic cached launch sent (splash cached-route branch + DAG N14 both fire the identical empty bootstrap). Test: distinct payloads (e.g. a practice-return) each still get their own POST.
- **2 — Paywall promotion marks N11 critical (`launch_intent_resolver.dart`):** the dead `if (…) { // ensure critical }` block is replaced by a real rebuild of `N11:revenuecat_init` as `critical: true` (the field is final → reconstruct same id/deps/action/timeout/fallback). A failed/timed-out RevenueCat init now blocks `N12` instead of routing to a degraded paywall. Test: a failed N11 makes the dag run error and N12 never routes (and the control: without the promotion a non-critical N11 failure does NOT block N12).
- **3 — N14 carries the gate hints (`startup_nodes.dart`):** the refreshed `BootConfig` now includes `biometricConsentRequiredHint`/`ageBlockedHint`/`recoveryRequiredHint` (mirroring the splash's `_cacheEntryRoute`), so an N14 refresh no longer erases the hints. Test runs the real N14 action with a fake API + `ReportCache` round-trip.
- **4 — Splash auth-wait timeout prefers the cached route over sign-in (`splash_screen.dart` + `report_cache.dart`):** on a null-`currentUser` 2s timeout with a cached `BootConfig` on the device (new `ReportCache.readAnyBootConfig`), the splash paints the last-known route; a recovery helper then waits longer for auth and runs the background bootstrap, correcting to `/signin` honestly (via the captured router — it outlives the disposed splash; I caught and fixed a `mounted`-guard that would otherwise strand a tokenless user on the shell) only if auth never restores. A clean sign-out wiped the cache, so a signed-out user still lands on sign-in. Tests: cached→route / no-cache→signin / cached-but-never-restores→signin.
- **5 — `ensureAuthReady` re-arms on identity change (`auth_service.dart`):** `notifyAuthSessionRestored` re-arms the readiness completer whenever the signed-in uid changes (sign-out / account switch); an idempotent same-uid repeat is a no-op. Was single-use (returned the first user forever — latent, no current post-switch caller).
- **Quality:** all five have tests (`api_client_test`, `launch_intent_resolver_test`, `n14_boot_config_hints_test` NEW, `splash_auth_wait_recovery_test` NEW, `auth_service_ready_rearm_test` NEW); `flutter analyze` 0; startup suites + layout matrix green. No new route. Inference untouched (100% LiveKit). Docs: ARCHITECTURE.md §11.6c startup-audit hardening.

## NOTE 274 — fresh-install sign-in impossible on 2526 + PROFILE first-launch Firebase-init race — FIXED (fleet/signin-consent-hitbox off origin/staging @a8535710, 2026-11-19, Scout NOTE 274) — DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **P0 — no new user can sign in (both variants):** on a fresh install both clickwrap boxes are unticked, so every auth button was `onPressed: null` = **disabled = a silent no-op** — a new user tapped dead buttons with no feedback. Measured in a probe at the device's real metrics: there is **no geometric overlap** between the consent tiles and the buttons (a `Column` guarantees it); the uiautomator "overlap" is a semantics-rect artifact of the scroll view, worsened at the device's font scale where the long biometric consent text grows tall and the real buttons scroll below the fold, so a tap where the button "appears" lands on the (tall) consent tile and toggles face-recognition consent. **Fix (`sign_in_screen.dart`):** every auth affordance (Apple/Google/Email/Phone + the email & phone submit buttons) is now **always enabled**; a tap before both consents are ticked calls a new `_requireConsents()` that shows a nudge toast ("tick both boxes above…") and does nothing else — the affirmative-consent gate is unchanged, only its feedback is, so the buttons now respond at their centres and edges and a button tap always hits the button (never a consent tile behind it). Also trimmed the hero-orb glow padding 32→20 so the buttons sit higher and stay on-screen at larger font scales.
- **P3 — PROFILE fresh-install ANR + "No Firebase App DEFAULT has been created" → dead Page Not Found:** the router auth gate read `FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser` **unconditionally**; on a cold fresh-install launch `N7:firebase_core` (3s timeout, init error swallowed to a `debugPrint`) can miss under ART-compilation pressure, so `FirebaseAuth.instance` throws `[core/no-app]` inside the go_router `redirect`, which surfaces as a GoException + a dead "Page Not Found" (force-stop relaunch is warm/clean). **Fix (`app_router.dart`):** the gate is extracted to a pure, unit-testable `authGateRedirect(loc)` that guards the Firebase read (`Firebase.apps.isNotEmpty` + try/catch) and falls back to the synchronous `AuthSessionState`; the `refreshListenable` re-runs the gate the moment the auth listener ticks, so no signed-in user is stranded.
- **P2 — DEBUG first launch dies silently by 120s (report-only):** DEBUG-only (2nd/3rd launches clean; PROFILE never died). It is the debug-JIT cold-start artifact — the process is reaped during the long debug baseline compile while MainActivity is paused (NOTE 239 Leg 4 family, amplified by JIT); it is OS process-reaping, not a Dart exception, so no Dart-level fix applies and AOT/profile (what ships) is clean. The P3 redirect hardening covers the real fresh-install death. No code change — consistent with the standing "don't ship a fix for debug-only boot artifacts" finding.
- **Tests:** `sign_in_screen_test.dart` (+2: auth buttons stay enabled and respond before consent — a tap nudges, never toggles face-recognition consent, never signs in/opens a form; Google proceeds once consent is given), `auth_gate_redirect_test.dart` (NEW, 3: Firebase-down signed-out → sign-in with no core/no-app throw; open + share routes stay reachable; a signed-in `AuthSessionState` is not stranded). `flutter analyze` 0. No new route, so no app_registry/layout_targets change. Inference untouched (100% LiveKit).

## PUBLIC-PROFILE-EDIT — the My public profile pencil opens a PUBLIC-ONLY edit view — DONE (fleet/pubprofile-public-edit off origin/staging @4a9a0393, 2026-11-19, founder order) — DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Order (founder, Nov 19 ~16:32 PT):** pressing edit on My public profile used to open the full Your Story editor; now it opens an edit view showing ONLY the public elements the public profile renders, each editable in place, plus an add-note that creates a new public note. No private or Ayma-only content anywhere in that view.
- **New screen `PublicProfileEditScreen` (`/profile/me/public/edit`):** loads `GET /profile/bullets`, keeps sections with ≥1 PUBLIC bullet (grouped/ordered exactly as the profile renders them), each public bullet in an editable field with a per-section **Add a note** (new bullet, `visibility: public`) and an in-place remove. Save → `PUT /profile/bullets/{pageId}` per CHANGED section only.
- **Same node, nothing dropped:** bullet id == graph node id (no copies). The PUT replaces the whole section, so the screen re-sends that section's PRIVATE bullets unchanged; the server re-attaches the Ayma-only bullets itself (`profile_bullets._preserved_ai_only`). Order preserved (edited public in place, private verbatim, new notes appended).
- **Pencil rewired:** `MyPublicProfileScreen._editManually` now pushes `PublicProfileEditScreen` (was `StoryEditorScreen`); tooltip "Edit public profile". The `story_editor_screen` import is removed from that file. The full Your Story editor is unchanged and still reached from Your Story — nothing dead left to delete.
- **Registration/robustness:** app_registry `edit_public_profile` entry; route in `app_router`; `layout_targets` entry (layout matrix stays green). Tour: no new entry — this is a refined edit entry point for an existing feature (editing your public profile), not a new destination.
- **Tests:** `public_profile_edit_screen_test.dart` (+4 NEW: public-only + grouped, private/ai_only never shown; edit + add-note saves public(edited+new) and re-sends the private bullet unchanged; a removed public bullet drops but private survives; Save disabled until an edit), `my_public_profile_screen_test.dart` (pencil now opens the public-only editor, never Your Story). Backend `test_app_registry` 57 green; layout matrix (3 shards) + public_profile + app_registry 132 green; `flutter analyze lib/ test/` 0. Inference untouched (100% LiveKit).

## NOTE273-FRESH-INSTALL — build 2525, fresh-install authed fetches fail + 8-min "Waking up Ayma" stall + seeded-header race + profile signing-key mismatch — FIXED (fleet/note273-fresh-install off origin/staging @9b03a981, 2026-11-19, Scout NOTE 273) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Ruled out first (chat proves it):** chat history loaded 2211 messages in-app through the SAME app-wide biometric-consent gate and the SAME Bearer-token path — so token, consent, and Firebase project are all GOOD. The 2525 failures are NOT the NOTE 263 mixed-project 401 (the guard correctly stayed silent). Returning installs paint last-known caches (offline-first), so these live-fetch failures are only VISIBLE on a fresh install with no cache.
- **P1 — story photos "Couldn't load your photos":** `_loadPhotos` → `photoNodes()` was the one on-open load NOT wrapped in `withColdStartRetry` (story sections + chat already are), so the first scale-to-zero cold request hard-failed and the manual Retry re-hit the still-cold backend. Fix: wrap it in `withColdStartRetry` (`story_editor_screen.dart`). It rides the cold start like the sibling loads.
- **P1 — Plan "Couldn't load your plan":** the only uncaught throw in the Plan load was the STORE layer — `_rc.ensureReady()` (outside the try) and `rc.activeSubscription()` — which throw on an ad-hoc profile build that carries no RevenueCat key (not Codemagic-signed). Fix: the store layer never blocks the plan — `ensureReady()` wrapped in try/catch (`plan_screen.dart`); `SubscriptionStatus.load` catches a thrown `activeSubscription()` and falls through to the backend plan/usage, and the backend `usage()` now rides the cold start via `withColdStartRetry` (`subscription_status.dart`, honouring its "Never throws" contract). On a genuinely-down backend it degrades to Free; on a cold one it heals to the real plan.
- **P3 — fresh-install "Waking up Ayma" hung 8+ min (the master defect):** the splash's 45s overall watchdog is a one-shot `Timer`, and `_watchConnectivity` reset the WHOLE startup budget (`_attempt=0`, `_deadlineExceeded=false`, re-armed a fresh 45s watchdog, re-`_resolve()`) on EVERY connectivity event reporting a connected transport — not just a real offline→online transition. A cold boot emits repeated "still online" events (wifi revalidation, captive-portal probe, VPN), so the budget never expired and the usable Retry never surfaced → the loader churned indefinitely. Fix (`splash_screen.dart`): (a) the watchdog is now an ABSOLUTE wall-clock deadline, set once and only re-armed for the time REMAINING to it (never extended), cleared only by a deliberate manual Retry; (b) the connectivity path fires an early retry ONLY on a genuine offline→online TRANSITION (tracked `_online`), never on duplicate online events, and never clears `_deadlineExceeded`.
- **P2 — seeded Explore header flat-dark for SOME members on AOT (race):** the NOTE 269 width-match (decode at 260) only paints at once when that 260 decode is already warm in the ImageCache — true for a card inside the page-1 `prefetchCardPhotos` window, a MISS for a card scrolled in later or tapped mid-load, which then cold-decodes behind the ~12s GET. Fix (`explore_screen.dart`): on card tap, fire `prefetchCardPhotos` for just that card (unawaited, no nav latency) so the exact 260 decode warms during the push transition → the seed photo paints on first entry for every member, not only prefetched ones.
- **P4 — 2525 profile APK signing-key mismatch broke the bench `install -r`:** the gradle is correct (both `debug` and `profile` use the `stagingDebug` key under `-PaymaStagingSign`), but the 2525 profile APK was built WITHOUT `--staging`, so it fell back to the default debug keystore (SHA 16581138) while the debug APK used stagingDebug (90655f15) → `INSTALL_FAILED_UPDATE_INCOMPATIBLE`. Fix (`scripts/build-apk.sh`): refuse a `--profile` build without `--staging` (the AOT APK exists only for the staging bench and must share the staging key), with an `AYMA_ALLOW_UNSIGNED_PROFILE=1` escape hatch for the rare non-bench AOT build. See `docs/STAGING.md`.
- **Leg 4 / graph-tree "Nothing public yet" (report-only, server-side):** the own-public-profile body renders through `GraphTreeView` → `/profile/graph/tree?surface=publicProfile` and returned a 200 with EMPTY sections (not an error — the only client filter drops zero-node sections). The publicProfile visibility projection is built SERVER-side; on this fresh install it had not finished at fetch time (same window as the P3 bootstrap stall), so it returned empty, while the same endpoint returns full content minutes later. No client fix is correct (the data genuinely isn't there yet); it resolves once bootstrap completes cleanly (P3) and the backend warms. Flagged for the backend owner if it persists after P3.
- **Tests:** `subscription_status_test.dart` (+3 NEW: a thrown `activeSubscription()` still loads the backend plan; store + non-retryable backend failure degrades to Free, never throws; backend usage rides a 503→200 cold start to the real plan), `story_editor_screen_test.dart` (+1: photos load rides a cold-start failure instead of hard-failing), `splash_timeout_test.dart` (+1: repeated "still online" connectivity events never postpone the startup deadline — Retry still surfaces). `flutter analyze` 0 across changed files. No new route → app_registry N/A; resilience of existing surfaces → tour N/A; widget/startup-level → no diagram change. Inference untouched (100% LiveKit).
- **Report-only — 3 DEBUG ANRs on DEBUG 2525 (first launch / cold start / restore):** known debug-JIT / first-interaction artifact family (AOT launched clean, no ANR). No code fix — confirm on an AOT profile build before any action.

## NOTE272-LEG3-HERO-THUMB — build 2524, story hero thumb NOT visible at 2s (shimmer only) though hero renders by 12s — FIXED (fleet/note272-hero-thumb off origin/staging @41880eef, 2026-11-19, Scout NOTE 272) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Root cause (two gaps the NOTE 269 reservation couldn't reach):** (a) `_loadMainPhoto` was fired AFTER `await storyGet()`, and storyGet is itself cold-start-retried — so the hero GET didn't even start until the story body returned (~12s). (b) At 2s the cover wasn't on screen at all: the whole screen was the top-level `_StorySkeleton` (text-bar shimmer, no hero area) while storyGet was in flight, and the thumb URL only arrives with the cold `publicProfile` GET — so on a cold backend NO request delivers the thumb by 2s.
- **Fix:** (a) fire the hero load in parallel with the story fetch (`_seedAndLoadMainPhoto`). (b) a tiny per-user persistent cache of the LAST-KNOWN thumb URL (`MainPhotoCache`, shared_preferences) — the thumb bytes already survive force-stop in cached_network_image's on-disk cache (keyed by URL), so the cached URL paints the hero from disk with no network at ~2s. The hero is now rendered INSIDE the skeleton (one shared `_CoverHero`, in its final position), so the cached thumb shows while the body loads; the fresh GET replaces it (full fading over fresh thumb) and re-persists the thumb. Uid-keyed (never another account's photo); stale thumb replaced within the same open; no cache → the NOTE 269 reserved frame.
- **Tests:** `main_photo_cache_test.dart` (+5: save/load/clear, uid-keyed, no cross-user leak), `story_editor_screen_test.dart` (+2: cached thumb paints the `_CoverHero` in the skeleton and is not the reserved blank, then the fresh hero replaces it and the fresh thumb is persisted; no cache → reserved frame holds). All green across cache+story+public_profile+photo+explore; layout matrix green; `flutter analyze` 0. No new route → app_registry N/A; tour/diagram N/A (widget-level). Inference untouched (100% LiveKit).
- **Report-only (per brief):**
  - *Credits drift 8808→8805 (3):* no inference ran from this fleet work (flutter tests only; no sweep/judge). Most likely Scout's 2524 retest exercised LiveKit-routed inference (a chat turn / voice tap during the walkthrough) — 3 credits is consistent with a couple of short calls, NOT a leak and NOT an off-LiveKit reroute (still 100% LiveKit). Can't attribute to the exact call without the LiveKit dashboard; within normal test-session usage — watch only if it recurs with no test session.
  - *First Explore-tap ANR (1 in ~80 min, "Wait failed", force-stop recovered, non-gate):* known debug-JIT / first-interaction artifact family (same as the first-launch ANR class — AOT launches/runs clean). First Explore tap JIT-compiles the grid + decodes several card photos on the first frame under the debug kernel; under AOT that paint is pre-compiled. No code fix — confirm on an AOT profile build before any action; evidence doesn't point at app code.

## NOTE269-SEEDED-PHOTO — build 2522, Leg 3 seeded header paints NAME at 2s but PHOTO flat-dark ~12s on first entry + Leg 2 story hero area collapses while GET retries — FIXED (fleet/note269-seeded-photo off origin/staging @cc1999f2, 2026-11-19, Scout NOTE 269) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Leg 3 — seed photo decode mismatch (`_LoadingHeader`, public_profile_screen.dart):** the Explore grid + `prefetchCardPhotos` warm the card photo at `displayWidth: 260`, but the loading header requested the *same URL* at `displayWidth: 400`. Flutter's `ImageCache` keys on the resize dim (`memCacheWidth = displayWidth × dpr`), so 260 ≠ 400 = a cache MISS: the grid's live decode was never reused and the header cold-decoded (~12s flat-dark under first-launch load); cached re-entry was clean because the 400-entry was then warm. Fix: header decodes at 260 → reuses the warm grid decode → paints at once (first entry looks like a cached re-entry). Full-res hero replaces it when the GET lands.
- **Leg 2 — story cover hero area collapsed (`_StoryCover`, story_editor_screen.dart):** the hero block was omitted whenever `heroPhotoUrl == null` (covers both "loading" and "no photo"), so during the cold-start-retried `_loadMainPhoto` (~12s) the area was a photo-less gap that popped in; the NOTE 267 thumb could never be seen because its URL arrives with the same GET as the full photo. Fix: `_mainPhotoLoading` flag distinguishes loading from resolved-no-photo; while loading the cover RESERVES the rounded 4/3 frame with a calm surface (`Key('storyCoverLoadingHero')`), hero fades into it over its thumb once the GET resolves. Genuine no-photo still omits the hero.
- **Tests:** `public_profile_screen_test.dart` (+1: seed photo requested at grid width 260, not 400), `story_editor_screen_test.dart` (+1: gated GET → reserved hero frame present + hero absent, then hero fades in). 106 green across public_profile+story+photo+explore; layout matrix 29 green; `flutter analyze` 0. No new route → app_registry N/A; resilience of existing surfaces → tour N/A; widget-level → no diagram change. Inference untouched (100% LiveKit).

## NOTE268-OBSERVATIONS — build 2521, Leg 3 story cover hero ABSENT + Leg 2 Explore card photos never load — FIXED (fleet/note268-observations off origin/staging @cd0c3612, 2026-11-19, Scout NOTE 268) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Verified live first (not a server/client-parse bug):** ayma-staging returns valid photo URLs for the self `GET /explore/profile/{uid}` (cover hero) and the `/explore` cards — identical to the `/profiles/mini` photo DM avatars use (which loaded fine). Both failures were one-shot loads that never recover.
- **Leg 3 — story cover hero ABSENT (not dark):** `_loadMainPhoto` fetched the cover via a *separate, un-retried* `publicProfile` call (silent catch), unlike `_load`'s `withColdStartRetry` story fetch — so one cold/stressed-start miss (the 2521 first-launch ANR window) left the hero node absent while the retried story photos still rendered (sailboat with no hero). Fix: wrap it in `withColdStartRetry` (+ empty-uid guard). The NOTE 267 thumb now has a hero to fade over.
- **Leg 2 — Explore grid photos stuck on initials 25+ min (shared photo path):** `AymaPhoto`'s 10s PC8 stall was *terminal* — it dropped the tile to its fallback and cancelled the in-flight load, resetting only on a scroll/re-mount (exactly why the NOTE 266/267 hero "settled after a scroll"). Fix: bounded self-healing — on a stall it waits `_kStallRetryDelay` and re-attempts with a bumped attempt suffix (fresh `CachedNetworkImage` key → new fetch), up to `_kMaxStallRetries`, then the terminal fallback. Tiles recover once the backend warms; a dead URL still gives up (~36s), never churns.
- **Tests:** `ayma_photo_test.dart` (stalled tile re-attempts `@0→@1→@2`, falls back only once retries spent), `story_editor_screen_test.dart` (+1: a cold-start cover-fetch failure retries → hero renders, not absent). 137 green across photo+story+carousel+public_profile+explore+layout-matrix; `flutter analyze` 0. No new route/surface → app_registry N/A; resilience of existing surfaces → tour N/A; widget-level → no diagram change. Inference untouched (100% LiveKit).
- **Gate note (no code):** 2521 DEBUG ANR cluster (6 in ~45 min) is the known debug-JIT / first-launch artifact family (AOT launched clean) — the trigger that exposed these one-shot-load gaps, not a bug. No fix; evidence doesn't point at app code.

## NOTE267-OBSERVATIONS — build 2520, graded self-route hero PASS; two more hero-lag surfaces the 2520 fix didn't cover — FIXED (fleet/note267-observations off origin/staging @8c3e9193, 2026-11-19, Scout NOTE 267) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Same family as NOTE 266 Obs 2, on surfaces the carousel-only fix didn't reach.**
- **Obs (a) — other-member profile opens on a flat-dark "Loading this profile…"** (`PublicProfileScreen`, from Explore): the progressive thumb can't paint because the photo URLs arrive with the still-in-flight `GET /explore/profile/{uid}`. Fix (generalizable, no per-person special-casing): a seed handoff — `PublicProfileScreen` gained `seedPhotoUrl` + `seedName`; while the GET is in flight and a seed is present it renders a real header skeleton (`_LoadingHeader`: square photo + bottom scrim + name + small spinner) painting the seed photo the Explore card already prefetched (instant). The router reads the seed from go_router `extra`; the Explore card passes `{photoUrl: card.gridPhotoUrl, name: card.name}` on push. No seed (chat/deep link) → the existing loader, unchanged.
- **Obs (b) — story-page cover hero** (`_StoryCover`, `displayWidth: 600`): got the NOTE 266 treatment directly — the screen captures `p.thumbPhotos.first` alongside `detailPhotos.first` and passes it as the cover `AymaPhoto`'s `placeholderUrl`, so the cover fades in over its thumb instead of flat-dark.
- **Tests:** `public_profile_screen_test.dart` (+2: a seeded load shows the seed photo + name and NOT the "Loading this profile…" wall; no seed → the plain loader unchanged — gated completer so the request's timeout timer clears), `story_editor_screen_test.dart` (+1: the cover hero carries its thumb variant as `placeholderUrl`). `flutter test` public_profile+story+explore+layout-matrix+photo green (33 + 118 among them); `flutter analyze` **0** (full project). No new route/surface (the seed rides existing go_router `extra`) → `app_registry` N/A; loading refinements of existing surfaces, not new features → tour N/A; widget-level → no architecture-diagram change. Inference untouched (100% LiveKit). Privacy: the seed/thumb are the member's own already-public photo the Explore card already shows — nothing new exposed.
- **Also noted by Scout, NOT acted on (correctly):** first-launch ANR on 2520 recovered by Wait — this is the known DEBUG-build / first-launch artifact (see `first-launch-ANR` memory; AOT launches clean), not a code bug; completeness still 85% (unchanged — the NOTE 266 Obs 1 narrative-judge jitter, expected). Neither is a code defect to fix here.

## NOTE266-OBSERVATIONS — build 2519, all legs PASS; two ungraded observations for investigation (fleet/note266-observations off origin/staging @5185c012, 2026-11-19, Scout NOTE 266) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Obs 1 — completeness 86% → 85% after the self-route dedup: NOT the dedup; report only, metric unchanged (full write-up: `docs/PROFILE_COVERAGE.md` §7).** The ring number is `completeness._score(evaluated)` = `round(95 · Σ weightᵢ·coverageᵢ / Σ weightᵢ)`, with `coverage` fed ONLY by (1) preboarding profile fields, (2) the questionnaire store, (3) the free-text story judged by the narrative LLM (Gemini, temp 0.0, cached on a hash of wiki text + target-id set). `completeness.py` imports none of `person_graph_render` / `person_graph_tree` / the profile route — the dedup changes only the public RENDER surface and never deletes/alters store nodes, so the effective graph `_evaluate` reads is unchanged by NOTE 265 (the only 2518→2519 code change was the one-line self-route allowlist). So it is neither "dedup removed double-counted fields" (the metric never counts rendered/public fields) nor a structural regression (no node lost its answer). The 1-point move is narrative-LLM/`round()`-boundary jitter — the same live wobble §6.1 of `person_graph_completeness.py` was built to retire via the fixed-seed tier model (not yet wired to the live endpoint). **Per the order, the metric was left unchanged** (report the cause first). Follow-up IF the founder wants the wobble gone: cut the ring over to `person_graph_completeness.compute_tier`.
- **Obs 2 — recurring hero photo lag (dark on first entry, settles after scroll): FIXED (real first-load delay).** Root cause (code-confirmed): the carousel hero renders at `displayWidth: 900` (`NodeImage(fill)` → `AymaPhoto`, disk-resize path), so on first entry it fetches a large variant COLD — the Explore prefetch warms only the 260px thumb (a different provider key) and the founder's self-profile has no prior card to warm it — and sat on `AymaPhoto`'s flat dark placeholder until the big image arrived (the "after scroll" is coincidental, not scroll-triggered). Fix (progressive load, opt-in, surgical): `AymaPhoto` gained `placeholderUrl` — a small low-res variant painted UNDER the full-res fade on the non-stalling plain path, falling back to the flat tile; `NodeImage` threads it through; the hero carousel passes the aligned `thumb_400` (`stripPhotos`, the same variant the filmstrip already loads, small/fast/usually already on disk). Cold hero now shows the thumb instantly and the full-res sharpens in over it. Every other `AymaPhoto` caller is unchanged (no `placeholderUrl` → flat placeholder as before).
- **Tests:** `ayma_photo_test.dart` (+3: thumb painted under a hung full-res on the plain path, thumb stays off the disk-resize path; a null placeholderUrl and a placeholderUrl == url both keep the flat tile with no self-wait), `carousel_node_visualiser_test.dart` (+2: the hero page wires the aligned thumb through to `placeholderUrl`; null when no distinct thumb list). `flutter test` photo+carousel+node_visualiser+layout-matrix green (12 + 100); `flutter analyze` 0 on touched files. No new route/surface → `app_registry` N/A; progressive load is a render refinement of an existing surface, not a new user-facing feature → tour N/A. No architecture-diagram change (widget-level render detail). Inference untouched (100% LiveKit). Privacy: the thumb is the profile's own already-public photo variant; nothing new is exposed.

## NOTE264-DEDUP-GRAPHTREE — Leg 2 PROFILEDEDUP still FAILs on build 2517: /profile/graph/tree?surface=publicProfile (the path the app's GraphTreeView renders) still shows Age 29 / Austin / "Interested in: Women" in About Me (fleet/note264-dedup-graphtree off origin/staging @c44380a0, 2026-11-19, Scout NOTE 264 build 2517 Leg 2 FAIL) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Root cause (verified by running both renders on the real demo-test1 fixture shape):** the NOTE 263 fact-based fix landed in `person_graph_render.public_sections` (the Living Report path) — Scout confirmed story_public + /explore/profile PASS. But the app's public profile is drawn by **GraphTreeView** from `person_graph_tree.node_tree(surface="publicProfile")`, a THIRD render path still doing the id-only `n.id in _HEADER_FIELD_IDS` drop. That drop only matched canonical seed ids (which carry `section`/`visibility` None and never render), so the `emergent.editor.identity.*` duplicates leaked into About Me on device.
- **Fix:** route the tree's public walk through the SAME `render._public_fact_placement` (drop/move/keep), delete the now-dead `_HEADER_FIELD_IDS` alias + import from `person_graph_tree`. Sharing one predicate exposed a latent bug it also fixes: the duplicate drop was NOT surface-aware. The two renderers format a standard row differently (Living Report inlines `label: value`; Node-Visualiser keeps label/value apart), so a moved editor duplicate deduped textually in the report but rendered TWICE in the tree. `_public_fact_placement` is now surface-aware — an editor duplicate of a non-header standard fact is **dropped wherever it sits when the canonical row renders publicly** (the canonical is the single source), and **moved/kept only when the canonical is PRIVATE** (then the editor bullet is the sole public copy and must survive). Net: all three public surfaces agree on CONTENT — gender once in About Me, Interested-in once under What I'm Looking For, nothing duplicated — each keeping its own row formatting.
- **Tests:** `test_person_graph_tree.py` — `test_note264_graphtree_public_drops_editor_identity_header_duplicates` (EXACT demo-test1 overlay through `node_tree`: no name/age/location/Interested-in in About Me, the fact once under looking_for, gender once), `test_note264_graphtree_story_keeps_header_fields` (public-only: Your Story unchanged); `test_public_surface_keeps_fact_when_standard_row_is_private` now also guards the surface-aware keep. `test_person_graph_render.py` — `test_note264_public_restatement_of_private_standard_survives` (a public editor bullet restating a PRIVATE standard field survives, never silently dropped). 129 render+tree+route+dedupe tests green; 453 green across public_profile/report_adapters/explore/story. **No Flutter change** → layout matrix / tour / app_registry N/A. Inference untouched (100% LiveKit). Privacy: only suppresses/relocates/keeps-public — never adds a field, and the surface-aware keep prevents a public fact silently vanishing.

## NOTE263-APPEARANCE-ISUSER-GATE (ITEM 3) — Appearance report can't be triggered: the sheet offers a dead-end Run / 400 fails silently when the user has no confirmed-as-you photos (fleet/note263-appearance-isuser-gate off origin/staging @5a695323, 2026-11-18, Scout NOTE 263 build 2515 FAIL on demo-test1) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Verified finding (corrects Scout's root cause).** On build-2515 staging the sheet's source (`photo_attrs.eligibility`) ALREADY applies the same `is_user == "yes"` gate as the run path (`appearance_engine._grid_photos`/`_pick_all`) — commit `9c671072`, Nov 17 — so the sheet does NOT offer a photo the server refuses; there is no offer-then-refuse mismatch. demo-test1 has **zero `is_user == "yes"`** photos (14 `no`, 13 unset), so eligibility correctly returns EMPTY groups. The real defects: (1) the empty sheet implied "you have no photos" (misleading — the user HAS photos, just none confirmed) and left a **live Run button that dead-ended**; (2) a server 400 surfaced only as a generic "Couldn't finish — try again", not the honest is-you reason.
- **Why zero `is_user == "yes"`:** `is_user` is written at photo ingest by the face-match pipeline — `photo_pipeline` sets `"yes"` **iff** the face check returns `self` (needs fresh biometric consent), `"no"` for `not_self`, `""` for unknown; `main.py:1692` sets it on an explicit "this is me" confirm. The demo-test1 fixture simply never produced `self` verdicts. **Engineering call: KEEP the gate** — it's a correctness/safety rule (analyse only photos confirmed to be the user; never analyse someone who isn't them). Loosening `unset`→eligible would re-introduce analysing unconfirmed photos, defeating the Nov-17 intent.
- **Fix (sheet + server AGREE + never fail silently).** Server `photo_attrs.eligibility` now returns `has_unconfirmed_photos` (grid photos dropped solely for being unconfirmed); `POST /appearance/report` 400 detail is now honest ("No confirmed photo of you to analyse — add a photo, or confirm an existing one is you, first."). Flutter sheet (`report_run_flows.dart`): honest blocked banner when nothing is analysable (confirm-a-photo copy when `has_unconfirmed_photos`, add-a-photo copy otherwise), **Run button disabled** unless a run would analyse something (`_canRun`), and `_runAppearanceInBackground` surfaces a 400's `detail` verbatim instead of the generic retry toast. `AppearanceEligibility.hasUnconfirmedPhotos` added.
- **FIXTURE NEEDS is_user VERDICTS (staging data pass for the founder to run):** to make demo-test1 actually runnable (not just honest-blocked), set `users/<demo-test1-uid>/user_media/<mediaId>.is_user = "yes"` on the solo photos that ARE the user (at minimum the primary portrait) — same field the face-match pipeline writes. Confirming the portrait "is you" in-app also sets it (`main.py:1692`). Do NOT set `yes` on group/other-person photos (that's what the gate exists to prevent).
- **Tests:** server `tests/test_appearance_isuser_gate.py` (5): `_grid_photos` drops unconfirmed / keeps `yes`; `run_appearance` returns None (→400) when all unconfirmed, with AND without an explicit face selection; `eligibility` excludes unconfirmed + flags `has_unconfirmed_photos`, surfaces a confirmed photo. Flutter `appearance_prerun_sheet_test.dart` (3): no-confirmed-photos → honest banner + DISABLED Run; no-photos-at-all → add-a-photo banner; open→pick→Run with a 400 → honest reason surfaced, not "Couldn't finish". Layout matrix gains `appearance pre-run sheet (no confirmed photos)` (via `layoutApiAppearanceUnconfirmed`). `pytest` 89+5 green; `flutter analyze` 0; `flutter test` layout matrix green. No route/surface change → `app_registry` N/A; blocked state is a state of an existing feature, not a new feature → tour N/A. Inference untouched (100% LiveKit). Privacy/18+/consent: is-you safety intent kept intact (gate NOT weakened).

## NOTE263-401-AUTH (ITEM 2, TOP-PRIORITY) — app signs in but ALL authed calls 401, blocking every on-device authed-surface test (fleet/note263-401-auth off origin/staging @5a695323, 2026-11-18, Scout NOTE 263 build 2515/2516) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Root cause (classification (c): WRONG-PROJECT token — diagnosed on-device, not guessed).** The APK installed on emulator-5554 was **mis-built**: its **native** `google-services` resources (`resources.arsc`) target **prod** (`1:235381544962:…`, project `ayma-ai`) while its Dart `firebase_options` (`libapp.so`) is **staging** (`1:187469876115:…`, `ayma-staging`). On Android the native config auto-creates the `[DEFAULT]` FirebaseApp before Dart runs, and `Firebase.initializeApp(options: …)` returns that existing app unchanged — so `getIdToken()` mints a **prod-aud** token that the staging API rejects (401) on `/story`, `/bootstrap`, every authed call; the splash's 401→sign-in path then loops (matches Scout's "came up signed out, form pre-filled"). Proven: staging API returns **200** for a freshly minted staging token (`aud=ayma-staging`), **401** for any non-empty wrong/garbage token, **403** for no token. The **published** `staging-5a695323-profile.apk` (md5 712d00d2…) is built **correctly** (staging on BOTH axes) — reinstalling it on the emulator unblocks Scout. The defect reproduces only when a mixed-project APK (e.g. a plain `flutter build apk` or a stale incremental build) is installed.
- **Fix (client, surgical — makes the class fail LOUD instead of silent).** `FirebaseConfigGuard` (`lib/core/firebase_config_guard.dart`) compares `Firebase.app().options.projectId` (effective/native) vs `DefaultFirebaseOptions.currentPlatform.projectId` (intended/Dart) right after init (startup DAG N7, `startup_nodes.dart`); on a mismatch the splash (`splash_screen.dart` `_resolve`, before the cached-route fast path or any bootstrap) shows an honest **non-retryable** diagnostic ("Firebase project is ayma-ai but built for ayma-staging … 401 … reinstall a scripts/build-apk.sh build") instead of looping through sign-in. Healthy single-project builds (store/prod, correct `--staging`) keep both axes on one project, so the guard is silent. **No token-refresh-on-401 added** — a refresh re-mints the same wrong-project token, so it would mask nothing; the only real fix is matching native+Dart Firebase config, which `build-apk.sh --staging` already does (it swaps the native config and busts the google-services resource cache).
- **Tests:** `firebase_config_guard_test.dart` — pure `firebaseProjectMismatchMessage` (match→null, Scout prod-vs-staging case→flagged w/ both ids + "401", empty id→no false positive, whitespace tolerant) + splash widget tests (mismatch → honest error shown, NO authed request fires, NO bounce to sign-in/shell; healthy build → guard transparent). `flutter analyze` 0 on touched files; splash + layout matrix green. No route/surface change → `app_registry` N/A; no new user-facing feature → tour N/A. Inference untouched (100% LiveKit). Privacy/security: auth hardened, not weakened — token validation unchanged, no token ever logged in committed code.

## NOTE263-DEDUP-REALFIXTURE — profile dedup STILL FAILs on live 2515: About Me shows "Gender identity: Man / Age: 29 / Interested in: Women", no What-I'm-Looking-For section (fleet/note263-dedup-realfixture off origin/staging @5a695323, 2026-11-18, Scout NOTE 263 build 2515 FAIL) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Root cause (verified LIVE against `users/demo-test1/graph/main` on ayma-staging, then by running `public_sections`):** the batch-2515 fix dropped only `n.id in HEADER_FIELD_IDS`, but in the real graph those canonical nodes carry just an answer in the overlay and the bullets that actually render in About Me are `emergent.editor.identity.*` DUPLICATES a header import left behind — `.0`="Age: 29", `.1`="Gender identity: Man", `.4`="Interested in: Women". The id drop never matched them. And because the global text-dedup saw the identity-section "Interested in: Women" duplicate FIRST, it suppressed the canonical `target_match_genders` bullet in `looking_for`, so that section vanished. The 2515 in-memory test used SYNTHETIC canonical-id nodes, so it passed while the real fixture shape slipped past.
- **Fix (backend only, surgical):** `person_graph_render` now routes every node on the public surface through one predicate, `_public_fact_placement(graph, node)` → `drop`/`move`/`keep`. It catches editor duplicates by the FACT they restate (`_editor_restated_standard` → the high-precision, cross-section `text_restates_standard_fact`), NOT by id: a header fact (name/age/location) or its editor duplicate is dropped; a mis-sectioned "Interested in: …" duplicate is MOVED to `looking_for` (so the section appears and About Me loses it) even when the section's own canonical bullets are private; a same-section duplicate (second "Gender identity: Man") is dropped, the canonical renders. `HEADER_FIELD_IDS` still honored for canonical ids (node_tree surface unchanged). Your Story / persona / grounding keep every row (no `drop_header_fields`).
- **Deliberate deviation flagged:** Gender is KEPT in About Me (exactly once, from the canonical node). The photo header shows only name/age/location and the Flutter client never reads the top-level `gender` payload field, so the About Me bullet is the ONLY place gender shows — dropping it would erase gender from the public profile. Scout's NOTE 263 leg asks only for name/age/location suppression + Interested-in relocation; this matches. If the founder wants gender in the header instead, that needs a one-line `ProfilePhotoHeader` change (Flutter + layout matrix) — separate follow-up.
- **Tests:** `test_person_graph_render.py` — `test_note263_editor_identity_duplicates_drop_and_relocate_real_fixture` (EXACT demo-test1 overlay shape: canonical answer-only seed nodes + the three `emergent.editor.identity.*` duplicates; asserts no name/age/location in About Me, What-I'm-Looking-For exists with "Interested in", not in About Me, Gender once), `test_note263_interested_in_relocates_even_when_canonical_is_private` (relocation surfaces the section even when the canonical bullet is private). Both verified RED without the fix, GREEN with it. Deleted the misleading synthetic `test_note261_public_profile_dedups_header_and_moves_interested_in`. 215 affected backend tests green (render + tree + store + public_profile + report_adapters + profile_manual). No Flutter change → layout matrix / tour / app_registry N/A. Inference untouched (100% LiveKit). Privacy: suppresses/relocates, never adds a field — no new leak.

## NOTE262-DEVPANEL-OPEN — Developer panel "would not open" on build 2514, blocking verification of the retained `ayma_only` Photos group (fleet/note262-devpanel-open off origin/staging @9143cd5a, 2026-11-18, Scout NOTE 262 build 2514) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Verdict: NO code regression — the 2514 failure is a `--debug` (JIT) build / automation artifact, not a broken gesture.** Verified in code across 2511→2514: the 7-tap open gesture (`settings_screen.dart` `_tapsToUnlock=7`, `_tapWindow=2s`, version row key `build-stamp` → `_onBuildTap`/`_openDeveloper` → `context.push('/settings/developer')`), the `ENABLE_DEV_MODE` flag (`dev_mode.dart`; `build-apk.sh` passes `--dart-define=ENABLE_DEV_MODE=true` unconditionally), and the route redirect-gate (`app_router.dart`) are all **byte-identical** between build 2514 (`aa6d7c70`) and now — zero changes to `settings_screen.dart`'s gesture region, `dev_mode.dart`, `dev_panel_session.dart`, `build-apk.sh`, or the router dev route across those batches.
- **Two real (non-code) reasons the 7-tap fails under adb/uiautomator2 on a debug APK:** (1) **per-tap 2s window** — each tap must land <2s after the previous or the counter resets to 1 and never reaches 7; slow automated taps silently reset. (2) **first-launch JIT ANR** (documented NOTE 226, `docs/STAGING.md`) — the `--debug` JIT APK's main-thread kernel JIT eats input dispatch on a loaded emulator, so navigation/taps don't register. Reproduced locally: the fresh 2515 `--debug --staging` APK persistently threw the first-launch "Ayma isn't responding" ANR on emulator-5554; the gesture must be verified on the `--profile` (AOT) APK, which launches clean.
- **Repro for Scout:** verify on the `--profile --staging` (AOT) APK (`build-apk.sh --profile --staging`). Settings → scroll to bottom **Version** row (key `build-stamp`) → tap 7× with ≤1.5s cadence (tight `for` loop, no per-tap sleep) OR long-press once → panel opens to the **Developer** password screen ("Enter the developer password to continue.", key `dev-locked`). Then enter the `DEV_PANEL_PASSWORD` to `POST /dev/verify` → `DevPanelSession.unlocked` → Story editor shows the **Ayma-only Photos** group with the retained dog + cat. Full runbook added to `docs/STAGING.md`.
- **Secret note:** revealing `ayma_only` needs the `DEV_PANEL_PASSWORD` (Secret Manager, ayma-api) — Scout must have it to complete the unlock leg; the code does not and must not hardcode it.
- **Tests:** `settings_screen_test.dart` — added a security-neutral injectable clock (`SettingsScreen.nowOverride`, defaults to `DateTime.now`) so the 2s window is deterministically testable, plus two NOTE 262 regression tests: "seven taps each within the 2s window accumulate and open the panel" and "taps spaced wider than the 2s window reset the counter and never open the panel (the NOTE 262 slow-automation failure)". Existing "seven version taps open / long-press opens / off = no-op" tests retained. `flutter analyze` 0; layout matrix green. No route/surface change → `app_registry` N/A; no new user-facing feature → tour N/A. Inference untouched (100% LiveKit). Dev-panel security gates unchanged (NOT weakened).

## NOTE260-UNIFIED-PHOTO-STRIP — Your Story "Photos": ONE swipeable strip + ONE gallery across ALL visible photos (fleet/note260-unified-photo-strip off origin/staging @25c8b421, 2026-11-18, Scout NOTE 260 Leg 1 FAIL build 2511) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **VERIFY (build-timing ruled out):** the prior Leg-1 fix `5eddb517` (swipeable `PhotoGallery` `ListView`) IS an ancestor of the tested build `e87a1593` and of staging `25c8b421` — it was in the APK. So NOT a build-timing miss; the swipeable `ListView` was present but the Story Photos section still read as grouped tiles because of a SEPARATE bug.
- **Root cause (confirmed in code):** `story_editor_screen.dart` `_PhotoGroupsList.build` rendered **one `PhotoGallery` per source group** (`for (final (group, label) in groups) … PhotoGallery(photos.where(displayGroup==group))`), each with its own labelled header ("PROFILE PHOTOS (1)", "FROM CHAT & SKILLS (1)", …). With 1 photo per group each "strip" was a single tile; and `_openPhotoDetail` scoped `galleryUrls` to the tapped photo's own display group, so the fullscreen gallery held 1 URL and could not advance. That is why Scout saw grouped tiles + a non-advancing detail image.
- **Fix:** `_PhotoGroupsList` now renders the VISIBLE photos (all non-`ayma_only`, ordered by `photoGroups`) as ONE `PhotoGallery` strip (no per-group headers) + `_openPhotoDetail` builds `galleryUrls` from `visibleStripPhotos(_photos)` — ONE `openGallery`/`PageView` across every visible photo, index = tapped photo's slot. New ordering helpers `visibleStripPhotos`/`aymaOnlyStripPhotos` in `photo_gallery.dart` keep strip + gallery ordering identical. Per-photo tap → owner detail sheet (delete / make public-private / chat) unchanged.
- **Leg 2 retention kept (dev-gated, SEPARATE):** the "AYMA-ONLY — KEPT, NOT ON YOUR PROFILE" group stays its own labelled strip below the visible strip (dev mode only); `ayma_only` photos are never merged into the main strip, story body, or public/shared surface. Leg 2 unchanged and still green.
- **Tests:** `story_editor_screen_test.dart` — "the Photos section renders ALL visible photos as ONE swipeable strip, not per-group tiles" (one `photo_gallery_strip`, 3 tiles, no group headers), "tapping a strip photo opens a gallery spanning ALL visible photos that advances on swipe" (`galleryUrls` = all 3 visible, `galleryIndex` 0), dev-unlocked = 2 strips (visible + separate ayma_only), Ayma's Notes = one visible strip + separate ayma_only strip. `photo_gallery_test.dart` unchanged (already covers the swipeable `ListView` + `PageView`). `flutter analyze` 0; layout matrix green (29). ## NOTE256-CAROUSEL-RETENTION — Story section images swipeable + `ayma_only` retained image off the story body + truthful delete copy (fleet/note256-carousel-retention off staging @cd8a7a78, 2026-11-18, Scout NOTE 256 build 2507 FOLLOW-ONS) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Leg 1 (swipeable strip — prior fix missed the real surface):** `fleet/photo-carousel-swipeable` made `NodePhotoCarousel` (the report-framework carousel) swipeable, but **Your Story's "Photos" section** renders through a DIFFERENT widget — `_PhotoGroupsList → PhotoGallery` (`photo_gallery.dart`), which was a non-swipeable **`Wrap` grid** (thumbnails wrapping to multiple rows). So the Story showed no swipeable strip. Fix: `PhotoGallery` is now ONE horizontal, swipeable `ListView` strip (`Key('photo_gallery_strip')`) of all a group's photos — a DRAG scrolls through them, no static grid remains. Tapping a tile opens the owner photo-detail sheet (delete / make public-private / chat preserved); the sheet's big image now opens `AymaImageViewer.openGallery` — the fullscreen `PageView` across the whole group that ADVANCES ON SWIPE (new `galleryUrls`/`galleryIndex` on `PhotoDetailSheet`, group computed in `_openPhotoDetail`). Owner management kept (not regressed to a read-only gallery).
- **Leg 4.1 (Privacy P0 — `ayma_only` image leaked into the story body):** the retention fix excluded `ayma_only` from the Photos STRIP (`_livePhotos`) but NOT the story **body render**. Root cause: `routes/profile._tree_payload` builds the story tree with `include_ayma_only=CONFIG.graph_show_ai_only` (default ON, founder keeps it ON), so `person_graph_tree` put EVERY `ayma_only` node — images included — on the owner story surface, and the body photo_carousel aggregated the retained image. Fix at the render layer: `person_graph_tree.node_tree` now drops an `ayma_only` IMAGE node from the **story / public body** regardless of the dev flag (`hide_ayma_only_images`), while `ayma_only` TEXT inferences still follow the flag. The retained image shows ONLY in the owner's Ayma-only Photos view (Ayma's Notes, separate `photoNodes()` strip). ayma_only never on story body / public / shared.
- **Leg 4.3 (owner-visibility — build 2509 founder voice):** after deleting an image it flips to `ayma_only` and is KEPT (traced: `emergent.image.u90b2f46`, founder uid `iWhWjiWhEpU7iyu9B9vy7GlT0PD2`, still present, `ayma_only`), but with dev mode ON the owner saw it NOWHERE. The retained image must satisfy BOTH facets: (a) OUT of the story body / public / shared (Leg 4.1), and (b) findable by the OWNER in his Photos view + Ayma's Notes when dev mode is on. The backend owner path (`/story/photos` → `image_nodes_for_view`) already includes `ayma_only` (only `public_only` drops it) and the `node_tree` body fix is a SEPARATE path, so it didn't regress the data. The gap was the **Story Photos section**, which unconditionally stripped the `ayma_only` GROUP (`photoGroupsForView` + `_livePhotos`) even in dev mode — so the owner only had the buried Ayma's Notes screen. Fix (`story_editor_screen.dart`): when `DevPanelSession.instance.unlocked.value` (Developer panel unlocked), the Story Photos view sources from `_photos` (incl. `ayma_only`) and shows the full `photoGroups` incl. the labelled "AYMA-ONLY — KEPT, NOT ON YOUR PROFILE" group; a locked/normal user is unchanged (group + photos excluded). Ayma's Notes already surfaces the group (`_photos` + full groups). Story BODY still excludes `ayma_only` images (backend). Test: `story_editor_screen_test.dart` "Story Photos view surfaces the retained Ayma-only group when the Developer panel is unlocked".
- **Leg 4.2 (stale delete copy):** both image-delete confirmations updated to be truthful to retention — new string **"This removes it from your story and profile. Ayma keeps its own copy in your Photos."** (title "Remove this photo?", button "Remove"), replacing the old "Delete this photo? This removes it everywhere." In `node_chat_window._delete` the new copy is IMAGE-only (`widget.node.isImage`); a non-image node keeps "Delete this [and N lines under it]?".
- **Tests:** server `tests/test_person_graph_images.py::test_retained_ayma_only_image_never_on_story_or_public_body` (retained `ayma_only` image absent from story + public node-tree bodies even with `include_ayma_only=True`, public image still present, retained image still in owner view); Flutter `test/photo_gallery_test.dart` (ONE horizontal swipeable `ListView` not a `Wrap`; tile tap → detail sheet; detail-sheet image → fullscreen `PageView`; delete copy = new retention wording + "Remove"), `test/node_visualiser_test.dart` (image-node delete shows retention copy, never hits delete-count). `test/story_scroll_test.dart` retargeted to the outer vertical list (horizontal strips are ListViews now). Backend green; `flutter analyze` 0; layout matrix green (28). No route/surface change → `app_registry` N/A; no new user-facing feature → tour N/A. Inference untouched (100% LiveKit).
## PROFILE-DEDUP — public profile: header fields (name/age/location) not repeated in About Me; "Interested in" moved to What I am Looking For (fleet/public-profile-dedup off origin/staging @d2e7c907, 2026-11-18, build 2509, founder order) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Founder (build 2509, his PUBLIC PROFILE screenshot):** the photo header already shows name / age 33 / San Jose, then About Me REPEATED them as icon rows (33, Man, San Jose United States) AND showed "Women" (his Interested in) inside About Me. Asks: (1) header fields must not show again in About Me; (2) "Interested in" belongs under "What I am Looking For", not About Me.
- **FIX 1 — header de-dup (RENDER placement, public surface only):** on `SURFACE_PUBLIC`, `person_graph_tree.node_tree` skips the standard rows the photo header already shows — `_HEADER_FIELD_IDS = {legal_first_name, date_of_birth, primary_location}` — so name/age/location appear exactly ONCE (in the header). They stay in `_standard_facts_by_section`, so prose/editor restatements are still suppressed (single surviving copy = the header). **Your Story has no photo header (only a `_StoryCover`), so these rows are NOT skipped there** — de-dup is public-only; verdict on the "same for Your Story" check = NO (no header there, no duplication), while FIX 2 (below) DOES move "Interested in" there too.
- **FIX 2 — "Interested in" placement (SECTION assignment, not render):** root cause was the section itself — `target_match_genders` was assigned to the `identity` (About Me) section, so it rendered there. Reassigned its seed `page_type` (`question_tree.json`) to `looking_for` and its `STANDARD_FIELDS` `order` to `9` (leads the Looking-For section, before partner age at 10). A SECTION move, so it applies on EVERY surface (public profile AND Your Story) — matches the founder's ask everywhere. **Value/visibility unchanged** — still in `_PUBLIC_ALLOWLIST` (public), hard_filter/dating_relevant kept.
- **Prose bullets untouched:** the name also appears as a prose bullet ("His full name is…") — that is a narrative node, not a standard-field row, so neither fix touches it (test asserts it survives on the public surface).
- **Scope:** backend only — `person_graph.py` (STANDARD_FIELDS), `question_tree.json` (page_type), `person_graph_tree.py` (public header skip) + tests + docs (`PERSON_GRAPH.md`). NO Dart change (both fixes are server-side; the client renders the tree it is handed) → `flutter analyze` 0 and layout matrix green are inherited from staging (zero Dart files touched). No route/surface change → `app_registry.json` N/A; no new user-facing feature → tour N/A. Inference untouched (100% LiveKit).
- **Tests (green):** `tests/test_person_graph_tree.py` — `test_public_profile_omits_header_fields_from_about_me`, `test_story_keeps_header_fields_in_about_me`, `test_interested_in_renders_under_looking_for_not_about_me`; updated `test_standard_fields_marked_in_canonical_order` (identity vs looking_for by seed section), the zero-dup identity test, `test_node_dedupe.py` (Interested-in restatement now suppressed in looking_for), `test_completeness.py` (identity core_answered 4→3). Section-sensitive suites all green (127 in the focused run).
## REPORTS-PER-ELEMENT-NODES — web-search + appearance reports write ONE source-tagged node per element, not a lump (fleet/reports-per-element-nodes off origin/staging, 2026-11-18, founder voice order) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges). NOT device-verified — Scout checks render.
- **Founder order (voice, Nov 18):** web-search and appearance reports must be ONE NODE PER ELEMENT, each source-tagged — not a single lump. Tapping a report element must open just THAT element in the partial chat, never the whole report.
- **STEP 0 confirmed (what was there):** a web search landed as ONE lump node `emergent.skill.web_findings` (whole digest markdown) via `skill_memory.save_web_findings`; appearance findings as the `emergent.skill.{face,body}_analysis` lumps + the structured `emergent.appearance.report` `report_doc` node. Tapping the one lump node opened the whole report.
- **Web (commit 1):** `save_web_findings` (flag ON) now writes one node per finding — `emergent.webfind.{summary.<n>,fact.<fid>,link.<hash>,social.<hash>}` — each `source_kind="web_search"` + its web link in `source_ref`; stale findings cleared (overwrite); legacy lump retired; nodes map to `web_findings` page_type so grounding/persona are unchanged. `migrate_web_findings` re-splits the lump losslessly from the live `emergent.webfind.report.*` digest.
- **Appearance (commit 2):** `save_face_analysis`/`save_body_analysis` + auto-analysis `merge_*` (flag ON) write one node per discrete finding (each strength/improvement/recommendation) at `emergent.skill.{face,body}_analysis.<kind>.<n>`, `source_kind="appearance"` + source photo in `source_ref`; legacy lumps retired. Report-level metadata (score/verdict/category_status/finding_history/photos) stays on `emergent.appearance.report`; `appearance_blocks` keeps rendering the templated page from it (report screen already renders per-element via `report_tree` → tap opens one block; no render rewrite = no render-correctness regression). `node_page_type` now maps a multi-segment skill suffix to its base page_type. `migrate_appearance_findings` re-splits the lumps losslessly from the metadata node's face/body axes.
- **Dedupe/visibility UNCHANGED:** stable ids make re-runs idempotent, batch dedupe drops identical findings, default `private` (prior promotion kept); node controls apply.
- **Deliberate engineering calls (flagged):** (1) KEPT the flag-OFF (prod) `appearance_reports` doc store + the `report_doc` render source — deleting them breaks prod; they're removed only at the prod cutover (same pattern as "reports as nodes"). (2) KEPT `appearance_blocks` reading the metadata node (render-safety) instead of rebuilding it to read scattered finding nodes — the report screen is already per-element and tapping already opens one element. (3) The `physical_profile` (attributes) lump is left as-is (attributes are already labelled key-value, not the face/body "findings" named; bounded scope).
- **Scope:** backend only — `skill_memory.py` (web + appearance per-element writers + migrations), `person_graph_render.py` (page_type base-segment map) + tests + docs. No Dart changes → `flutter analyze`/layout matrix unaffected (no new route → `app_registry.json` N/A; no new user-facing surface → feature-tour N/A). Inference untouched (LiveKit).
- **Tests:** `tests/test_web_findings_nodes.py` (6), `tests/test_appearance_finding_nodes.py` (5); updated the superseded lump assertions in `tests/test_gist_first_writing.py`. Full backend suite green (minus the pre-existing `livekit.agents`-missing env collection error, unrelated).
- **SCOUT MUST CHECK (device):** (a) in `/profile/graph`, a web search now shows per-element web-finding nodes and an appearance report per-element finding nodes; tapping one opens a partial chat about THAT finding only. (b) the Web-research report screen and the Appearance report screen still render correctly (facts/strengths/score/photos) and tapping an element opens that element. (c) after Astro/Scout run the migrations on ayma-staging (founder uid `iWhWjiWhEpU7iyu9B9vy7GlT0PD2` + demo-test1), the founder's existing findings appear as per-element nodes with nothing lost and the old lumps gone.
## SOFIA-PERSONA-OCCUPATION — practice persona surfaces the match's public headline so it knows their occupation (fleet/sofia-persona-trace off origin/staging @d2e7c907, 2026-11-18, build 2509, founder NOTE 259) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Founder report (NOTE 259, build 2509):** in a PRACTICE conversation Sofia (Sofia Marino, `demo-test2`) said she works in TECH / marketing; her real profile is a CERAMIC ARTIST. "Is the wrong profile getting pulled, or did we lose the data?"
- **VERDICT — the model was IMPROVISING; her data is intact.** NOT a wrong-uid pull (name/age/San-Jose all correct), NOT a stale avatar snapshot (practice uses live `build_persona_context`, not the `users/{uid}/persona/avatar` snapshot — that is only the user's OWN avatar for AI↔AI sims), NOT the `emergent.webfind` "Boeing industrial engineer" node (that is `ayma_only`, `is_self=false`, a DIFFERENT web Sofia Marino, and never reaches the public MATCH persona). Her occupation was simply never in the persona prompt: `build_persona_context` → `public_profile.build` returns `pages` (her wiki) which is **empty**, and `PersonaContext` dropped the profile `headline`. So the composed prompt held only `Name/Age/Gender/City/Photos` and explicitly tells the model to "improvise small talk" for anything else → "tech/marketing" (San Jose default). Verified live against ayma-staging (read-only): `profile/main.headline` = "Ceramic artist & art teacher who lives for folk harmonies" is intact; her 61 graph nodes carry no occupation node (nearest `core_existential_anchor`="Creativity & Artistry"). **Dedupe did NOT touch demo-test2** (explicit): her `graph/main.updated_at`=2026-11-16T23:12:57 (a web-find write) and she has NO `graph/dedupe` doc; only `iWhWj…` (founder) + `demo-test1` (Daniel) have dedupe docs — Daniel's at 2026-11-18. No data repair needed.
- **FIX (grounding, generalizable — every persona now carries its public tagline):** added the public profile `headline` to `public_profile.build`, to `PersonaContext` (new `headline` field, rendered in `to_prompt_block` as "Profile tagline (what they do …)"), populated it in BOTH `build_persona_context` (match) and `build_self_persona_context` (own avatar), and round-tripped it through the `persona_snapshot` grounding + source hash so AI↔AI sims keep it. `headline` is viewer-visible (already indexed by Explore) → no privacy change. Inference untouched (100% LiveKit). Verified live: Sofia's persona block now includes "Ceramic artist & art teacher …".
- **Scope:** backend only — `practice_core.py`, `public_profile.py`, `persona_snapshot.py` + tests + docs. No route/surface → `app_registry.json` N/A; no Dart → layout-matrix/analyze N/A; no new user-facing feature → tour N/A.
- **Tests (green, 135):** `tests/test_practice_date.py::test_persona_context_includes_public_headline_occupation` (reproduces NOTE 259: occupation ONLY in `headline`, no wiki/graph node → it reaches the composed persona prompt); existing `test_practice_date.py`/`test_practice_extraction.py`/`test_persona_snapshot.py`/`test_public_profile.py`/`test_date_sim.py` all stay green.

## DEDUPE-CANDIDATE-CLASSES — candidate generation covers seed/standard-emergent/assess-story classes; standard-field merge never drops facts (fleet/dedupe-candidate-classes off origin/staging, 2026-11-18, Scout NOTE 255 add.3 Leg 28/39) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges). Dedupe apply STAYS HELD — re-ran DRY-RUN only; Astro reviews before any apply.
- **Defect (Scout NOTE 255 add.3):** 5 old FAIL pairs (coffee/Career, taste lists, finances, long-term/Relationships, listening) rendered twice — unmerged AND unraised, none in `dedupe_pending` — the candidate finder never surfaced them.
- **PART A — root causes (verified against live ayma-staging, read-only):** (1) **`candidate_report` REPLACED lexical with embeddings** when `GRAPH_EMBEDDING_DEDUPE` on — so taste lists + seed-vs-seed restatements (strong lexical dups) vanished the moment the flag turned on. **Fix:** lexical ALWAYS runs; embedding NN is **UNIONED in on top** (`crosscheck._union_candidates`). (2) **Embedding vectors never populated** (the Leg-28/line-118 "secondary finding"): `gemini-embedding-2` on Vertex returns ONE vector regardless of list size, so the batch `_embed_backend` failed `refresh_vectors`'s len-check and stored nothing. **Fix:** embed **one text per call**. (3) **Sweep `_has_emergent` dropped every seed-vs-seed pair** before it was judged (coffee, hybrid-finances are two seed NARRATIVES). **Fix:** `_eligible` allows a seed **narrative** (only two *structured* seed fields are skipped); a judged-duplicate pair with no deletable side (both seed) is **raised**, never dropped. **Confirmed against staging:** all 5 classes now surface (coffee NEAR_DUP+cos .97→raise; taste NEAR_DUP→merge; finances RELATED→raise; long-term lexical-MISS/cos .95→merge via embedding; listening RELATED+cos .88→merge).
- **PART B — merge-rule + data restore:** (1) a richer bullet (content tokens the short standard value lacks) is **never folded into a standard field** (`_standard_fold_loses_facts` → raise), because folding retires it to history and DROPS facts (the founder's "…and speaks Malayalam and English" lost into "San Jose, United States"). (2) **RESTORED** the founder's lost languages fact (ayma-staging, uid `iWhWjiWhEpU7iyu9B9vy7GlT0PD2` ONLY): recovered the EXACT text from `primary_location`'s `history` — "He currently lives in San Jose, California and speaks Malayalam and English." — restored as its own node `emergent.restore_languages` (section `identity`, visibility `public` to match primary_location, provenance `imported` preserved). Verified it renders in the effective graph. The new merge guard keeps the next sweep from re-folding it.
- **Scope:** backend only — `person_graph_crosscheck.py` (union + candidate_report), `graph_embeddings.py` (per-text embed), `person_graph_dedupe_sweep.py` (eligibility + fact-loss guard + raise-unmergeable) + tests + docs. No route/surface → `app_registry.json` N/A; no new route → layout-matrix N/A; no new user-facing feature → tour N/A. Inference: judge stays LiveKit, embeddings Vertex (founder-approved).
- **Tests (green, 81):** `test_graph_dedupe_sweep.py` (richer-bullet-not-folded, seed-narrative-raised-not-dropped, two-structured-fields-not-a-pair) + `test_graph_embeddings.py` (one-text-per-call batch embed, candidate_report unions lexical+semantic).
- **Re-ran the DRY-RUN only (HELD the apply), both graphs, ayma-staging ONLY, real LiveKit judge + Vertex embeddings now populating (sharded).** NEW candidate counts (embeddings ACTIVE — "semantic match ~0.NN" pairs now appear): **founder `iWhWjiWhEpU7iyu9B9vy7GlT0PD2` = 55 found (13 would-merge / 42 raise)** (was 45/11/34 lexical-only); **Daniel `demo-test1` = 51 found (15 would-merge / 36 raise)**. **All 5 named classes surface:** coffee=`career_role_narrative ? career_trajectory_narrative` (RAISED, both seed); taste=`taste_music/film/food` (MERGE, both graphs); finances=`financial_strategy_narrative ? financial_comingling` (RAISED); long-term=`emergent.assess.5653ad25 ← emergent.story.43fa7ba6` (MERGE — needed embeddings, lexical missed it); listening=`emergent.story.9d796a04 ← emergent.story.db9dfced` (MERGE). PART-B guard proven live: `emergent.restore_languages ? primary_location` is RAISED, NOT re-folded. **NOT `--apply`ed** (Astro reviews). Ran via the `tools/dedupe_sweep_run.py` dry-run against live ayma-staging with the deployed staging secrets (the deployed image predates these fixes, so a job on it can't populate vectors — this run uses the branch code against the identical staging env; no Cloud Run jobs created, none to delete). Dry-run wrote only the derived embedding-vector cache (no graph merges, `dedupe_pending` untouched).
## LEG43-APPEARANCE-REPORT-DEDUP — appearance report: recommendations once at bottom, STANDS-OUT chips don't restate findings + wrap full, each stat once (fleet/leg43-appearance-report-dedup off staging @693296f3, 2026-11-18, Scout NOTE 255 Leg 43 FAIL build 2502) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)

(2) All 4 STANDS OUT chips restated Face/Body strength findings shown below AND cut off mid-word (share card `_chip` `maxLines:1`+ellipsis). (3) `report_adapters.appearance_blocks` emits a physical-attributes `build` (`attr:build`, Attributes) and a body `quick_facts` `build` (`body:quick:build`, Body) — two nodes, different ids.

- **Scope:** server `report_adapters.py` (stat dedup); Flutter `appearance_card.dart`, `appearance_report_card.dart`, `appearance_report_screen.dart` (recos-once, chip dedup+wrap). No route/surface change → `app_registry.json` N/A; no new feature → tour N/A. Inference untouched (100% LiveKit).
