Flutter `test/appearance_report_card_test.dart` — screen test now serves the real node tree (the dup only shows when card + tree render together), asserts "What to do next" once, each reco once at bottom, STANDS OUT deduped off the in-app card; model test `standOutsToShow drops chips that restate a shown finding (in-app), keeps all for the share image`; chip wrap verified by the long-findings overflow test. `flutter analyze` 0; layout matrix green (81).
- **Part 2 (read-only, founder graph gender):** ayma-staging `users/iWhWjiWhEpU7iyu9B9vy7GlT0PD2/graph/main` — standard `gender_identity`="Man" (section "identity") + emergent `emergent.editor.identity.5`="Gender identity: Man" (section "identity", public) STILL present in the store (not merged by the dedupe sweep). RENDERS ONCE: the section-scoped standard-restatement suppression (NOTE 252 leg 28 colon-tail match, `person_graph_tree._restates_standard`) collapses the emergent restatement since both are in section "identity" and the standard row renders. `target_match_genders` is the separate "Interested in" field, not a gender restatement.
## WEB-PHOTOS-BROKEN — web-research report shows NO broken image tiles: filter unrenderable photo candidates + clean client fallback (fleet/leg33-web-photos-broken off origin/staging @693296f3, 2026-11-18, Scout NOTE 255 Leg 33 build 2502 SPLIT) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Defect (Scout NOTE 255 Leg 33, build 2502, Web research report):** 7 candidate photos rendered as broken image tiles, from `cdn.sanity.io`, `framerusercontent.com`, `www.cockcroft.ac.uk`, `media.nngroup.com`, `openalex.org`, `www.tuwien.at`, `www.maxiv.lu.se`.
- **WHY (two classes):** (1) **Non-image PAGE URLs captured as "photos"** — `openalex.org/W…` (scholarly entity), `*.ac.uk` / `*.tuwien.at` / `*.maxiv.lu.se` people/group pages: no image at the URL, so a client's image load can only fail → broken tile. (2) **Real image CDNs that block hotlinking / 403 a non-browser client** — `cdn.sanity.io`, `framerusercontent.com`, `media.nngroup.com` serve a valid image to the collector's UA but reject the Flutter client → broken tile. The Node-Visualiser photo carousel (`NodePhotoCarousel` → `NodeImage`) passed no `error:` fallback, so `AymaPhoto` showed its default `image_not_supported_outlined` broken-image glyph.
- **FIX (layered, no broken tile can ever show):**
  - *Server filter (class 1, preferred — bad candidates never reach the client):* new `functions/bootstrap/web_photo_filter.is_renderable_image_url` drops obvious non-image page URLs (no image extension and not a known image CDN), keeps real image URLs (incl. hotlink-blocked CDNs). Applied at collection (`deep_dive_images.find_web_images`, before fetch) AND on read (`report_adapters.deep_dive_blocks` — web_images, confirmed_photos, and owner candidate blocks), so already-stored bad URLs are cleaned too.
  - *Client fallback (class 2, the HARD guarantee):* `node_visualiser.nodeImageSourceFallback` — `NodeImage` (both fill + bounded) and the candidate tile now fall back to a clean neutral tile with a tappable "View source" chip (opens the node's origin page) on ANY load failure, NEVER the broken-image glyph.
- **Scope:** `web_photo_filter.py` (new), `deep_dive_images.py`, `report_adapters.py`; Flutter `node_visualiser.dart` (IMAGE/NodeImage render only — minimal overlap with the concurrent appearance-chips edit). No route/surface change → `app_registry.json` N/A; no new user-facing feature → tour N/A. Inference untouched (100% LiveKit).
- **Tests (green):** `tests/test_web_photo_filter.py` (image exts pass, page URLs dropped, extensionless image CDNs kept, garbage rejected), `tests/test_report_adapters.py::test_deep_dive_adapter_drops_unrenderable_photo_candidates`; Flutter `test/node_photo_broken_fallback_test.dart` (a failing web photo shows the clean source fallback + NO `image_not_supported_outlined` glyph; a valid image still renders). Python 118 pass; `flutter analyze` 0; layout matrix green (82).

## WEB-PHOTO-OVERFILTER — Leg-33 renderability gate was dropping VALID extension-less image URLs (`i.insider.com`) (fleet/note258-web-photo-overfilter off origin/staging @d2e7c907, 2026-11-18, Scout NOTE 258 follow-on to Leg 33) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Defect (Scout NOTE 258, 25-candidate set):** the Leg-33 `is_renderable_image_url` OVER-filtered — a real JPEG from `i.insider.com` (slug URL with no `.jpg`/`.png` extension and host not in the image-CDN allowlist) was wrongly DROPPED, so a valid web-research photo never rendered. Only known-CDN extension-less hosts survived; a legit extension-less image host did not.
- **FIX (`web_photo_filter.py`, pure heuristic relaxation — callers/flow unchanged):** (1) added `i.insider.com` to the explicit `_IMAGE_HOSTS` allowlist (named so `www.insider.com` article pages still drop); (2) general rule — a new `_IMAGE_SUBDOMAINS` set (`i, img, imgs, image, images, pic, pics, photo, photos, media, cdn, static, assets, thumb, thumbs`): an extension-less URL whose leading host label is an image subdomain is KEPT. The dropped page URLs (`openalex.org`, `www.cockcroft.ac.uk`, `www.tuwien.at`, `www.maxiv.lu.se`) are apex/`www` hosts → still DROP. Err toward KEEP; the client `node_visualiser.nodeImageSourceFallback` is still the last line. The URL gate stays cheap/pure; genuine HTTP-error candidates (NOTE 258 `spenet` 402, `brightervision` 404) are dropped at the `_fetch_image` download stage (`public_url.open_public` raises), a separate layer untouched by this fix.
- **Scope:** `web_photo_filter.py` only (plus tests). No Dart, no route/surface change → `app_registry.json` N/A, tour N/A. Inference untouched (100% LiveKit).
- **Tests (green):** `tests/test_web_photo_filter.py::test_insider_extensionless_image_kept`, `::test_extensionless_image_subdomains_kept`, `::test_insider_www_article_page_dropped` (+ existing page-URL-drop / hotlink-CDN-keep cases unchanged); `tests/test_deep_dive_images.py::test_insider_kept_http_error_candidates_dropped` (i.insider.com kept end-to-end; 402/404 candidates dropped at fetch). 7-host verdict: `i.insider.com` + sanity/framer/nngroup KEPT, openalex/cockcroft/tuwien/maxiv DROPPED. `test_web_photo_filter` + `test_deep_dive_images` + `test_report_adapters` green.

## CAREER-TIMELINE-VIEW — Career renders the editable timeline view over dated career nodes; LinkedIn stays the source link (fleet/career-timeline-view off origin/staging @9a5c545b, 2026-11-18, build 2502, founder order via Astro) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges), NO invented data on his graph
- **Founder (build 2502, Career screenshot):** "why is Career just a LinkedIn link — where is the timeline pulled from LinkedIn, and it should be editable by users." Settled spec (NOT redesigned): LinkedIn stays the SOURCE link; the Career section renders the career graph nodes AS a timeline where they carry dates, editable through the usual node path; the wiki-backed timeline subsystem stays DELETED.
- **FINDINGS (verified against live ayma-staging, read-only):** (1) **Founder career data today** = 5 nodes, ALL `answer_type=narrative` / `gist_row` prose (`emergent.assess.38ccfb80` BTech, `ad23494b` prior products, `c4ca893f` earlier background, `59de7b0a` + `emergent.story.50d421f2` autonomous-systems role), plus the read-time-injected `career.linkedin` button. **ZERO of his 221 nodes carry a `dated` link** (confirms the earlier `timeline_entry: 0` backfill). (2) **The timeline VIEW already exists end-to-end** — `person_graph_tree._default_visual` turns any dated node into a `timeline_entry`, and the shared `NodeVisualiser` (`_timelineRowWidget`/`_siblingWidgets`) renders consecutive dated siblings as ONE tappable→editable timeline rail. It doesn't show because no career node carries a date → all render as prose bullets. (3) **No LinkedIn import path** creates dated role/company/period nodes: only the user-given `PF_LINKEDIN_URL` (source link + `deep_dive` url_context research source, which writes prose). The deleted wiki subsystem was NOT replaced.
- **FIX (surgical, backend-only render change):** `person_graph_tree._order_timeline_siblings` orders a sibling list's DATED entries by date, most-recent-first (CV/LinkedIn convention), leaving non-dated siblings in place; applied to each section's roots in `node_tree` and to `children` in `_build_node`. <2 dated siblings = no-op, so an undated graph is byte-for-byte unchanged and the Career timeline orders itself the MOMENT dated nodes exist. Reuses the existing `timeline_entry` palette render (no parallel widget). **Invented nothing on his graph.**
- **STILL NEEDED (founder decision) → NOW BUILT:** a LinkedIn→dated-node import is what POPULATES the timeline. Built via the data-export route — see the LINKEDIN-IMPORT section below.
- **Scope:** backend `person_graph_tree.py` only (ordering) + tests + docs. No Flutter render change (avoids the concurrent node_visualiser.dart edit); no route/surface → `app_registry.json` N/A; no new route → layout matrix entry N/A; the timeline view is not a new feature surface (Career already in the tour via My story) → tour N/A. Inference untouched (100% LiveKit).
- **Tests (green):** `tests/test_person_graph_tree.py::test_career_dated_entries_render_as_an_editable_timeline_date_ordered`, `::test_career_without_dates_renders_bullets_not_a_fabricated_timeline`; `tests/test_graph_tree_route.py::test_career_section_renders_linkedin_source_link_plus_prose_no_timeline`; `ayma_flutter/test/node_visualiser_test.dart` — "Career: dated children render as an editable timeline…" + "Career: with NO dated children renders the LinkedIn source link + prose bullets…". `flutter analyze` 0; layout matrix green (32).
## LINKEDIN-IMPORT — LinkedIn work history → dated career nodes that populate the Career timeline (fleet/linkedin-import off origin/staging @d2e7c907, 2026-11-18, build 2510, founder voice order) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges), NO invented data
- **Founder order (voice, Nov 18 15:21):** build the LinkedIn → dated-career-nodes import so the Career timeline (CAREER-TIMELINE-VIEW above — ordering already ships) finally renders with real dated roles.
- **ROUTE VERIFIED → DATA-EXPORT (not OAuth).** Official "Sign in with LinkedIn (OpenID Connect)" grants ONLY `openid`/`profile`/`email`; its published `claims_supported` is `iss,aud,iat,exp,sub,name,given_name,family_name,picture,email,email_verified,locale` — **no positions / work history** (verified against learn.microsoft.com OIDC reference + LinkedIn Partner Program analyses, Nov 2026; work history needs a restricted partner program Ayma is not in). No LinkedIn OAuth app/client/secret exists in the codebase or Secret Manager (only the user-given `PF_LINKEDIN_URL`). So: the user uploads their LinkedIn **data-export `Positions.csv`** (LinkedIn → Settings → "Get a copy of your data": Company Name, Title, Description, Location, Started On, Finished On). No credentials, no multipart, **no scraping.**
- **BUILD:** `functions/bootstrap/linkedin_import.py` — deterministic CSV parse (tolerant headers + "MMM YYYY"/ISO/year date formats; blank Finished On → current → "… – Present") → one emergent `career`-section node per role with a node-level `dated` (start date, the timeline sort key), a `timeline_entry` visual, gist "Title · Company", and the `linkedin_import` SOURCE (`source_kind`/`source_ref`, node-sources rule). Endpoint `POST /profile/career/linkedin-import` (`routes/profile.py`, `{csv}` body → `asyncio.to_thread`). **Dedupe vs existing prose career notes:** a role whose company+title tokens are both contained in an existing career node FOLDS into it (`person_graph_store.fold_into_node` + `set_node_dated` → the prose note gains the dates and moves onto the timeline) — never a second node. Stable per-role id (`linkedin.<company>__<title>`) → re-import is idempotent (updates, mints nothing).
- **Schema (founder Nov 18 order "set the node's `dated` field"):** added one optional node-level `dated` to `OverlayNode`/`GraphNode`; `person_graph_tree._node_dated` prefers it over `Link.dated`. Additive, backward-compatible, no migration — this supersedes the earlier "no date field added" note (that was the render-only CAREER-TIMELINE-VIEW scope).
- **What the founder sees & taps:** Your Story app bar → work-history icon → **Import from LinkedIn** screen (3 steps + "Choose your Positions.csv"); also **Settings → Import from LinkedIn**. Pick the file → "Imported N roles" → the Career timeline in Your Story fills, most-recent-first. Registered (`app_registry.json` destination `linkedin_import` → `/settings/linkedin-import`); feature-tour entry `linkedin_import` (anchor `story_linkedin_import`, tab 3).
- **Replaced path:** none deleted — the public `career.linkedin` URL-link button is complementary (a source link), kept per the Career spec.
- **Tests (green):** `tests/test_linkedin_import.py` (parse dates/company/title/period; current→Present; rejects a non-positions file; creates dated career nodes with source; timeline orders most-recent-first; folds a duplicate prose note into one node; idempotent re-import). `ayma_flutter/test/linkedin_import_test.dart` (screen flow + tour anchor). `flutter analyze` 0; layout matrix green (34); Python registry + route tests green. Inference untouched (CSV parse is deterministic, 100% LiveKit elsewhere).
- **Founder step (not a blocker):** he connects HIS own LinkedIn by downloading his archive + uploading Positions.csv after this lands.

## IMAGE-DELETE-RETENTION — deleting an image keeps an Ayma-only copy (move to `ayma_only`, not a hard erase) (fleet/image-delete-retention off origin/staging @9a5c545b, 2026-11-18, founder retention rule via Astro) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Founder question + rule:** "where did the Ayma-only image I deleted go — Ayma should still store it." TRACE RESULT: image delete was a **HARD delete** — `person_graph_store.delete_nodes` did `del overlay[nid]` + `replace_overlay` (whole rewrite), no copy. The deleted image's trail ends there: **nothing in `graph/main`, the subcollections (`photo_notes`, `user_media`, `story_versions`, `appearance`, `web_photo_candidates`) or any node history/audit store retained the bytes or storage ref** — image nodes carry no history and a delete writes none. The founder's already-deleted Ayma-only image is **UNRECOVERABLE** (no restore possible; nothing to restore from). This fix prevents recurrence going forward.
- **Retention fix (founder rule, forward-looking):** both delete paths — Your Story Photos sheet (`POST /story/photo/delete`) and the node-chat / visualiser Delete (`POST /profile/graph/node/{id}/delete`) — now call `person_graph_store.retire_image_or_delete`, which **moves an IMAGE node to `ayma_only`** (node + `image_read` + provenance kept) instead of deleting it. The retained image leaves the story/profile/public surfaces but stays findable in the owner's **Ayma-only Photos view** (dev-flag-gated "Ayma's Notes", under an `AYMA-ONLY` photo group).
- **Scope — IMAGE nodes only:** `retire_image_or_delete` retires only `answer_type == "image"` nodes; **every other node kind still hard-deletes with its subtree** (`delete_node_with_descendants`), unchanged. Flutter: the Story Photos strip (`_livePhotos`) now excludes `ayma_only`; `displayGroup`/`photoEdgeColor` key on `ayma_only` (the dead server `deleted` flag — hardcoded `False` — was removed from the API, the `AiNotePhoto` model and the UI).
- **Privacy (hard, P0 kept):** `ayma_only` is never public — `image_nodes_for_view(public_only=True)` and the public persona render both drop it, public-profile grid photos come from a separate `user_media` store, and match/shared personas use `public_only`. A retained image can never leak onto the public profile or a shared report. Inference untouched (100% LiveKit).
- **Tests:** server `tests/test_person_graph_images.py` — `test_image_delete_retains_as_ayma_only` (node survives as `ayma_only`, read+provenance kept, in owner view, excluded from public), `test_non_image_node_hard_deletes_as_before`; Flutter `test/story_editor_screen_test.dart` (Story strip excludes `ayma_only`; Ayma's Notes shows the `AYMA-ONLY` group), `test/photo_gallery_test.dart` (Ayma-only detail-sheet label + AI-only colour). Backend green; `flutter analyze` 0; layout matrix green.
## PHOTO-CAROUSEL-SWIPEABLE — photo_carousel is a real swipeable carousel of ALL section images; detail advances on swipe (fleet/photo-carousel-swipeable off origin/staging, 2026-11-18, Scout NOTE 255 build 2502 SPLIT) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Defect (Scout NOTE 255, Daniel fixture, build 2502):** image nodes rendered as REAL images but NOT as a carousel — no swipeable strip (the detail didn't advance on swipe, the tiles didn't scroll), and the aggregate showed only 4 of 7 (Daniel's 3 own appearance photos — face/body/candid — rendered as separate single "large embedded" images below the 4 web-research candidates).
- **Root cause (verified in code):** `_siblingWidgets` (`node_visualiser.dart`) grouped only **consecutive** report image leaves into a `NodePhotoCarousel`. The 3 appearance photos were not adjacent to the 4 web photos, so each non-adjacent image became its own single-image carousel (fill, no dots) = a "large embedded image". And `NodePhotoCarousel` was a one-image-per-screen square `PageView` — technically swipeable but a single-image carousel can't advance, so Scout read the split-out ones as static.
- **Fix:** (1) `_siblingWidgets` now aggregates **EVERY** image leaf in a sibling list into ONE carousel at the first image's slot (non-adjacent included); non-image nodes between them still render in place. (2) `NodePhotoCarousel` (`node_photo_carousel.dart`) is now a horizontal, swipeable `ListView` STRIP of square `NodeImage` `fill` tiles with per-tile captions that SCROLLS through all images; tapping a tile opens the fullscreen DETAIL (`AymaImageViewer.gallery`), a `PageView` that ADVANCES ON SWIPE. Strip height follows the viewer's text scale, captions cap at 2 ellipsised lines → no overflow. Carousel is built from the already viewer-filtered list → private/Ayma-only never leaks to public.
- **Scope:** Flutter report render only (`node_visualiser.dart`, `node_photo_carousel.dart`). No route/surface change → `app_registry.json` N/A; no new user-facing feature → tour N/A. `docs/PERSON_GRAPH.md` P0 carousel-aggregation note updated. Inference untouched (100% LiveKit).
- **Tests (all green, `test/unified_report_framework_test.dart`):** "NON-consecutive image nodes still assemble into ONE carousel of ALL of them" (7 images split by a finding → one carousel of 7, finding still renders), "the carousel is a horizontally swipeable strip across ALL images" (horizontal `ListView`, all 6 images, a drag scrolls it), "tapping a carousel tile opens the fullscreen detail — a PageView that advances on swipe", "privacy: a private image never enters the public-surface carousel"; existing consecutive-carousel + framework tests kept green (12 pass). `flutter analyze` 0; layout matrix green (81, incl. `/appearance/:id`).

## RELATED-LINKS — related links between graph nodes + root-"You"-biggest render (fleet/graph-related-links off origin/staging @cfb18fc3, 2026-11-18, founder order via Astro) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges), DO NOT apply backfill to his graph
- **Founder order:** the graph only shows parent→child; wire RELATED nodes together (same entity/topic across sections — the dog that came up under Family linked to the other dog notes). Plus two amendments: links only between **level ≥3** nodes (both ends), and the related-link reason recorded in a form the dedupe path can consume (data shape only, does NOT un-hold `fleet/zero-dup-leg28-rootcause`). Plus a folded render change: make the root **"You" node the BIGGEST** in every state.
- **Built (reuses the existing first-class `StrictLinkType.RELATED` link `{target, type, label=reason}`):**
  - *Model/mutuality:* `pg.related_targets`, `pg.mutual_related` (derives both directions so a seed-target reads back), `pg.eligible_for_related_link` + `RELATED_MIN_LEVEL=3`.
  - *Write-time proposer:* `person_graph_related.sweep_user` (flag `NODE_RELATED_PROPOSE`, off), session-end ALONGSIDE the dedupe sweep (unchanged). Reuses the dedupe candidate finder, drops level-0/1/2 pairs, LiveKit judge (own prompt, never rerouted) → CONFIDENT `related` = `store.link_related` (mutual), UNCERTAIN = `record_pending_dedupe` raise (never silent), `duplicate` = left to dedupe (not linked).
  - *Dedupe-consumable data:* `crosscheck.related_link_pairs(graph)` yields `(a,b,reason)` from stored links — data shape only, triggers no merge.
  - *Render (Flutter):* dashed accent related line (`_GraphPainter._paintRelatedEdge`, distinct from solid tree edges); tap lights related (already folded in `graphHighlightSet`); tappable "Related" chips in the node visualiser (`_RelatedRow`); **root "You" strictly largest** via `person_graph_layout.rootNodeRadius` + `SimNode(isRoot)` (render-only, independent of level/filter/highlight).
  - *Privacy (hard):* related traversal confined to the viewer's visibility set in render (`profile_graph._filter_related_to_visible`) AND lookup (`connected_node_ids(allowed_vis=…)` / `graph_lookup(public_only=…)`) — no cross-visibility leak of id or reason.
- **Founder decision (Nov 18):** level rule is now **LEVEL 2 AND UP** (both ends ≥2; levels 0/1 never link; You stays level 0) — `RELATED_MIN_LEVEL` 3→2.
- **Founder-graph DRY-RUN @ threshold=2 (ayma-staging, read-only, NOTHING written):** lexical pass over his 252-node graph = **74** candidates, **7 dropped** by the level rule → **67 proposed links** (pre-judge; the LiveKit judge then links CONFIDENT `related`, raises UNCERTAIN, and leaves same-fact `duplicate` to dedupe). Top section-pairs: misc↔misc 31, family↔misc 4, career↔misc 3, relationships↔relationships 3, finances↔goals 2, interests↔taste 2, misc↔relationships 2. Genuine cross-section samples: goals↔finances "early retirement (FIRE)"; looking_for↔goals "would relocate for the right person"; family sisters-dentists (grounding↔seed); social_circle "small close circle" pair. Some pairs are near-duplicates the judge will route to dedupe. **NOT applied — Astro reviews and green-lights the apply separately.**
- **TWO-"You" verdict (report only, NOT fixed):** RENDER ARTIFACT, not two root nodes. His `graph/main` holds **one** synthesized centre "You" (`profile_graph` always mints a single `id:"me"`), **no** node reads as "You", **no** level-0 duplicate. The two entries (green + yellow) are the detail panel echoing the centre node twice — the panel HEADER ("You", type/accent dot ≈ green) and the NodeVisualiser body row ("You", private-visibility dot = yellow) — because the centre node's body text is empty so `headerRestatesBody` doesn't collapse it.
- **Tests:** `tests/test_person_graph_related.py` (level rule; mutuality; proposer link/raise/skip/level-drop; dry-run writes nothing; flag-off; judge-failure→raise), `tests/test_related_links_privacy.py` (render drops cross-visibility link+reason; lookup `public_only` never reaches a private related node), `test/person_graph_layout_test.dart` ("root is the biggest" group), `test/person_graph_screen_test.dart` (relatedChipLabel; related highlight already covered). Backend suites green; `flutter analyze` 0; layout matrix green (33). Scout verifies the root-You-largest render on the founder graph on-device.

### HELD-APPLY JUDGE FIX (fleet/related-judge-fix off origin/staging, 2026-11-18, Astro ruling held the apply) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE, apply STAYS HELD for Astro's go
- **Three defects found in the held dry-run, all fixed (`person_graph_related.py` + `prompts/related_link_judge.md`):**
  1. *Same-fact mislabelled `related` (→ would LINK):* judge prompt tightened so a pair that restates the SAME underlying request/fact — even phrased as a different feature, wording or specificity — returns `duplicate` (→ dedupe), reserving `related` for genuinely different facts. Tripwire `emergent.assess.2c9fdabd ↔ a013429e` ("clear all notifications" ↔ "add a 'Mark all as read' button…") now routes to DEDUPE.
  2. *Judge truncation:* `_judge` hardcoded `max_tokens=1024` → verdict JSON truncated past ~40 pairs (whole array unparseable, all verdicts lost). Now BATCHED (`_JUDGE_BATCH=24`, per-batch `max_tokens=256+n*48`), verdicts merged by global `i`, a failed batch skips only its own pairs — same fix the dedupe judge got.
  3. *Bare-numeric-seed noise:* slider seeds whose value is a bare number ("4","5") "matched" on the digit via embedding cosine → noise. `_is_bare_numeric` drops any pair with a bare-number end from the candidate set (`dropped_numeric`); neither linked nor raised.
- **Founder-graph DRY-RUN re-run (ayma-staging, uid `iWhWjiWhEpU7iyu9B9vy7GlT0PD2`, branch code vs live DB + deployed staging LiveKit secrets, READ-ONLY, NOTHING written):** 241 nodes → 80 candidates → **4 dropped bare-numeric** (social_energy/capital_vs_experiential/cleanliness_index/extended_family_boundary digit matches — GONE), **7 dropped by level**, **69 judged in batches of 24 → 69 verdicts, NO TRUNCATION**. Buckets: **LINK 37, RAISE 0, DEDUPE 21, unrelated 11**. The notifications pair verdict = **`duplicate` → DEDUPE** (confirmed, not a link). **NOT applied — the apply stays HELD for Astro's explicit go.**
- **Tests added:** `test_same_fact_pair_routes_to_dedupe_not_link`, `test_judge_batches_large_set_without_truncation`, `test_bare_numeric_seeds_are_not_candidates` (all green; full `test_person_graph_related.py` 10/10, privacy + dedupe suites 62 green).

## EDIT-MANUALLY-LABEL-DOUBLING-CAREER — the fix covers the Career questionnaire-field node too (fleet/leg26-career-prefill off staging build 2501 @d0318556, 2026-11-18, Scout NOTE 254 Leg 26 retest SPLIT, build 2501) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Defect (Scout NOTE 254, Leg 26 retest, build 2501):** the 2501 fix cured label-doubling for the About-Me **Location** node (a STANDARD field), but the Career **"Role of work in life"** node still FAILED BOTH halves — Edit manually prefilled "Role of work in life: Balanced 9-to-5 / Priority on Leisure", and saving stored that labelled string so the row rendered DOUBLED ("Role of work in life: Role of work in life: …"), compounding each re-edit.
- **Root cause (verified in code):** the Career node is **NOT standard** — `career_integration_vector` (`question_tree.json:791`, label "Role of work in life") is not in `STANDARD_FIELDS` (`person_graph.py:106`, only the 6 identity fields). For a non-standard, non-emergent, non-narrative node the SERVER bakes the label into the text: `node_display_text` → `_answer_text` returns `f"{node.prompt}: {text}"` (`person_graph_render.py:135`). So the node arrives client-side with `standard:false`, `label:'Role of work in life'`, `text:'Role of work in life: Balanced…'`. The leg26 strip (`stripNodeOwnLabel`) gated on `node.standard` and used only `standardLabel`, so it passed this shape through untouched → prefill carried the label → save re-stored it → server prefixed again = doubled. (The leg26 TEST modelled this node as `standard:true`, which is why it passed in CI but failed on-device.)
- **Fix (client, `node_chat_window.dart`, generalized):** new `nodeOwnLabel(node)` returns the SINGLE label the render prefixes — `standardLabel` for a standard field, else `node.label` (the server-baked prompt) for a non-standard questionnaire-field node; `''` for a truly label-less node (emergent/narrative/child-bullet, empty label). `stripNodeOwnLabel` no longer gates on `node.standard`: it strips every leading "`<nodeOwnLabel>:`" prefix for BOTH shapes (idempotent, defensive against already-doubled stored data). `editableNodeValue` therefore prefills the bare value for the Career node too. Standard/Location behavior and truly label-less nodes are unchanged.
- **Scope:** Flutter manual-edit path only. No route/surface change → `app_registry.json` N/A; AlertDialog not a new route → layout matrix entry N/A; no new user-facing feature → tour N/A; diagram unchanged. Inference untouched (100% LiveKit).
- **Tests (all green, `test/node_visualiser_test.dart`):** new — "editableNodeValue bares the Career questionnaire-field node (non-standard)", "stripNodeOwnLabel strips a non-standard questionnaire-field own-label", "Edit manually bares + saves the Career node without doubling (re-edit idempotent)", "Edit manually recovers already-doubled stored Career data on save"; existing leg26/Location tests kept green (30 pass). `flutter analyze` 0; layout matrix green (28).

## EDIT-MANUALLY-LABEL-DOUBLING — value-only prefill + save guard against label-doubling (fleet/leg26-edit-label-doubling off staging build 2498 @7506cd74, 2026-11-18, Scout NOTE 252 Leg 26 SPLIT, build 2499) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Defect (Scout NOTE 252, Leg 26, build 2499):** tapping **Edit manually** on a node prefilled the field with the *rendered* "label: value" (e.g. "Role of work in life: Balanced 9-to-5 / Priority on Leisure"). Saving stored that whole string as the VALUE, so the standard-label render prefix doubled it to "Role of work in life: Role of work in life: Balanced…", and each re-edit added another copy (accumulated).
- **Root cause (verified in code):** a standard node renders as "`<standardLabel>: <value>`" (`node_visualiser.dart:254-267`). The stored value (`StoryNode.text`) is the bare value, but existing doubled DATA already carried the label baked in, and the prefill/save path did not strip it — so the field showed, and re-saved, the label-prefixed form, which the render then prefixed again.
- **Fix (client, `node_chat_window.dart`, two parts):** (1) **prefill = `editableNodeValue(node)`** — the bare value only (`StoryNode.text`), with any leading own-label stripped; a standard node with no value prefills EMPTY (the label is never put in as the value). (2) **save guard = `stripNodeOwnLabel(result, node)`** — before `graphSetNodeText`, strip every leading "`<standardLabel>:`" prefix so the stored value is the bare value; idempotent, and non-standard nodes (no rendered label prefix) are untouched. The label is re-applied at render time, never baked into the value. Defensive against already-doubled data and any paste of the rendered row.
- **Scope:** Flutter manual-edit path only (`node_chat_window.dart`). No route/surface change → `app_registry.json` N/A; AlertDialog, not a new route → layout matrix entry N/A; no new user-facing feature → tour N/A; diagram unchanged. Inference untouched (100% LiveKit).
- **Tests (all green, `test/node_visualiser_test.dart`):** "editableNodeValue is the bare value, never the rendered 'label: value'" (bare / doubled / tripled), "stripNodeOwnLabel is idempotent and leaves non-standard nodes alone", "Edit manually prefills the VALUE only and saves it without doubling", "Edit manually guard strips a leading own-label on save (no doubling)" (26 pass). `flutter analyze` 0; layout matrix green (28).

## LINKEDIN-WIPE-RECURRENCE — preserve linkedin_url across any profile/main rewrite (fleet/linkedin-wipe-recurrence off batch 2498 @7506cd74, 2026-11-18) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Report (OPEN 2u item 1 recurrence / 2j regression):** at the build-2498 gate, `profile/main.linkedin_url` read `None` again; the 2485 restore "didn't hold."
- **Verified against live ayma-staging data (read-only):** **FOUNDER** (`iWhWjiWhEpU7iyu9B9vy7GlT0PD2`) `profile/main.linkedin_url = None` (HIT — last profile write 2026-11-15, i.e. NOT written at the 02:06:52 PT launch; the None predates the gate). **demo-test1** (Daniel) `linkedin_url` is **PRESENT** (`…/in/daniel-reyes-ux`) — NOT currently wiped; its `profile/main` WAS written 02:06:52 PT but by a scoped merge that preserved the field, and it additionally **self-heals** because it has a `questionnaire_supplemental/linkedin-profile` entry that `career_timeline.get_linkedin_url` backfills on read.
- **Root cause (corrected report, verified in code):** NO current `profile/main` writer drops an existing `linkedin_url` — every writer is a scoped merge (`merge=True` on named fields, or `merge=[explicit paths]` in `skills/set_profile_field.py`); the only full-doc overwrites are the demo seeds (`seed_demo_accounts.py` carries linkedin; `practice_seed.py` only touches `demo_practice_*`). The real defect: the 2485 guard lives ONLY in `set_linkedin_url`, and **demo-test1 survived via its supplemental self-heal while the founder had NO fallback** (no `user_doc`/supplemental copy) — so once his URL was absent from `profile/main` it could never self-heal. The founder's URL was in fact shared on 2026-09-07 ("read the details from this LinkedIn profile") as a one-off read request and was never captured via `set_linkedin_profile` (no provenance, no supplemental). The 2485 restore "didn't hold" because it (or the original) wrote only `profile/main` directly, bypassing the durable mirror.
- **Fix (surgical, `career_timeline.py`):** added `_mirror_durable(db, uid, url)` — writes a known-good LinkedIn URL to ALL THREE stores (`profile/main` + user doc + `questionnaire_supplemental`), the two fallbacks being ones no profile rewrite touches. `set_linkedin_url` now uses it, and `get_linkedin_url` calls it when it recovers from a fallback (previously it re-backfilled `profile/main` only) — so a URL found in one store becomes durable everywhere and a later `profile/main` overwrite can never make it permanently unrecoverable again.
- **Restore (ayma-staging ONLY, founder uid + `linkedin_url` only, merge):** read-confirmed `None`, then restored `https://www.linkedin.com/in/suhailpallathsulaiman` (the user's own slug — present verbatim in his chat message, raw turns, and graph web-findings; tracking `utm_*` params dropped) to `profile/main` + user doc + supplemental. demo-test1 needed no restore (value intact).
- **Regression test:** `tests/test_career_timeline.py::test_linkedin_url_survives_profile_main_overwrite` — reproduces the founder's exact path (capture → full `profile/main` overwrite dropping the field → recover; then wipe both profile AND user doc → still recover from supplemental). Suites green: career_timeline + story_manage + deep_dive (199 passed, 1 skipped). Python-only; no Dart, no route/registry/tour change; diagram unchanged. Inference untouched (LiveKit).
## MID-SESSION-SIGNOUT — a transient token hiccup no longer signs the user out; voice refuses to go live signed out (fleet/mid-session-signout off clean batch 2498 @7506cd74, 2026-11-18) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Scout NOTE 252 (build 2498 gate):** during a Leg 41 story-scroll retest, at 02:31:51 PT Firebase Auth fired a SIGN-OUT mid-scroll — NO user action, NO restart (same PID 5450), NO ANR; the app routed to sign-in; then a voice session connected anyway WHILE SIGNED OUT, went live, and its context call 401'd. The bench couldn't establish the cause.
- **Root cause (VERIFIED in code — two co-located bugs, no logs needed):**
  - **The spontaneous sign-out** is the detached `_backgroundBootstrap` (`splash_screen.dart:482`), which runs `ApiClient.bootstrap()` AFTER the splash has already navigated to the cached route (user is in-app, scrolling). Its ID-token refresh threw a FirebaseAuthException that `ApiClient.bootstrap` (old `api_client.dart:732-760`) OVER-BROADLY classified as `AuthSessionException` — ANY code except `network-request-failed`, PLUS a catch-all turning ANY unexpected error into one. `_backgroundBootstrap`'s handler (`splash_screen.dart:493-502`) then calls `auth.signOut()` + `router.go(SignInScreen.path)` from that detached task → sign-out mid-scroll, same PID, no user action. A TRANSIENT refresh error (e.g. `too-many-requests` under a repeated-launch bench, an `internal-error`/`unknown`) was misread as a dead session.
  - **Voice going live signed out** is the sign-in screen auto-starting a `welcome: true` greeting session (`sign_in_screen.dart:206`), whose `VoiceSessionController._startScreenAwareness` did NOT exempt `welcome` — so it pushed the AUTHED `POST /voice/context` mirror, which 401s with no account (the "context call 401" Scout saw). The welcome greeting itself is intentionally pre-auth; its authed context mirror was the bug.
- **Fix 1 — session persists through a transient 401 / token-refresh hiccup (`api_client.dart`):** extracted the token-error classifier into pure `classifyBootstrapTokenError()`. It returns `AuthSessionException` (→ sign out) ONLY for a genuinely dead session — a definitively auth-invalid code (`kAuthInvalidTokenCodes`: user-disabled/not-found/token-expired/token-revoked/invalid-user-token/user-mismatch/invalid-credential/…) or an unreadable persisted credential (Auto-Backup keystore decrypt failure). EVERYTHING else — a token-refresh timeout, `network-request-failed`, `too-many-requests`, any transient `internal-error`/`unknown`, or any other unexpected error — returns a retryable connection-class `ApiException` (no statusCode), which the splash's `_isRetryable` RETRIES (foreground + background) and NEVER signs out. Real sign-out (user-initiated, account deleted/disabled) unchanged.
- **Fix 2 — voice refuses to go live without a valid auth session (`voice_session_controller.dart`):** the authed connect path now gates on an injected `isSignedIn` predicate (defaults to `FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser != null`, read only at runtime, tolerant of a Firebase-less test): a signed-out, non-welcome `start()` refuses with an honest error ("Sign in to Ayma to use voice.") and NEVER connects/bootstraps — no silent live-but-401 session (ties to the standing rule: voice unavailable shows an honest error, never a silent "Listening"). `_startScreenAwareness` now also skips for `welcome` (no account → never mirror to the authed `/voice/context`), and `_pushContext` skips when signed out (covers the gap between a mid-call sign-out and teardown's async `stop()`). Inference/voice stays 100% on LiveKit — an auth-gated refusal is an honest error, not a reroute.
- **Scope:** Flutter client only (api_client + voice controller). No route/surface change → `app_registry.json` N/A; no new user-facing feature → tour N/A; diagram unchanged. No backend/inference path touched.
- **Tests (green):** `test/api_client_test.dart` group `classifyBootstrapTokenError (mid-session sign-out regression)` — transient codes + unexpected errors are retryable `ApiException` not `AuthSessionException`; genuine auth-invalid codes + keystore-decrypt sign out; the null-token 401 passes through. `test/voice_session_controller_test.dart` — "start() REFUSES to go live when signed out (no connect, honest error)" (no connect, no bootstrap, no context push, error message) + "a signed-in session connects and goes live as normal". `flutter analyze` 0; layout matrix green.

## NO-TRUNCATION-PROSE-BULLETS — the derived gist is a COMPLETE line, never an ellipsis cut (fleet/no-truncation-prose-bullets off staging build 2497 @12dafe29, 2026-11-18, Scout Leg 41 SPLIT) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Scout Leg 41 (build 2496):** flush PASS, bullets-not-cards PASS, but **no-mid-sentence-truncation FAIL** — prose bullets STILL cut off with an ellipsis after ~two lines in every section (About Me, Interests, Family, Life Goals, Values), on Story AND reports. The 2495 indent fix had already removed every `maxLines`/`TextOverflow.ellipsis` cap in `node_visualiser.dart` (existing Dart regressions "a long gist renders in FULL, no ellipsis" (visualiser)+(report section) prove the renderer wraps full). **The remaining cap was NOT in Flutter.**
- **Root cause (verified in code):** the ellipsis came from the BACKEND gist derivation — `functions/bootstrap/node_gist.py:_trim` cut the fallback gist to `GIST_MAX` (80 chars) on a word boundary and **appended `…`** (old `node_gist.py:43-53`). This `derive_gist` is the single shared gist source for BOTH surfaces — `report_tree.py:210` (reports) and `person_graph_tree.py:305,324` (Story) — so one chop hit both (Scout's "affected family"). At phone width the 80-char cut reads as ~2 lines ending in "…" — exactly the mid-sentence truncation Scout saw.
- **Fix (surgical, `node_gist.py`):** removed `GIST_MAX` and `_trim` entirely. The fallback is now the FIRST COMPLETE SENTENCE of the text (`_SENTENCE_END` split), trailing punctuation stripped, returned **in full** — no length cap, no ellipsis; a single run-on line returns whole. A STORED writer gist still wins (unchanged). The full prose stays in the node's `text` for the tap-in. Honors the founder order (Nov 17 20:57): "no text should cut off partially … the row shows its complete stored gist … the tap opens the full detail as already designed — the visible line itself is never a chopped sentence."
- **Leg 38 determination (STATED, no change):** tapping a gist line opening the Ask Ayma sheet IS the intended design (founder: "the tap opens the full detail as already designed"). In-place expansion was NOT ordered and is NOT implemented. The sheet path (full prose + owner controls) is untouched.
- **Out of scope (named):** `skill_memory.py:_clip_gist` (`_GIST_MAX` 100 + "…") is the web-findings/report **verdict headline** (skill cards), NOT the prose body bullet — intentionally a short headline, left as-is. The `small_table` cell cap (`node_visualiser.dart:423`) stays (a fixed tabular slot).
- **Scope:** backend gist derivation + tests only. No Dart source change (renderer already correct); added one Dart regression for the exact failing path. No route/surface change → `app_registry.json` N/A; no new user-facing feature → tour N/A; diagram unchanged. Inference untouched (100% LiveKit).
- **Tests (all green):** Python `tests/test_node_gist.py` — updated `…_long_single_sentence_is_returned_in_full_no_ellipsis`, new `…_long_first_sentence_is_returned_in_full_no_ellipsis`, new surface regressions `test_person_graph_tree_long_about_me_bullet_gist_has_no_ellipsis` + `test_report_tree_long_text_block_gist_has_no_ellipsis` (12 pass; node_gist+person_graph_tree+report_tree 48 pass). Dart `test/node_visualiser_test.dart` — new "REGRESSION: a long About-Me section-member bullet renders in FULL, no ellipsis, spanning >2 lines (story)" (22 pass). `flutter analyze` 0; layout matrix green (28).

## STORY-SECTION-INDENT-FIX — flush bullets + prose-as-bullets + no truncation (fleet/story-section-indent-fix off staging build 2495, 2026-11-17) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Founder batch (build 2495), three fixes in one branch:**
- **(a) Indentation made no sense.** On his own profile under About Me, free bullets after the standard items rendered INDENTED as if children of the last standard item ("Interested in: Women"). Root cause (verified in the staging graph): those nodes (`emergent.story.5c5470d1`, `emergent.story.e1ff8e89`, `emergent.editor.identity.5`, `emergent.story.b7aba075`, `emergent.story.d755457b`) carry NO parent yet sit at LEVEL 2, and `node_visualiser.dart` indented by a level-floor (`max(level − 1, depth)`). A RENDER artifact. **Fix:** `_indentSteps` now indents ONLY by real depth under a TRUE PARENT (`depth`), capped at 5; `level` no longer touches list/tree indentation. A section member renders FLUSH at the section base; a node indents only when it genuinely renders under a parent. The six fixed About Me standard items stay ordered first (backend `_order_key`) and all render flush. **"Ethnic & cultural origin" finding:** the circled one is NOT the fixed `ethnic_cultural_origin` standard field — it is an emergent free bullet (`emergent.editor.identity.5`) carrying the same text, a parentless level-2 section root indented only by the old level-floor; flush now. (Its duplication vs the standard field is the Leg-28 dedupe family, OUT of scope.) **`level` still drives node SIZE in the person-graph view (`person_graph_layout.dart`) — untouched.**
- **(b) Prose rendered as grey card/chip bubbles** (My public profile → Interests) instead of bullet points. Root cause: the read-time default derivation shaped list-like-section prose into `chip_set` (a one-item chip bubble). **Fix:** `node_visual.default_for`/`for_section` + `person_graph_tree._default_visual` now derive `gist_row` (a BULLET) for ALL prose, every section; structure comes ONLY from a genuine signal — an image → `photo_carousel`, a node with a real DATE → `timeline_entry` (read-time), and explicit writer picks for `chip_set` (discrete `items`), `bar_meter`/`gauge` (scale), `card` (title+body finding). Ayma never picks `card` for a plain fact. The old per-section `chip_set`/`timeline` defaults for undated prose are removed.
- **(c) Text cut off mid-sentence** (Web research report Summary/Facts: "completed a Ma…", "in Color…"). **Fix:** removed the `maxLines`/`TextOverflow.ellipsis` caps on BODY + GIST text in `node_visualiser.dart` (the standard-label `RichText`, the gist `Text`, the bar label, the timeline title) — gist/bullet text WRAPS in full, never chopped. **Surviving ellipsis (safe, named):** ONLY the `small_table` cell — a fixed column slot of genuine tabular data (an unbounded cell would misalign the grid); a strict fixed slot MAY keep a clean cap. Chip labels / short value readouts are short by construction. **Confidence row:** its "High" band was baseline-aligned (a pill has no baseline), floating it against the card edge; now the pill row centre-aligns and lays the pill out INLINE (`Flexible`), no overlap.
- **Scope:** Flutter render + backend read/write visual-default only. No route/surface change → `app_registry.json` N/A; no new user-facing feature → tour N/A. Diagram unchanged (no structural change). Inference 100% LiveKit — nothing rerouted.
- **Tests (all green):** Dart `test/node_visualiser_test.dart` — "REGRESSION: parentless level-2 section members render FLUSH at base level…", "six fixed About Me standard items render flush, in fixed order", "nested tree indents children by TRUE parentage", "REGRESSION: a long gist renders in FULL, no ellipsis (visualiser)" + "(report section)", "confidence band lays out inline, no overflow". Python `tests/test_person_graph_tree.py` prose-interest → `gist_row`, `tests/test_report_tree.py` prose + confidence → `gist_row`, `tests/test_gist_first_writing.py` updated to prose → `gist_row` (dated/explicit picks preserved). Python tree/visual suites 45/45 + 57 across touched files; Dart node/report/story/profile suites 111 + node_visualiser 21 green; layout matrix green (28); `flutter analyze` 0. Screenshot evidence: `ayma_flutter/test/story_section_indent_screenshot_test.dart` → `test/goldens/story_about_me_flush_bullets.png` (standard items first, free bullets flush, bullets not bubbles) + `test/goldens/web_report_full_bullets_confidence.png` (full-length bullets, inline Confidence).

## PERSON-GRAPH-LAYOUT-REALFIX — real 221/122-node graph renders as a fitted mesh + Hide-all filter shows selected types (fleet/person-graph-layout-realfix off staging build 2495, 2026-11-17) — DONE on branch, pushed, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges) — TOP-PRIORITY FOUNDER GATE
- **Reproduced on the founder's REAL graph** (uid iWhWjiWh…, read-only ayma-staging dump → `ayma_flutter/test/fixtures/founder_real_graph.dart`, 122 nodes/121 edges, dominated by ONE `sec:assessment` hub with 96 degree-1 children). Prior synthetic fixtures (12 clean sections) never had a high-degree hub → passed CI while his graph failed. This is why the gate kept reopening.
- **Bug 1 (pink streak/comet tail, VERIFIED cause):** the force sim EXPLODES on a high-degree hub — a node with ~95 neighbours inside the repel cutoff gains ~95×`_repelCap` (~2850) velocity in ONE step, which cooling + 0.8 decay can't absorb → `sec:assessment` flies to ~1.17M px, bounds aspect ~4.6, `fitToViewport` clamps to scale 0.02 → off-screen knot + comet tail. The soft global containment missed it (self-scales to the exploded mean). FIX: per-step velocity clamp `_maxSpeed` 40 in `GraphSim.step` — integration stays stable, real graph settles to a fitted mesh (ratio 98→2.6, aspect 4.6→1.2, scale 0.02→0.50), no equilibrium change for normal graphs. BEFORE (pg_real_before.png = streak) / AFTER (pg_real_after.png = mesh).
- **Bug 2 (Hide-all → tap chips → EMPTY canvas, folded into same branch per founder):** `_passesFilter` AND-gates `_hiddenKinds` AND `_hiddenLevels`; Hide-all hid BOTH, so un-hiding one KIND left all levels hidden → chip lit, canvas empty. FIX: pure `toggleKindFilter`/`toggleLevelFilter` release the vestigial all-hidden OTHER facet on un-hide → tap a chip after Hide-all shows exactly that type; more chips ADD; Show-all restores. The old screen test only checked the chip LIT (enshrined the bug); now it counts lit canvas pixels.
- **Flutter-only, no backend/inference change (100% LiveKit).** No new route → no `app_registry.json`/tour change. Diagram unchanged (forces + filter logic only).
- **Tests:** `test/person_graph_real_layout_test.dart` (real fixture: finite, max ≤5×median [fail-before ~98×], aspect ≤2.5 [~4.6], fit scale >0.1 [~0.02], both axes framed); `test/person_graph_filter_state_test.dart` (Hide-all→select exactly that type, ADD, symmetric level release, Show-all); `test/person_graph_screen_test.dart` canvas-pixel regression; `test/person_graph_real_screenshot_test.dart` (BEFORE/AFTER). `flutter analyze` 0; layout matrix green. Docs: `PERSON_GRAPH.md` → real-graph explosion + Hide-all fix.
## REPORT-NO-DUP-FINDINGS — a report never shows the same point twice; recommendations go last (fleet/report-no-dup-findings off staging build 2495, 2026-11-17) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Founder report (screenshot, build 2495, Appearance report):** the report listed FINDINGS as bullets ("Overgrown mustache hanging over the upper lip line", "Under-eye fatigue and slight puffiness", …) and then RECOMMENDATIONS as MORE bullets that RESTATED the same findings ("Trim the mustache clear of the upper lip…", "Introduce a caffeinated eye serum…"). "this is duplicating what is shown above already — the report should not show the same node twice." Founder correction: do NOT fold each recommendation into its finding — instead MOVE recommendations to the BOTTOM as their own "what to do next" final section; findings first, actions last; recommendations phrased as actions, not restatements.
- **Assembler (`functions/bootstrap/report_tree._finalize_sections`, applied to EVERY report on the unified framework):** (1) **recommendations-last** — a recommendation block is marked `role="recommendation"` (`report_blocks.fact_block`, set by `scored_result_blocks` for every face/body reco); the assembler lifts every recommendation node out of its section and collects them, in order, into one trailing **"What to do next"** section (slug `what_to_do_next`). Findings + their stats render first; the actions last. (2) **node-ID dedupe across sections** — a node id renders at most once (first wins; a repeat in a later section is skipped); sections emptied by the lift/dedupe are dropped. Generalizes to any findings+recommendations report (opt-in by the `role` marker; deep_dive/match/story set no role → unchanged).
- **Prompts (`prompts/face_report.md`, `body_report.md`):** each recommendation must be phrased as the ACTION to take and must NOT restate the finding it addresses (the observation is already shown above).
- **No app route/surface change** (`app_registry.json` N/A); the new "What to do next" section renders through the existing `GraphTreeView(surface: report)` — **no Dart UI change** (the other agent's `node_visualiser.dart` indent/ellipsis work untouched). Feature tour N/A; diagram text updated (ARCHITECTURE.md unified-report-framework "No point shown twice + findings-first ordering"). Inference untouched (100% LiveKit — no inference path here).
- **Tests:** NEW in `functions/bootstrap/tests/test_report_tree.py` (`test_recommendations_are_lifted_into_one_final_section`, `test_a_node_in_two_sections_renders_only_once`); `test_report_blocks.py` extended (reco carries `role="recommendation"`, a finding carries none); NEW `ayma_flutter/test/appearance_report_no_dup_screenshot_test.dart` (findings-first / "What to do next"-last render through the real `GraphTreeView`, PNG saved). Python report suites 104/104 + appearance 106/106 green; `flutter analyze` 0; layout matrix green.

## NODE-CARD-DUP-COLLAPSE — a node context card no longer prints the same statement twice (fleet/node-card-dup-collapse off staging build 2494, 2026-11-17) — DONE on branch, pushed, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Founder report (screenshot, build 2493):** tapping a Values node opened the partial "Ask Ayma" sheet whose context card printed the SAME sentence twice — once as the small-caps header and once as the italic quote. Verified in the staging graph: emergent NARRATIVE nodes stored the identical statement in BOTH the `prompt` (label) and the `answer` (text) field (e.g. `emergent.assess.ab71fdd6`, `emergent.story.b910b0fa`), and the card rendered both.
- **Render side (collapse existing duplicated nodes):** new `normalizeForDisplayMatch` / `headerRestatesBody` in `ayma_flutter/lib/models/story_node.dart` (lowercase + whitespace/punctuation-collapsed, mirroring the backend dedupe `person_graph_index.normalize_text`). A surface that renders a node's header PLUS body shows the statement once when the two normalize-equal, keeping both when they differ: the Ask Ayma context card (`chat_screen.dart` `_SelectedQuote` — `quoteTitle` header vs `quoteText` quote), the graph inspector detail header (`person_graph_screen.dart` `_VisualiserPanel` — `n.label` vs the visualiser body), and the visualiser node card (`node_visualiser.dart` `_cardContent` — card `title` vs `body`).
- **Write side (stop minting new duplicates):** `person_graph_store._prompt_without_restatement(prompt, answer)` blanks the `prompt` when it merely restates the `answer` (reuses `normalize_text`); wired into every emergent narrative writer — `add_node`, `add_loose_node`, `upsert_fact_node` (the `ai_assessment._write_graph` path — a genuinely distinct label like "Ayma's assessment — …" is preserved), and `replace_section_bullets` (the Your Story editor / "story writer"). `merge_node` (`person_graph.py`) now preserves an EXPLICIT empty prompt (`o.prompt == ""` → statement lives in the answer only) while still synthesizing one for a prompt that is MISSING entirely (`None` — the legacy 54-node partial-write protection, unchanged). **No visibility or other field touched (P0).** Existing duplicated nodes are NOT backfilled — the render collapse covers them.
- **Backend + Flutter only, no route/surface change** → `app_registry.json` N/A, feature tour N/A, diagram unchanged (no structural change). Inference untouched (100% LiveKit).
- **Tests:** NEW `ayma_flutter/test/node_context_card_dedup_test.dart` (a prompt==answer node on the context card renders the statement ONCE, the upper-cased header collapsed; a distinct header like "Projects" preserved). NEW write-side tests in `functions/bootstrap/tests/test_person_graph_store.py` (`upsert_fact_node` without a label stores the statement once + the tree builds cleanly with `label == ""`, text once; a distinct label preserved; `replace_section_bullets` stores once). Python person-graph suite 140/140; touched Dart suites + layout matrix (90) green; `flutter analyze` 0.

## ZERO-DUP-LEG28-ROOTCAUSE — the render dedup is working; the remaining Daniel pairs are TRUE PARAPHRASES for the DATA SWEEP (fleet/zero-dup-leg28-rootcause off staging, 2026-11-17, NOTE 252 build 2493 FAIL) — DONE on branch, pushed, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Scout NOTE 252 Leg 28/39 (build 2493):** every 2491 duplicate pair STILL rendered on the Daniel fixture (`demo-test1`), graph still ~213 rendered / 232 stored nodes; the 2492 render fix + 2493 server sweep changed neither.
- **Root cause (verified against the REAL staging Daniel graph, not guessed):** TWO separate causes.
  1. **Almost every listed pair is a TRUE PARAPHRASE** — distinct content-token sets, not stopword/word-order variants. Social-circle close-friend ("small, loyal group…share quiet evenings, long conversations" vs "small, devoted circle…appreciate good food and meaningful conversation"), the attentive-listener pair, the Life-Goals discipline triple (3 differently-worded nodes across ayma_only/public/private), Finances hybrid triple, Relationships long-term triple, the Family restatements/supersets, pottery/fiction/films, coffee (career_role vs career_trajectory), the Taste labelled-vs-unlabelled pairs ("Music: Bon Iver…" vs "Bon Iver…", differ only by the non-standard label word). The render dedup is **exact section-scoped token-set equality** (`person_graph_tree._build_node`, `seen_tokens`), so it correctly CANNOT and MUST NOT collapse these — forcing them at render time would hide genuinely-distinct wording. These are the **DATA sweep's** job (embedding-NN + LiveKit judge in `person_graph_dedupe_sweep.py`), and the sweep **never auto-ran on the fixture** (triggers only on conversation-session-end + nightly backstop; nobody converses as Daniel). Confirmed with a read-only dump: only 8 exact-token-set collisions exist in the whole graph and NONE are the Scout pairs.
  2. **ONE genuine render bug — the Gender pair.** The editor bullet "Gender identity: Man" rendered beside the standard Gender row "Man". `render.text_restates_standard_fact` suppresses a restatement only when its text equals the bare value OR its token set equals the standard field's `label+value` tokens. The bullet's self-authored label ("Gender identity") differs by a word from the field label ("Gender"), so `{gender,identity,man} ≠ {gender,man}` and it survived — while its 5 siblings ("Age: 29", "Interested in: Women", …) were correctly suppressed because their labels match. **Fix:** `text_restates_standard_fact` also matches when the text after the FIRST colon normalises exactly to the bare value (high precision; section-restricted; a longer distinct sentence never has a colon tail equal to the bare value). Verified on the live fixture: identity dropped 10→9, the Gender bullet is suppressed, every paraphrase pair still renders (no over-suppression).
- **Resolution split:** render fix ships the Gender pair; **all other pairs resolve only when the DATA sweep runs on the fixture** (run manually — see below — or wait for a Daniel conversation / nightly). Paraphrase pairs are collapsed by the SWEEP, not the renderer, by design.
- **Backend-only** (no route/surface change → `app_registry.json` N/A, tour N/A, diagram N/A). Inference untouched (sweep judge stays 100% LiveKit; embeddings Vertex, founder-approved).
- **Tests:** NEW `functions/bootstrap/tests/test_person_graph_tree.py::test_editor_bullet_with_differently_worded_label_restates_standard_fact` (pumps the exact Daniel Gender shape — standard Gender "Man" + editor "Gender identity: Man" — asserts the bullet is suppressed and a distinct fact survives). Full render/tree/store/dedupe/sweep suites green (101 passed).
- **PART B — ran the Layer-2 dedupe sweep manually on the Daniel fixture (`demo-test1`), ayma-staging ONLY, DRY-RUN.** One-off Cloud Run job on the live staging image (`ayma-api-staging@sha256:d8bac480…`, project ayma-ai, SA `235381544962-compute`), staging env+secrets replicated + `GRAPH_DEDUPE_SWEEP=true`/`GRAPH_EMBEDDING_DEDUPE=true`, `tools/dedupe_sweep_run.py demo-test1 --project=ayma-staging` (tool aborts unless project==ayma-staging), `--max-retries=0`, job DELETED after.
  - **Confirmed the sweep never auto-ran on the fixture:** it triggers only on conversation-session-end + a nightly backstop; nobody converses as Daniel and no nightly has swept it → stored graph still **232 nodes** (Scout's "213" is the rendered count). This manual run is the first time it ran.
  - **Dry-run result:** indexed 232 nodes, **PAIRS FOUND 68**, **WOULD-MERGE 10**, **RAISED 58**. Social-circle close-friend pair (`emergent.story.370859da ? 71dc17bf`) FOUND → raised. The Taste labelled/unlabelled pairs (`b658dd72 ? taste_music`, etc.) FOUND → raised. The attentive-listener pair (`9d796a04 ? db9dfced`) was **NOT surfaced as a candidate at all** (see finding 2).
  - **BLOCKER found — the sweep judge truncates (fixed here).** The LiveKit judge replied CORRECTLY (verdicts for pairs 0..63 visible in logs) but the reply hit `finish_reason=length` at 2302 chars and the JSON failed to parse → every verdict lost → sweep degraded to the LEXICAL split (only the 10 EXACT-text pairs in WOULD-MERGE; all 58 near-dups/paraphrases RAISED). Root cause: `person_graph_crosscheck._judge` hard-coded `max_tokens=1024` and judged ALL pairs in ONE call. **Fix (this branch):** `_judge` now batches pairs (`_JUDGE_BATCH=24`, per-batch `max_tokens`), merges verdicts by each item's global `i`, and a single failing batch is skipped without nuking the rest (returns None only on total failure). 3 regression tests added. This is NOT a LiveKit reroute — inference stays on LiveKit; the model was fine, the completion budget was the bug.
  - **Finding 2 (embeddings) — secondary, not fixed here.** Despite `GRAPH_EMBEDDING_DEDUPE=true` (flag maps correctly to `CONFIG.graph_embedding_dedupe`), candidate finding ran LEXICAL (reason strings are "same topic (tokens)", not "semantic match ~0.NN"; no embedding logs, no "using lexical" warning) → `graph_embeddings.refresh_vectors`/`load_vectors` returned no vectors and `candidate_report` silently fell back. Consequence: low-lexical-overlap paraphrases like the attentive-listener pair (shares only {attentive, friends}) are never even surfaced as candidates. Worth a follow-up (why Vertex embedding vectors don't populate in the job) — the embedding-NN finder is what Leg 39's attentive-listener pair needs.
  - **DID NOT run `--apply`.** The DEPLOYED staging image still has the truncating judge (the fix above is on this branch, not deployed), so an apply now would merge only the 10 exact pairs and write 58 broken-judge "raise" records to the shared fixture WITHOUT resolving the Scout paraphrase pairs. The correct resolution: merge this branch → staging deploys the judge-batch (+ Part A render) fix → re-run the sweep `--apply` (or a Daniel session/nightly) → the paraphrases are judged "duplicate" and merged, the fixture shrinks, and Leg 28/39 pairs clear. Node count before = 232; after = N/A (not applied). ayma-staging ONLY; no prod/ayma-ai DB touched; job deleted.

## STORY-PHOTO-TILES-FIX — re-mint frozen image-node URLs at serve time (fleet/story-photo-tiles-fix off staging 4a58979e, 2026-11-17, build 2490, NOTE 252 Chunk 2) — DONE on branch, pushed, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges); needs Scout on the Daniel fixture
- **Defect (Scout NOTE 252, Leg 6, build 2490, Daniel fixture):** in "Your Story" ALL FOUR story photo tiles showed the broken-image glyph and the header image was a dark empty rectangle — the image nodes did not load.
- **Root cause (serve-side, verified from code):** a Story photo tile's URL is frozen in the image node's `answer` at ingest (`person_graph_store.add_image_node`, minted once by `public_profile._photo_url`). `/story/photos` (`main._photo_nodes_for_view` → `image_nodes_for_view` → `url = node.answer`) and the person-graph tree (`person_graph_tree._image_fields` → `image_url = node.answer`) served that frozen URL verbatim and NEVER re-resolved it. A Firebase download token rotates on object re-upload (a demo `--reset`), and a URL minted in one environment's bucket (a prod↔staging copy / a bucket recreated under the same name) carries a token the current object doesn't match — either way every frozen URL 403s → broken glyph on every tile. The cover photo escaped only because the public profile re-mints its grid fresh from the storage PATH; the node path never did (and `photo_notes.resolve_storage_path` even refused any cross-bucket URL).
- **Fix (serve-time re-resolution, honest):** new `public_profile.refresh_storage_url(url)` recovers the object path from any firebasestorage URL (bucket-agnostic) and re-mints a loadable download URL via `_photo_url` against the bucket THIS backend serves. Wired into `main._photo_nodes_for_view` (the four tiles) and `routes/profile._refresh_tree_image_urls` (walks the story/public graph tree, NodeImage's source). A web-search photo passes through unchanged; an object that genuinely can't be resolved falls back to the original URL — never a fabricated placeholder, never worse than today. The Flutter render side was already correct (given a loadable URL, `NodeImage`/`AymaPhoto` paint the photo, not the glyph).
- **Backend fix** (no app route/surface change → `app_registry.json` N/A, feature tour N/A, diagram N/A). Inference untouched (100% LiveKit — storage token minting is not an inference path).
- **Tests:** NEW `functions/bootstrap/tests/test_story_photo_tile_url_refresh.py` (re-mint stale-token + cross-bucket URLs, web/empty pass-through, honest original-URL fallback, nested-tree walk — fails-before / passes-after, 6/6); NEW `ayma_flutter/test/story_photo_tile_render_test.dart` (a resolvable image node paints the photo not the broken glyph, four-tile **golden** `test/goldens/story_photo_tiles_loaded.png`, empty-URL negative control). `test_public_profile.py` + `test_image_nodes.py` 43/43 green; layout matrix green; `flutter analyze` 0.
- **Residual (data, not code):** if the Daniel fixture's Storage objects are absent from build 2490's bucket entirely, the real remedy is re-seeding demo accounts on that environment (`scripts/seed_demo_accounts.py`, a Muse/founder op) — the code fix resolves every case where the object exists at the path in the served bucket.

## PERSON-GRAPH-LAYOUT-FIX — graph opens fitted/spread/readable, no far streak or corner blob (fleet/person-graph-layout-fix off staging, 2026-11-17, build 2491) — DONE on branch, pushed, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges) — TOP-PRIORITY settled-design GATE
- **Founder report (build 2490, screenshot):** "My person graph" showed a thin PINK STREAK of edges to one distant point with every cluster crushed into a tiny corner blob — "is this a distance issue, why so far." The 2490 sections fix (radial seed + fitToViewport) did not cure it.
- **Root cause (reproduced on ~195-node founder-shaped data, not guessed):** an EDGELESS node — a cross-surface emergent fact (story/publicProfile/report/graphDetail) that never linked to a section — has NO spring pull; under only the weak `_gravity` (0.0035) against full cluster repulsion it drifts to the gravity/repulsion equilibrium ring (~680px) while the connected tree stays ~350px. That lone far node dominates `graphBounds`, so `fitToViewport` zooms far out (scale ~0.28) and crushes the dense cluster into a corner; the per-tick open fit even follows it outward (0.53→0.28). The "pink streak" = the fit framing an orphan the rim can't pull back.
- **Fix (force cause, not a post-hoc clamp — `person_graph_layout.dart`):** every degree-0 node (except centre) gets a BOUNDED virtual anchor spring to the centre (`_orphanAnchorRest` 170px, `_orphanAnchorK` 0.045) — layout-only, never a drawn edge — so it settles INSIDE the cluster (measured 143–280px) like a real leaf; orphans also seed on a tight ring near that radius (not the wide spiral) so the fit stays framed from the first frame; integration resets any non-finite position to the origin (one stray NaN can't poison `graphBounds`). With no outlier, `fitToViewport` of all nodes frames the true tight spread cluster.
- **Extension (18:20 report):** a second screenshot found the lone far node with the Skills filter ON was the Ayma-ASSESSMENT root — DEGREE-1 (one edge to its hub), which the degree-0 anchor missed. Verified vs backend topology + in-tree: a degree-1 node tied to the pinned centre does NOT drift (its spring holds it at ~80-140px even flung to 4000); what the sim couldn't bound is a weakly-connected component with no tie to centre (flung → stays ~4000, ratio ~14). Fix: a soft GLOBAL containment in `GraphSim.step` — self-scaling bound `max(600, 2.2× mean non-centre radius)`, gentle capped linear pull only BEYOND it, so interior/sprung nodes (incl. the degree-0 anchor) are untouched and only a genuine runaway is caught (flung component → ratio ~1.7). Screenshot re-rendered with Skills filter ON (assessment inside the fitted cluster, no off-screen edge).
- **Flutter-only, no backend/inference change (100% LiveKit unaffected).** No new route → no `app_registry.json`/tour change (existing `open_person_graph`). Diagram unchanged (no structural change).
- **Tests:** `test/person_graph_outlier_fit_test.dart` — founder-shaped fixture (12 weighted sections of cross-surface facts + 5 edgeless orphans): settled max distance ≤ 2.2× median AND every orphan ≤ 1.2× median (fail-before: orphans ~580 over median ~325; pass-after: inside the cluster), and fit puts every node on-screen with the cluster filling ≥60% width centred (not a corner). `test/person_graph_fit_screenshot_test.dart` — pumps the REAL `PersonGraphScreen` on founder-shaped data (reduced motion) and captures the fitted open frame to `pg_layout_fitted.png` (fitted/spread/readable, no streak). `flutter analyze` 0; layout matrix green (29). Docs: `PERSON_GRAPH.md` → "outlier + Fit crush fix".
## ACCENT-SINGLE-PAGE — combine the two accent surfaces into one page (fleet/accent-single-page off staging 4a58979e, 2026-11-17, build 2491) — DONE on branch, pushed, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Founder order (build 2491):** "we do not need an extra step to go to the accent color page — combine both." Appearance already showed preset swatches inline AND its Accent Color row pushed a separate standalone Accent Color page with the full picker. Make Appearance the ONLY accent surface; delete the standalone page.
- **What changed (`ayma_flutter/lib/screens/appearance_settings_screen.dart`):** the Accent Color row that pushed `AccentColorScreen` is gone; the full custom picker (hue strip, saturation/brightness sliders, neutral chips, live `AymaOrb` preview, "Use this color", "Reset to default") moved INLINE under the theme-mode radios, alongside the existing preset swatches + camera/eyedropper entry. The screen became stateful: `initState` captures `_savedColor = _accent.color` (the revert baseline), `dispose()` calls `_accent.cancelPreview(_savedColor)` (revert-on-leave, no write). The Nov-17 realtime model is carried over verbatim — EVERY pick (preset tap, hue drag, slider, neutral chip, camera sample) is a live preview only; **"Use this color" is the SOLE persist** (`_accent.set`, + `saveCustomColor` for a non-preset so it becomes the Custom swatch); "Reset to default" previews the default without persisting. Camera (`pickFromCamera`) hands its sampled colour back as the current pick WITH live preview and does NOT save.
- **Deleted:** `ayma_flutter/lib/screens/accent_color_screen.dart` (standalone page + `_HueStripPainter`, moved into Appearance), its `GoRoute` (`/settings/accent-color`) + import in `app_router.dart`, its `navigation_history.dart` name case, its `layout_targets.dart` matrix entry + import, and `test/accent_color_screen_test.dart`.
- **Registry (`functions/bootstrap/app_registry.json`):** removed the `accent_color` *destination* (route gone); folded its aliases ("accent color", "colour picker", …) into the `appearance_settings` destination; repointed the `accent_color` client *action*'s `found_on` to `appearance_settings`. `camera_color_picker` destination unchanged.
- **Tour:** anchor `settings_accent_row` already spotlights the Appearance row on Settings (not the deleted page) — no repoint needed. `tour_content.dart` had no accent entry.
- **Tests:** `test/appearance_settings_screen_test.dart` rewritten to the new contract — presets preview live, custom picker (hue drag) previews live, "Use this color" persists (local + server), leaving without saving reverts + zero writes, commit-then-leave keeps it, reset previews-only, camera return lands as an UNSAVED pick, a committed non-preset becomes the Custom swatch (10/10). `settings_cleanup_test.dart` updated for the inline surface (3 tests). Registry tests updated (python `test_app_registry.py` / `test_app_control.py`, dart `app_registry_test.dart`). `flutter analyze` 0; layout matrix green; app_registry python 443 passed. Pre-existing unrelated reds on base (settings "Default assistant app" row; `test_app_action_graph` backtick check) left as-is. No inference touched — nothing rerouted off LiveKit.

### Follow-up: revert-on-leave stayed on the unsaved colour (Scout Leg 36, build 2495, `fleet/accent-revert-on-leave` off staging 41bc36c6) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, NOT-MERGED
- **Founder/Scout bug:** leaving Appearance twice without "Use this color" left the live app theme stuck on the unsaved preview (stored value stayed correct green; restart restored it → in-memory only).
- **Verified root cause (not a baseline/leak bug):** `dispose()` called `_accent.cancelPreview(_savedColor)` synchronously, which fires `notifyListeners()` while the widget tree is LOCKED (finalizeTree/unmount phase). A theme `AnimatedBuilder` listening to the controller then threw `markNeedsBuild called when widget tree was locked`; that thrown error aborted the notification dispatch mid-iteration, so the MaterialApp theme builder never received the revert and the theme stayed on the preview (worse on the 2nd/3rd leave, where more transition `AnimatedBuilder`s are mid-teardown). Reproduced only via REAL push/pop navigation — the old `pumpWidget`-replacement `leave()` test force-disposed and never hit the locked phase.
- **Fix (`appearance_settings_screen.dart` dispose only):** defer the revert one frame — capture `_accent` + `_savedColor` into locals, then `WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) => accent.cancelPreview(saved))`. Runs after the frame when the tree is unlocked → clean repaint every leave, including the 2nd/3rd. Realtime preview + single-save behaviour (`preview`/`set`/`cancelPreview`) UNTOUCHED; revert baseline is still only the last SAVED value.
- **Tests:** added to `appearance_settings_screen_test.dart` — "leaving via a real pop reverts the live theme to the saved colour" + "leave twice without saving still reverts on the SECOND leave" (both assert reverted accent value, `takeException()` null, zero server/local writes). Confirmed both FAIL on the unfixed dispose (locked-tree throw) and PASS with the fix. Full file 12/12; `flutter analyze` 0; layout matrix green (28). No inference touched.
## UNIFIED VISUAL REPORT FRAMEWORK — ALL reports → one node-backed visual page (fleet/visual-report-framework off fleet/visual-node-renderers @cbaeb43c, contains Parts 1+2+3, 2026-11-17) — DONE on branch, pushed both remotes, DO NOT MERGE / NOT-MERGED (hourly sweep owns merges; Astro merges Part1 → Part3 → Part2 → this)
- **Founder order (Nov 17 17:45):** ALL reports formatted well with visual-first graphics, assembled from the report NODES, generalizable, never breaking. Supersedes the earlier web-report-template chunk AND Part 4 (report-summary-cards) — built as ONE piece.
- **ONE assembler, no per-report code:** every report renders through `GraphTreeView(surface: report)` → `NodeVisualiserSection` → the palette, driven purely by each node's `visual`. A new report kind of known node shapes renders with ZERO new code.
- **Delivered:** (1) report **section cards** (`NodeVisualiserSection.cardStyle`, `GraphTreeView` passes it on the report surface); (2) **photo-carousel aggregation** of consecutive report image leaves → one swipeable `NodePhotoCarousel` (`widgets/node_photo_carousel.dart`), reused from the appearance carousel; (3) **appearance migrated** to `GraphTreeView(reportType:'appearance')` and the bespoke `appearance_templated_report.dart` (+ its test) DELETED; (4) **pre-open SUMMARY cards** from the nodes (`models/report_summary.dart` `ReportSummary.fromTree` → `widgets/report_summary_card.dart`), wired into the chat inline card for appearance/deep_dive/match; (5) **no raw JSON pre-open** — a streaming Ayma turn now strips the `[appearance:<id>]` marker before finalize; (6) `GraphTree` now carries `title`+`meta`.

NO sim-result report exists (Date Sim is a live transcript, not a report_type) — when one is added it renders free through this framework.
- **Never-breaking + privacy:** malformed/partial nodes degrade to gist_row/omit (never crash/overflow/JSON/empty); report tree is viewer-filtered by `report_tree.py` so shared/public visuals+scores+counts use only viewer-visible nodes.
- **Tests (green):** `test/unified_report_framework_test.dart` (assembly + malformed/partial + privacy), `report_summary_test.dart`, `report_summary_card_test.dart`, golden `test/golden/unified_report_golden_test.dart`; layout matrix (3 shards) + node/graph/report suites green; `flutter analyze` = 0.
