## VISUAL-FIRST PALETTE RENDERERS — less-text-heavy program, Part 2 (fleet/visual-node-renderers off staging @228df581, contains Part 1 + Part 3, 2026-11-17) — DONE on branch, pushed, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges; Astro merges Part 1 → Part 3 → this)
- **Founder order:** the RENDER side of "the visual lives on the node". The UI is a THIN renderer — one renderer per palette entry, driven ENTIRELY by the node's `visual` field (`{type, params, v}`). NO per-kind/per-report presentation logic beyond the single palette dispatch. HARD: a new node of a known shape renders visually with ZERO new code.
- **Server (surface + read-time default):** `person_graph_tree._build_node` (+ child bullets) and `report_tree` now surface each node's `visual` beside `gist`. Stored visual wins (unknown/newer type passes through); a node with none (every existing node — NO backfill) gets `node_visual.default_for(section, answer_type)` enriched from its OWN fields (`_default_visual`): career→`timeline_entry` (gist + `Link.dated`), list-like section→one-item `chip_set`, image→`photo_carousel`, else `gist_row`. `report_tree` maps numeric STAT→`bar_meter`.
- **Flutter (one dispatch, `widgets/node_visualiser.dart`):** `StoryNode.visual` (`NodeVisual`, `NodeVisualType` mirrors `node_visual.py`). `_content` switches on `node.visualType`: `gist_row`→Part-1 row, `chip_set`→chips(`items`), `timeline_entry`→dated rail(`date`+`title`), `bar_meter`/`gauge`→`ScoreBar`(`value,min,max,label`), `card`→finding card(`title`+`body`), `photo_carousel`→shared `NodeImage`, `small_table`→table(`columns,rows`). Unknown/insufficient params → `gist_row` (never raw JSON/crash/overflow). Legacy kinds (stat/link/candidate) live in the gist-row fallback — nothing regressed. `_siblingWidgets` groups consecutive leaf chip/timeline siblings into one visual, shared by `NodeVisualiser` (children) + `NodeVisualiserSection` (section roots) — reusable by the report framework (`fleet/visual-report-framework` branches off this).
- **P0 privacy/controls (non-negotiable):** aggregates built ONLY from the already visibility-filtered node list (`_hasVisibleContentFor` before grouping) — a shared/public surface never aggregates private/Ayma-only into a visual/score/COUNT; no visual leaks a node onto a forbidden surface. Every chip/timeline/bar/card/table-row tap opens the SAME partial node chat + owner controls (Make public/private, Edit, Chat to edit, Delete) via `_openNodeChat`. Can't honor → `gist_row`. Delete drops a node from every aggregate in the same pass (aggregates are a pure function of the filtered list).
- **Tests:** `tests/test_person_graph_tree.py` (+5: every node has a visual; career→timeline, interest→chip, stored-wins, plain→gist_row), `tests/test_report_tree.py` green. `ayma_flutter/test/node_visual_palette_test.dart` (per-type render; unknown/insufficient→gist_row; per-visual tap-to-controls; privacy — private/Ayma-only never on public nor in aggregate; delete drops from aggregate; palette mix overflow-free through the full layout matrix). `flutter analyze` 0; layout matrix 84/84. Screenshot: `ayma_flutter/test/goldens/palette_mix.png` (regen by `test/palette_screenshot_test.dart`). Docs: `PERSON_GRAPH.md § Visual-first palette RENDERERS`. No new route → no registry/tour change. Inference untouched (pure render/derive; 100% LiveKit).

## GIST-FIRST RENDERING — less-text-heavy program, Part 1 (fleet/gist-first-rendering off staging 4a58979e, 2026-11-17) — DONE on branch, pushed, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Founder order:** the app reads text-heavy. Every node renders as a SHORT GIST LINE by default on EVERY surface (Your Story, public profile, reports, the graph inspector, match views); the full prose shows ONLY on tap-in. Generalizable — category/kind-level, NO per-node custom code.
- **THE GIST CONTRACT (shared with Part 3 `fleet/gist-first-writing` + Part 2 `fleet/visual-node-renderers`):**
  - **Field name is exactly `gist`** — a short punchy one-line headline (aim ≤ ~80 chars). Part 3's writer PRODUCES `gist` going forward; Part 1 RENDERS it and derives a fallback for existing nodes.
  - **Fallback derivation (generalizable, one helper, applied to every node at tree-build time):** `functions/bootstrap/node_gist.py` → `derive_gist(text, stored=None)`. Rule: stored `gist` wins if present; else the first sentence of the text when it fits `GIST_MAX` (80); else that text trimmed to a word boundary ≤ 80 with trailing punctuation stripped and an ellipsis appended. Whitespace/newlines collapsed; returns `""` only when there's neither a stored gist nor any text (pure image node).
  - **Full prose is never dropped** — the node's `text` stays; tap-in reveals it.
- **Server:** `person_graph_tree._build_node` (node + child-bullet dicts) and `report_tree._sections_to_tree` add `gist` to every node dict (stored if present, else derived). A report block may carry its own `gist` (passed through `_block_to_nodes`).
- **Flutter:** `StoryNode.gist` + `StoryNode.displayGist` (gist → text → label fallback — the universal fallback text Part 2's `visual` field layers on top of). `node_visualiser.dart` renders `displayGist` capped to 2 lines + ellipsis by default (standard labelled rows too); tap target UNCHANGED (opens the partial node chat with full prose + owner controls). `appearance_templated_report.dart` findings render gist-first via a tappable `_FindingRow`. GraphTreeView inherits it (delegates to NodeVisualiserSection). Image nodes stay photo-forward (never gist-ified).
- **P0 (privacy/controls, non-negotiable):** visibility filter untouched — public profile shows public only, private/ayma_only never leak; the collapsed gist row stays fully tappable/controllable (Make public/private, Edit, Add a line, Delete inside the chat). Tests pin both.
- **Tests:** `functions/bootstrap/tests/test_node_gist.py` (helper rules + tree surfacing: derived fallback, stored-wins, full-prose kept — 11 cases), `ayma_flutter/test/node_gist_render_test.dart` (gist-by-default on Story + report fixtures, tap reveals full prose, P0 controls open on tap, P0 private node absent on public profile, no-gist falls back). `flutter analyze` 0; layout matrix green; public-profile rail fixture bumped to 40 sections (gist rows are shorter). Screenshot evidence in the commit. No new route → no registry/tour change. Inference untouched (N/A — pure render/derive, no model call).
- **For Part 2/3:** keep writing `gist`; `displayGist` is the fallback render Part 2's kind-keyed `visual` renderers wrap.

- **Tests:** `test/person_graph_outlier_fit_test.dart` — founder-shaped fixture (12 weighted sections of cross-surface facts + 5 edgeless orphans + the degree-1 assessment under a lone hub): settled max distance ≤ 2.2× median AND every orphan ≤ 1.2× median (fail-before: orphans ~580 over median ~325) AND the degree-1 assessment ≤ 1.2× median with a Skills-filtered fit framing it on-screen AND a far-flung weakly-connected pair contained ≤ 2.5× median (fail-before ~14×), and fit puts every node on-screen with the cluster filling ≥60% width centred (not a corner). `test/person_graph_fit_screenshot_test.dart` — pumps the REAL `PersonGraphScreen` on founder-shaped data (reduced motion), toggles the Skills filter ON, captures the fitted frame to `pg_layout_fitted.png` (assessment inside the cluster, no streak). `flutter analyze` 0; layout matrix green (29). Docs: `PERSON_GRAPH.md` → "outlier + Fit crush fix" + degree-1/global-containment extension.

## SERVER-DEDUPE-SWEEP — DATA-level dedupe: merge duplicate nodes on the server (fleet/server-dedupe-sweep off fleet/gist-first-writing, 2026-11-17) — DONE on branch, pushed, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Founder order (Nov 17 17:51):** a live screenshot showed TWO "Social Circle" nodes carrying the SAME fact in different words (one private, one Ayma-only). The render-level zero-dup fix only HIDES dupes on screen; this REMOVES them from the DATA. All server-side (the client does zero dedupe). Behind `GRAPH_DEDUPE_SWEEP` (default off; additive/reversible).
- **Layer 1 — write-time semantic fold** (`person_graph_store._dedupe_collapse_id`→`_semantic_fold_id`→`fold_into_node`, extends the existing `NODE_DEDUPE_PREVENTION` hook — no parallel path). With `GRAPH_DEDUPE_SWEEP`+`GRAPH_EMBEDDING_DEDUPE` on, a new fact near-identical (Gemini-embedding cosine ≥ 0.92) to an existing emergent node in the same section is FOLDED in: enrich the surviving text if it adds detail, KEEP the existing id, KEEP BOTH sources. Embeddings = Gemini Embedding 2 **direct** (approved embeddings-only exception); judge stays on LiveKit.
- **Layer 2 — post-processing sweep** (`person_graph_dedupe_sweep.sweep_user`): runs at **session end** (`post_turn.persist_and_ingest`, after ingest, best-effort) + a **nightly backstop** (`POST /cron/graph-dedupe`→`run_sweep`). Per section, finds near-dup pairs (embedding-NN or lexical, reusing `crosscheck.candidate_report`), the **LiveKit judge** (`classify_pairs`) labels every non-exact candidate, then MERGES clear duplicates: **fuller text survives**, **STANDARD node survives** (build-2490 rule), survivor keeps **BOTH sources**, **merged visibility = the MORE PRIVATE of the two** (a merge NEVER widens visibility — HARD rule, `apply_merge` only tightens). Seed-vs-seed pairs skipped.
- **Uncertain pairs NEVER silent:** a pair that's near but not judge-confirmed, merely related, or disagreeing is RECORDED (`store.record_pending_dedupe`) and raised in chat via `pg_find_duplicates` ("NEEDS YOUR CALL"); cleared on `pg_resolve_duplicate`. Reuses the settled conflicts-in-chat flow — no new popup.
- **Proof runner** (staging-pinned, dry-run default, `--apply`): `tools/dedupe_sweep_run.py <uid> --project=ayma-staging`. ABORTS unless project == `ayma-staging`.
- **Backend-only** (no Flutter → layout matrix N/A). No new navigable route (cron endpoint + CLI only → no `app_registry.json` change). Inference: embeddings direct-Google (approved), judge 100% LiveKit.
- **Tests:** `tests/test_graph_dedupe_sweep.py` (paraphrase detect+merge, fuller survives, both sources kept, more-private-wins, standard survives, uncertain raises-not-merges, parametrized **privacy proof** over every visibility pair, Layer-1 write-time fold). Touched suites green (`test_node_dedupe`, `test_merge_duplicate_fact_nodes`, `test_graph_embeddings`, `test_person_graph*`, `test_post_turn`, `test_feature_flags`). Docs: `PERSON_GRAPH.md` (new section), `FEATURE_FLAGS.md` (`graph_embedding_dedupe` + `graph_dedupe_sweep` rows).
- **Founder-graph proof:** needs ayma-staging Firestore + Gemini Embedding 2 direct creds (not reachable from this worktree) — coordinator to run `tools/dedupe_sweep_run.py iWhWjiWhEpU7iyu9B9vy7GlT0PD2 --project=ayma-staging` (dry-run then `--apply`).

- **Tests:** `test/assistant_orb_test.dart` (orb reflects the 3 states, expand-on-tap, dismiss-returns, honest-error), `AymaAssistantSessionSummonTest.kt` (summon builds no MainActivity intent / no overlay perm / hosts the orb entrypoint). `flutter analyze` 0; layout matrix green; `test_app_registry.py` 57/57.

## ZERO-DUPLICATES — standard-vs-emergent fact dedupe (fleet/zero-duplicates off staging aeacbddb, 2026-11-17) — DONE on branch, pushed, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Founder TOP PRIORITY (build 2488):** About Me showed facts TWICE — Ethnic origin ×2, Age ×2; Gender/Location/Interested-in restated as plain bullets under the labelled rows. Cause: the same fact lived in a STANDARD node AND an emergent node restating it (`emergent.editor.identity.11` = "Ethnic and cultural origin: South Asian", `.4` = "Age: 33", prose `emergent.story` bullets). The 2485 lexical dedupe missed standard-vs-editor pairs (a "`<Label>: <value>`" bullet isn't an exact-text match of the bare standard value); the render never suppressed a bullet that restated a standard fact in the same section.
- **Layer 1 — render guarantee (always on):** `person_graph_tree.node_tree`/`_build_node` suppress, surface-aware, any non-standard node whose text restates a rendering standard fact (bare value, or `<label> <value>` content tokens), plus exact-duplicate bullets anywhere (first wins). The standard labelled row is the keeper. Surface-aware so suppression never drops the only copy of a fact nor crosses the public/private boundary. Suppressing a node blanks its own line only; distinct children still render.
- **Layer 2 — write-time merge-not-mint:** closed the editor-import gap. `replace_section_bullets` drops any bullet that only restates a standard fact (a matched legacy restatement is left unconsumed → deleted on save); `add_loose_node`/`add_node` reuse the standard node's id (`_standard_restatement_id`, always on, before the flag-gated exact `_dedupe_collapse_id`). One shared matcher `person_graph_render.standard_restatement_id` so write-time and render never disagree.
- **Layer 3 — data merge (built + tested, NOT executed):** `functions/bootstrap/tools/merge_duplicate_fact_nodes.py` — for a uid finds every emergent restatement of a standard fact and MERGES it into the standard node (`store.merge_nodes`: standard survives, dup answer → survivor history, children rehomed, links carried, dup deleted). Visibility conflict keeps the STANDARD node's visibility, recorded in the report. `--dry-run` (default) only reports. ABORTS unless project is exactly `ayma-staging`. Main thread runs it (dry-run first) for the founder's uid only; other staging users governed by the in-chat raise flow (layers 1+2 protect their renders). NOT run against real Firestore by this agent.
- **Backend-only** (no Flutter touched → layout matrix N/A). No new route → no `app_registry.json` change (the tool is a CLI script, not a navigable screen). Inference untouched (the dedupe semantic judge stays on LiveKit).
- **Tests:** `tests/test_person_graph_tree.py` (render suppression + surface-aware privacy — the founder acceptance case: About Me shows each standard fact once, fixed order, no section repeats a fact), `tests/test_node_dedupe.py` (editor merge-not-mint, loose-node reuse), `tests/test_merge_duplicate_fact_nodes.py` (dry-run report, apply merge, child preservation, keep-standard visibility, staging-project guard). Docs: `PERSON_GRAPH.md` → "Zero-duplicates", `ARCHITECTURE.md` (remember/dedupe line).
- **Leg 28 FOLLOW-UP (NOTE 252, build 2490 Scout FAIL, Daniel fixture — fleet/zero-duplicates-leg28-fix off staging, 2026-11-17) — DONE on branch, DO NOT MERGE (sweep owns merges):** facts still rendered TWICE in five Your-Story sections on an UNMERGED graph (layer-3 data merge only ran on the founder's uid). Root cause: layers 1–2 collapse a restatement only against a *standard* row; when a section has TWO distinct non-standard emergent restatements of one fact and their `normalize_text` DIFFERS by a stopword/punctuation/word-order ("Ethnic & cultural origin: South Asian" vs "ethnic and cultural origin south asian" — differ by the `and` stopword), the exact-norm `seen_texts` key misses them and both render. **Fix (render layer 3, server tree builder so every surface benefits):** `person_graph_tree._build_node`/`node_tree` add a SECTION-SCOPED content-token-set dedup (`index_mod.token_set`, stopwords dropped, order-insensitive) at the same precision bar as the standard matcher's token-set form — a non-standard node/child-bullet whose content-token set already rendered in that section is suppressed; genuinely distinct facts (different token set) and cross-section repeats are untouched. **No stored data merged** (render-only; other users' graphs stay as-is). Backend-only → layout matrix N/A; no new route. Regression test: `tests/test_person_graph_tree.py::test_unmerged_graph_collapses_two_distinct_restatement_nodes_in_a_section` (fails before, passes after).

## LEVEL-INDENTATION — Node-Visualiser indents by actual level (fleet/level-indentation off staging aeacbddb, 2026-11-17) — DONE on branch, pushed, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Founder order (build 2488):** his Story bullets all sat FLUSH at one margin; the settled Nov-17 design is that children indent by level in the Node Visualiser. Fix so a child renders one indent step deeper than its parent, by its ACTUAL level (level 2 under level 1, level 3 under level 2), everywhere the Node Visualiser renders (Story, reports, public profile, below-graph).
- **Diagnosis (renderer-ignores-level, NOT payload):** the backend tree (`person_graph_tree.node_tree`) nests children AND carries each node's absolute `level`; `models/story_node.dart` parses `children` + `level` recursively. But `widgets/node_visualiser.dart` indented by **recursion depth** (`EdgeInsets.only(left: 18)` per recursion), ignoring `node.level`. The Your Story editor + live content writers mint `person_graph_store.upsert_fact_node` facts at `level: 2` with **no parent** → flat section roots (recursion depth 0) → every one rendered flush with the level-1 standard block. `node_visualiser.dart:125-138` was the bug.
- **What changed (`widgets/node_visualiser.dart`):** each row now indents by `_indentSteps * 18dp` where `_indentSteps = max(node.level − 1, depth)` clamped to a 5-step cap, applied as `EdgeInsetsDirectional.start` (LTR left / RTL right). Removed the recursion-depth wrapper `Padding` (so nested children don't double-indent — their `level` already grows). Standard rows (level 1) stay flush; reports (all level 1) unaffected; cap + `Expanded` text wrap keep deep nesting on-screen. Doc comments on `node_visualiser.dart` + `story_node.dart` `level` updated.
- **Not touched:** no backend change (payload was already correct), no new route (no `app_registry.json`/tour entry needed), no inference path (100% LiveKit, N/A).
- **Regression tests (`test/node_visualiser_test.dart`):** "parentless roots indent by their actual level" (a level-1/2/3 section-root trio renders at three strictly-increasing insets — the founder path) + "deep nesting never overflows at 320px / text-scale 2.0" (8 levels past the cap, long wrapping text, LTR + RTL, no overflow/exception).
- **Tests:** `node_visualiser_test.dart` 14/14, `graph_tree_view_test.dart` green, layout matrix 84/84 (all shards), `functions/bootstrap/tests/test_person_graph_tree.py` 13/13; `flutter analyze` 0 issues.

## PERSON-GRAPH-SECTIONS — live graph layout + highlight fix (fleet/person-graph-sections off staging aeacbddb, 2026-11-17) — DONE on branch, pushed, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Founder TOP-PRIORITY build gate (build 2488 "My person graph"), two parts on one branch:**
- **Part A — Sections layer broke the layout:** with the organic-mesh design, the `/profile/graph` force sim collapsed to a collision-packed blob (sections on → nodes flung off-screen as pink streaks; sections off → two tight blobs). Root cause (reproduced on ~230-node founder-shaped data, golden capture): the `GraphSim._seed` golden-angle **spiral** scattered each section's facts across the disk, and since spring/repulsion are alpha-scaled to 0 at settle while the no-overlap collision pass is not, the settled frame was dominated by uniform collision packing. Fix: **radial-sunburst seed** (sections in weight-sized angular sectors with a floor; facts seeded inside their section's sector) + re-tuned forces (`_repel` 42→220/cap 30/cutoff 900, `_gravity` 0.015→0.0035, longer springs, `_alphaDecay` 0.035→0.0225) so equilibrium spacing beats the collision minimum → organic hub-and-spoke mesh that `fitToViewport` keeps on-screen.
- **Part B — tap highlight:** the highlight set + painter dim/lit logic were already correct; "nothing dims" was Part A (the blob hid it). Painter now routes nodes through `graphNodeLit` and edges through `graphEdgeLit` (pure, testable). Spread mesh → a tap visibly lights the connected set (self+ancestors+descendants+related) and dims the rest.
- **Tests:** `person_graph_layout_test.dart` (bounded-box settle + fit on-screen at ~230 nodes; radial seed lobes facts by section), `person_graph_screen_test.dart` (`graphNodeLit`/`graphEdgeLit` render predicates). Goldens: `test/goldens/person_graph_{all_chips,tapped}.png`. Full required set (person-graph + layout_matrix + app_registry) green, `flutter analyze` 0. No backend/inference change (100% LiveKit).

- **Founder bug (build 2488):** the Web research report overflowed — "BOTTOM OVERFLOWED BY 466 PIXELS" across the Facts section; the report didn't fit or scroll. Founder: "never let anything like this slip."
- **Root cause:** `deep_dive_report_screen.dart` pinned the shared non-scrolling `GraphTreeView` (a `mainAxisSize.min` `Column`) inside a bare `Expanded` with NO scroll view — the ONLY report host that did (appearance/match/shared/public-profile/Your-Story all wrap it in a `ListView`/`SingleChildScrollView`). A long Facts/web-findings tree overflowed the fixed box. **Fix:** wrapped the body in a `SingleChildScrollView`.

Before this the `…/tree` fakes returned block-shaped JSON with no `nodes`, so `GraphTreeView` rendered EMPTY and the matrix never pumped scroll-length report content — which is exactly why the 466px overflow slipped. Every report/visualiser screen is already pumped by `layout_matrix_test.dart`; now with real content. Verified it catches the bug: reverting the `SingleChildScrollView` fails the matrix with "A RenderFlex overflowed by N pixels on the bottom".

## 2o-P5-SHARED-REPORT-NODE-VISUALISER — Node-Visualiser adoption, shared-report surface (fleet/shared-report-node-visualiser off staging edaddb6f, 2026-11-17) — DONE on branch, pushed, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)

- **Access-scope pivot (critical):** a share link is a FROZEN, public, UNAUTHENTICATED snapshot (`share.fetch_public`); it must NOT go through the owner-checked `report_node_tree`/`GET /report/{type}/{id}/tree`, or a token holder who isn't the owner would be denied the body. New `report_tree.snapshot_node_tree(snapshot)` converts the already-authorized snapshot `{title, sections}` straight to a node tree (shared `_sections_to_tree`/`_block_to_nodes`, no `report_hub` re-fetch), `meta={owner_uid:"", viewer_can_edit:False}` — identical to `/s/{token}/data`. Served at `GET /s/{token}/tree` (same public `_public_snapshot` path, `/s/` public rate limit). Client `ApiClient.sharedReportTree(token)` → `GraphTree`, no auth header.
- **Flutter:** `GraphTreeView` got a `loader` callback (token path can't use `reportType`/`reportId`, which hit the owner-checked endpoint); `SharedReportScreen` now renders `GraphTreeView(loader: …, isOwner:false, surface:report)` inside a `SingleChildScrollView`. `isOwner:false` suppresses every node chat/edit/correct affordance (read-only). Old `LivingReportView` body deleted; now-orphaned `ApiClient.sharedReport`/`/data` client method deleted (backend `/s/{token}/data` endpoint kept — stable public JSON API, still tested).
- **Remaining `LivingReportView` hosts after P5 (for P6):** `story_editor_screen.dart:769` (Your Story report body) and `match_report_screen.dart:356` (match body — P3 not yet in this staging base). `public_profile_screen.dart` already on `GraphTreeView` in this base. `living_report_view.dart` NOT deleted (P6 deletes once its last host migrates).
- **Tests:** backend `tests/test_report_tree.py` (snapshot cases) + `tests/test_share.py::test_tree_is_public_and_unknown_token_404s`; Flutter `test/share_report_test.dart` (renders through the tree unauthed; a revoked/unknown token shows NO body — access scope preserved). Layout matrix + app_registry (`/s/:token` already `non_navigable`) green. `flutter analyze` 0. Inference untouched (no LiveKit path changed).
## ACCENT-REALTIME-PREVIEW — live accent preview + "Use this color" save + revert-on-leave (fleet/accent-realtime-preview off staging edaddb6f, 2026-11-17) — DONE on branch, pushed, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Founder order (Nov 17):** in Settings → Appearance → Accent Color, the app theme must change in REAL TIME as the user picks/drags; a "Use this color" button does ONE thing only — save/persist the picked color; leaving the picker WITHOUT pressing "Use this color" reverts the app to the previously-saved accent and stores nothing.
- **What changed (`ayma_flutter/lib/screens/accent_color_screen.dart`):** added a prominent **"Use this color"** FilledButton (key `useThisColor`) in the preview row — the sole persist path (`_accent.set(_selected)`), shows a success toast, advances the revert baseline, and pops. Replaced the dead empty-onPressed "Sample button" with it. Made EVERY change preview-only: dropped the `_commit()` calls on hue `onPanEnd`/`onPanCancel` and the slider `onChangeEnd`s; neutral swatches (`_neutral`) now preview without auto-commit; "Reset to default" now previews the default (`_resetPreview`) instead of persisting via `resetToDefault()`. Removed the now-dead `_commit`/`_reset` methods. Revert-on-leave: capture `_savedColor = _accent.color` in `initState` (before any preview), and in `dispose()` call `_accent.cancelPreview(_savedColor)` — the single catch-all covering back arrow, Android back, swipe and nav-away.
- **Controller (`ayma_flutter/lib/services/accent_color_controller.dart`):** added `cancelPreview(Color saved)` — a readability wrapper over `preview` that reverts the live colour to `saved` with no SharedPreferences/Firestore write (no-op when already equal, e.g. after a commit). `resetToDefault()` (persisting) kept — still used by the chat/voice "reset accent" command path.
- **Tests:** `test/accent_color_screen_test.dart` rewritten to the new contract + 4 founder-path regression tests — (a) dragging previews live without persisting, (b) "Use this color" persists (local + server write asserted), (c) leaving without pressing reverts to saved + zero writes, (d) commit-then-leave keeps the committed colour. Plus reset/neutral now assert preview-only. `test/accent_color_controller_test.dart` adds a `cancelPreview` unit test. Screen 10/10 + controller 11/11 passed; layout matrix + app_registry green; `flutter analyze` 0 issues. Inference routing: N/A (no inference), nothing rerouted off LiveKit.

## NOTE-237-STORY-SCROLL-ANR — Story-scroll ANR regression on 2463 (fleet/story-scroll-anr-237 off staging 4d5de298, 2026-11-16) — DONE on branch, pushed, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Defect (Scout NOTE 237, build 2463):** ANR dialog during slow story scrolling (9 half-screen swipes at 3s pauses, first attempt after photo sends; `Waited 5001ms for MotionEvent, 12:54:40.083`), plus a second ANR trace stamped during re-run scroll. Regression vs NOTE 236 on build 2462 (zero ANR on 26 fast swipes).
- **Root Cause:**
  1. Commit `2279599f` rendered inline story images via bare `Image.network(b.imageUrl, width: double.infinity, fit: BoxFit.cover, errorBuilder: ...)`.
  2. Batch 2463 (`afb161ee`) introduced `_node_order_key` in `person_graph_render.py` so image nodes homed under facts render immediately after their fact's bullet in Your Story (Milo tabby photo homed under Daniel's cat fact bullet in Little Extras).
  3. When scrolling through Little Extras, bare `Image.network` bypassed `flutter_cache_manager` disk caching (where chat had already saved the downloaded photo), opened a cold connection, and synchronously decoded the unconstrained full-resolution original image (e.g. 12MP = ~48MB bitmap) on the UI thread without `memCacheWidth` capping.
  4. During slow scrolling (9 swipes at 3s pauses), the main UI thread was blocked for >5001ms by image decode and texture upload, triggering Android ANR `Waited 5001ms for MotionEvent`.
- **Fix:**
  1. Updated `ayma_flutter/lib/widgets/ayma_photo.dart` with optional `width` and `height` properties, forwarding them to `CachedNetworkImage`, `_placeholder`, and `_fallback`.
  2. In `ayma_flutter/lib/widgets/bullet_sections_editor.dart`, replaced bare `Image.network` with `AymaPhoto(url: b.imageUrl, width: double.infinity, height: 180, displayWidth: 400, fit: BoxFit.cover, error: const SizedBox.shrink())`, capping decode size via `memCacheWidth` and reusing disk cache from chat. Bare `Image.network` deleted.
- **Tests:**
  1. Added 9-swipe slow-scroll reproduction test in `ayma_flutter/test/story_scroll_test.dart` (repro fails on pre-fix bare `Image.network`, passes with `AymaPhoto` capped decode).
  2. Updated `ayma_flutter/test/bullet_sections_editor_test.dart` to verify `AymaPhoto` rendering for inline image bullets.
  3. Touched Flutter suites: `story_scroll_test.dart` (2/2 passed), `bullet_sections_editor_test.dart` (13/13 passed), `ayma_photo_test.dart` (5/5 passed).
  4. Layout matrix suite `ayma_flutter/test/layout_matrix_test.dart` (29/29 passed).
  5. `flutter analyze` 100% clean (0 issues).
  6. Inference routing: 100% LiveKit, zero direct provider routing.

## NOTE-236-ACCENT-HYDRATION — Accent hydration lag on fresh install (fleet/accent-hydration-lag off staging e4995d79, 2026-11-16) — DONE on branch, pushed, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Defect (Scout NOTE 236, build 2462):** After a fresh install + sign-in, the app rendered the DEFAULT pink accent (pink orb, pink Upgrade button, pink sign-in orb) even though `profile/main.ayma_accent_color` was still `#2d6a4f` (green) in Firestore. Only after a force-stop + cold relaunch did the saved green accent apply.
- **Root Cause:**
  1. On a fresh install, local storage (`SharedPreferences`) has no `accent_color_hex` key.
  2. StartupDAG `N19:remote_sync` only runs `syncRemoteIfMissing()` if `user != null && !user.isAnonymous`. On fresh launch, the user is unauthenticated, so `syncRemoteIfMissing()` was skipped.
  3. Upon sign-in on `SignInScreen` (email, Google, Apple, or phone), the screen immediately navigated to `SplashScreen.path` without hydrating or caching the user's saved accent color from their Firestore profile.
  4. Neither `SignInScreen` nor `SplashScreen` hydrated the accent color, leaving `AccentColorController` holding the default Cherry Fuchsia pink for the entire initial session until a cold relaunch triggered `N19:remote_sync`.
- **Fix:**
  1. Added `syncRemote({ProfileService? profiles, bool force = false})` to `AccentColorController`, allowing explicit forced sync on sign-in (updating `_color` and caching in `SharedPreferences`, resetting to default if remote is null).
  2. Exposed `AuthService.profileService` to access the bound profile service.
  3. In `SignInScreen`, added `_hydrateAccent()` right after authentication in `_run` and phone `autoSignedIn`, ensuring `syncRemote(force: true)` completes and `MaterialApp` rebuilds before navigating to splash / main shell.
  4. In `SplashScreen`, added `syncRemoteIfMissing()` in parallel with `bootstrap()` as defense-in-depth before navigating to destination routes.
  5. In `main.dart`, hooked `syncRemote(force: isSwitch)` to `authStateChanges`.
- **Tests:**
  1. Added repro & regression suite `ayma_flutter/test/accent_hydration_lag_test.dart` (3 tests verifying saved green accent `#2d6a4f` applies on first render after sign-in with no relaunch, default accent on new user, and phone auto-sign-in path).
  2. Added unit tests in `ayma_flutter/test/accent_color_controller_test.dart` (11/11 tests passed).
  3. Added sign-in hydration test in `ayma_flutter/test/sign_in_screen_test.dart` (49/49 tests passed).
  4. Layout matrix suite `ayma_flutter/test/layout_matrix_test.dart` (29/29 tests passed, 100% green).
  5. `flutter analyze` 100% clean (0 issues).
  6. Inference routing: 100% LiveKit, zero direct provider routing.


## NOTE-230-PHOTO-RENDER — Fix photo-reply live-render defect (fleet/note-230-photo-render off staging tip c208f451, 2026-11-16) — DONE on branch, pushed, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Defect (Scout NOTE 230):** Sending a photo in Ayma chat did not render live in the thread; view stayed stale for 3.5+ min and appeared only after a tab-switch + full-chat reopen, even though the reply was saved server-side in ~10s.
- **Root Cause:**
  1. `ApiClient.BudgetHttpClient` throws `ApiException('Request timed out — please try again.', statusCode: 408)` on client timeout.
  2. `_ChatScreenState._shouldReconcile` only checked `error.statusCode == null && error.message.startsWith('Network error')`. Because `statusCode == 408` is non-null and the message starts with `'Request timed out'`, `_shouldReconcile` returned `false`. `_store.dropPending()` was called immediately and toasted `Couldn't reach Ayma. Check your connection.`, dropping the pending turn from view rather than entering reconciliation.
  3. `_reconcilePendingSend` had no foreground periodic polling loop — after the initial 3 polls over ~4s and the fallback `rec.resend()` timeout, it left the pending turn stagnant without checking history again until app resume or tab switch.
  4. Backend `_classify_shared_photo` had no timeout guard around `face_match.classify_photo`, allowing slow/stalled face classification to block `_prepare_chat` for up to 75s.
- **Fix:**
  1. Updated `_shouldReconcile` in `chat_screen.dart` to recognize `error.statusCode == 408` and `error.message.startsWith('Request timed out')` as reconcilable connection-class timeouts.
  2. Implemented foreground periodic history polling via `_reconcileTimer` in `chat_screen.dart` (polling `chatHistory` every 3s up to 20 attempts while remaining on the chat screen) to adopt the landed reply as soon as Cloud Run commits it to Firestore, resolving `_store.resolvePending` live without any tab-switch or resume required. Disposed `_reconcileTimer` on unmount.
  3. Increased `ApiClient.chatStreamConnectTimeout` from 30s to 45s for cold-start multimodal stream requests.
  4. Added a 8.0s timeout guard to `_classify_shared_photo` in `functions/bootstrap/main.py` so slow/hanging face classification never blocks `_prepare_chat`.
- **Tests:**
  1. TDD regression suite `ayma_flutter/test/chat_photo_render_reconcile_test.dart` (2 tests reproducing both defect facets, failed before fix, now passing).
  2. Existing reconciliation suites `chat_connection_drop_reconcile_test.dart` (4 tests) and `chat_backgrounded_send_test.dart` (2 tests) green.
  3. Layout matrix test `ayma_flutter/test/layout_matrix_test.dart` 100% green (29 tests).
  4. `flutter analyze` 100% clean (0 issues).
  5. Python backend tests `test_app_registry.py` and `test_face_match.py` (66 tests passed).
  6. Zero inference rerouting: all inference flows through LiveKit.


## NOTE-224-OBSERVATIONS — Fix four Scout NOTE 224 defects (fleet/note-224-observations off staging tip 248aa5ab, 2026-11-15) — DONE on branch, pushed, DO NOT MERGE (hourly sweep owns merges)
- **Defect 1 (You-tab Plan row tap didn't navigate x4):** Root caused and fixed. Batch 2449 added "My person graph" to You tab, pushing the 1-item "Ayma" (Plan) group down. Because `_Group` had a single interactive child without `explicitChildNodes: true`, Flutter accessibility coalesced the `Eyebrow('Ayma')` header and `ListTile` into one merged semantics node with bounds `[0,1977][1080,2105]`. The top half (`y < 2049`) was dead padding, so center tap at `(540, 2041)` missed the ListTile. Fixed by marking `Eyebrow` with `Semantics(header: true)`, setting `Semantics(explicitChildNodes: true)` on `_Group`, and `Semantics(container: true)` on `_Item`.
- **Defect 2 (Plan screen transient store error):** Added retry loops in `RevenueCatService.plans()` and `proTrialOffer()` (up to 2 retries, 500ms delay) on transient misses (`PurchasesErrorCode.storeProblemError`, `offlineConnectionError`, `networkError`, or `offering == null` during asynchronous Play Billing connection), while preserving immediate failure for permanent configuration errors (`invalidCredentialsError`, `configurationError`). Hardened `PlanScreen._plans()` to wait and retry before showing unavailable state on transient errors.
- **Defect 3 (Career section "Add your LinkedIn" button shown when URL set):** In `career_timeline.py::get_linkedin_url`, added fallbacks to `user_doc` and `questionnaire_supplemental` (where volunteered LinkedIn links like `demo-test1`'s are captured), backfilling to `profile_doc`.
