## PHOTO-ADD-FIXES — Fix photo adding: face-embedding non-fatal graceful degradation + moderation reason toast surfacing (fleet/photo-add-fixes off staging tip ac75fa1e, 2026-11-08) — DONE
- **Bug 1 — Face-embedding step unconditionally raised in photo pipeline (BACKEND DEFECT — FIXED):**
  - **Root cause:** In `functions/bootstrap/media_embed.py:25-33`, `embed_image_bytes` unconditionally raised `DirectGoogleDisabledError` because embeddings call direct Vertex, which is disabled under the founder LiveKit-only inference rule. For any user with biometric face-matching consent (`biometric_consent.has_consent_fresh`), `photo_pipeline._embed_and_attrs` executed `embed_image_bytes` and failed before writing any `user_media` record, causing `POST /media` to return 502 for photos that had already passed moderation.
  - **Fix:** In `functions/bootstrap/photo_pipeline.py`, wrapped `_embed` and `_attrs` in `_embed_and_attrs` so face-embedding and attribute-extraction failures are logged clearly and non-fatal. If embedding fails or is unavailable, the pipeline continues, approves and publishes the photo, and marks `embedding_missing: True` on the `user_media` doc so it can be backfilled later. Handled `routes/media.py` so optional pipeline failures never cause 502.
  - **Tests:** `test_embedding_failure_is_non_fatal_and_records_missing`, `test_attribute_extraction_failure_is_non_fatal`, `test_moderation_flag_returns_reason` in `test_photo_pipeline.py`; `test_media_route_publishes_when_embedding_fails` and `test_media_reports_a_flagged_photo_without_publishing` in `test_media.py` (41 passed).
- **Bug 2 — Discarded server reason on photo addition rejection (CLIENT CONTRACT DEFECT — FIXED):**
  - **Root cause:** In `ayma_flutter/lib/widgets/inline_photo_carousel.dart:130`, `InlinePhotoController.pickAndAdd` discarded `batch.rejected` reasons and returned a fixed generic message `"That photo can't be used."` shown as a "Heads up" toast, making moderation blocks and server errors look identical.
  - **Fix:** Updated `pickAndAdd` to parse and humanize the real server moderation reason (e.g. `'That photo cannot be used - screenshots of text are not allowed.'`) while suppressing internal codes (e.g. `moderation_error`, `upload failed`), falling back to a clean generic message only when no reason exists or on true transport failure.
  - **Tests:** `test/inline_photo_carousel_test.dart` (15/15 passed) covering single and multiple rejections with human reasons, casing/punctuation normalization, internal code suppression, upload failure fallback, and full widget toast display on `MyPublicProfileScreen`. `flutter analyze` clean (0 issues).


## NOTE-155-OBSERVATIONS — Triage + fix for Scout NOTE 155 observations (fleet/n155-observations off staging 2a80d8e2, 2026-11-08) — DONE, lead engineer reviews + merges + builds
- **Observation 1 — "Signing you in…" sticks after cancelled Google flow (REAL DEFECT — FIXED):**
  - **Root cause:** Dismissing the native Google Sign-In picker (Credential Manager / Android bottom sheet) via system Back in some scenarios causes `CredentialManager` / `google_sign_in_android` to drop the completion callback. Dart's `await action()` in `_run()` remained indefinitely suspended and never reached `finally { _busy = false; }`. Additionally, `AbsorbPointer(absorbing: _busy)` wrapped the entire Scaffold body without any `PopScope` or Cancel button to exit the modal state, wedging the user in "Signing you in…" until app relaunch.
  - **Fix (client-only):**
    1. Added `WidgetsBindingObserver` to `_SignInScreenState`. On `AppLifecycleState.resumed`, if `_busy` is true, an auto-cancel timer (`_resumeTimer`, 1500ms) clears `_busy` and resets state if no auth callback resolves (mitigating dropped callbacks on native sheet dismiss).
    2. Added `_runEpoch` tracking to invalidate stale in-flight async callbacks when cancelled.
    3. Handled `GoogleSignInExceptionCode.interrupted` as a cancellation alongside `canceled` (clears busy state, no error toast).
    4. Handled `PlatformException` case-insensitively for `'sign_in_canceled'`, `'canceled'`, `'12501'`, and codes containing `'cancel'`.
    5. Added `PopScope` to `SignInScreen` that intercepts back button when `_busy == true` and invokes `_cancelBusy()` to release the screen rather than wedging or quitting the app.
    6. Added an explicit "Cancel" `TextButton` under `AymaWingLoader` when `_busy == true`.
    7. Scoped `AbsorbPointer(absorbing: _busy)` specifically around the consent checkboxes and provider stage buttons/forms so the "Cancel" button remains interactive.
    8. Preserved `SplashScreen.path` navigation on successful sign-in.
  - **Tests:** `test/sign_in_screen_test.dart` has 7 new widget tests covering: cancel via `canceled` code + Email tap, cancel via `interrupted` code + Email tap, cancel via `PlatformException` + Email tap, in-flight cancel via "Cancel" button + Email tap, in-flight cancel via `PopScope` + Email tap, in-flight cancel via resume timeout + Email tap, and active voice session permitting text field focus and entry (42/42 passed).
- **Observation 2 — Sign-in screen LiveKit greeting voice session blocks form while live (BENCH-ONLY / BY-DESIGN — NO CODE DEFECT):**
  - **Verdict:** By-design prompt behavior + emulator bench starvation; not a Flutter layout/gesture defect on real devices.
  - **Evidence:**
    1. No overlay widget intercepts touches on `SignInScreen`. The widget tree is a single `Column` inside a `SingleChildScrollView`.
    2. `VoiceSessionController(welcome: true)` explicitly sets `_userIdleTimeout = null` by design (lines 62-67) so user has time to read and sign in.
    3. The ~3.5 min session duration was due to the deaf emulator bench emitting no user speech; Ayma followed the 2dn prompt (`functions/bootstrap/main.py _WELCOME_GREETING` and `welcome_persona.md`) to introduce herself once and wait in silence. The session remained connected until LiveKit server-side room deletion at 07:19:49 (`RoomDisconnected: DisconnectReason.roomDeleted`).
    4. The inability of `uiautomator2` to focus/type was caused by emulator CPU starvation during concurrent WebRTC Opus audio playback and Flutter rendering, confirmed by Scout's 2x App ANRs, 3x System UI ANRs, and `n155_typed2.xml` dumping only the system status bar (`[11,10][115,53]`).
    5. Widget test `active voice session on sign-in screen does not block focusing or typing in email/password fields` passes cleanly; Flutter text fields remain 100% focusable and interactive during live voice playback.

## NOTE-156-TOUR-TRIGGER-TRIAGE — Voice messages tour popup trigger triage (fleet/tour-trigger-triage off staging 2a80d8e2, 2026-11-08) — DONE
- **Triage Finding: BY-DESIGN SHOW-ONCE BEHAVIOR — NO DEFECT.**
- **Root Cause & Forensic Analysis:**
  1. **Two different tour mechanisms:**
     - The **passive coach-mark tour** (`lib/services/tour_content.dart`, `coach_mark_overlay.dart`, `feature_tour_store.dart`) stores seen feature IDs on the user's Firestore profile doc (`users/{uid}/profile/main.seen_tour_features`).
     - The **backend-driven guided tour** (`skills/guided_tour.py`, `app_commands/tour_state`) stores transient step state in `users/{uid}/app_commands/tour_state`. Scout inspected `tour_state`, which is only for the live voice/chat guided tour ("walk me through the app"), not the passive coach-mark card.
  2. **NOTE 151 vs NOTE 156 lifecycle:**
     - In build 2299, `async_voice` ("Voice messages") was added to `tourFeatures` in `tour_content.dart`.
     - In NOTE 151, Daniel (`demo-test1`) had not seen `async_voice`. `decideTour` saw an existing user with an unseen feature and returned `spotlight: true, features: [async_voice]`. The card appeared with "NEW", "Got it", and "Skip tour".
     - In NOTE 151, Scout dismissed the popup via system Back. `TourController.skipFromBack()` executed `store.markSeen(['async_voice'])`, persisting `'async_voice'` to `users/demo-test1/profile/main.seen_tour_features` in Cloud Firestore (verified: 18 items including `async_voice`).
     - In NOTE 156, Daniel re-signed in. `decideTour` read `seen_tour_features`, found 0 unseen features (`unseen.isEmpty`), and returned `show: false`.
     - Clearing app data (`pm clear`) removes only local client storage; since `seen_tour_features` lives in Cloud Firestore, re-signing in restores the seen array immediately. This is intentional show-once behavior so users are not repeatedly nagged.
- **Re-trigger tool for QA/Scout (`scripts/reset_tour.py`):**
  - Re-trigger single feature spotlight (`async_voice`): `GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT=ayma-staging python3 scripts/reset_tour.py --uid demo-test1 --unsee async_voice` (already executed; `async_voice` is unseeded on staging for `demo-test1`).
  - Re-trigger full first-run walkthrough: `GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT=ayma-staging python3 scripts/reset_tour.py --uid demo-test1 --reset-all`
  - Inspect seen features: `GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT=ayma-staging python3 scripts/reset_tour.py --uid demo-test1 --inspect`
- **Tests:**
  - `ayma_flutter/test/feature_tour_gate_test.dart`: 7/7 passed (+2 new tests verifying returning user seen status & unseeing `async_voice`).
  - `ayma_flutter/test/feature_tour_seen_state_test.dart`: 7/7 passed (+1 new test verifying arrayRemove unsee).
  - `flutter analyze ayma_flutter`: clean (0 issues, ran in 28.7s).

## SIGNIN-GOOGLE-AND-VOICE — Google sign-in cache fix + Voice introduce-once-and-wait (fleet/signin-page-fixes off staging tip 6463d598, 2026-11-08) — DONE, lead engineer reviews + merges + builds
- **Part 1 — Google sign-in root cause:**
  - In `scripts/build-apk.sh`, cache busting previously purged only `build/app/generated/res/google-services` and `build/app/intermediates/merged_res`. In AGP 8.11.1, `merged_res` does not exist; resource merging is cached in `build/app/intermediates/incremental/debug/mergeDebugResources/`, `packaged_res/debug/`, etc. An incremental build in the watcher's worktree (`ayma-autodeploy`) reused cached prod resources in `resources.arsc` (`project_id: ayma-ai`, sender `235381544962`).
  - At 06:14 PST on build 6463d598, the staging self-check `apk_verifies_staging` caught this and marked `BUILD_RES=FAILED`, skipping the immediate install. However, `staging-autodeploy.sh` still advanced `last_staging="6463d598"`.
  - At 06:17 PST, the watcher's catch-up loop saw `NEW == LAST_STAGING` and `LAST_PHONE != LAST_STAGING`. It read `last_apk` (which pointed to the overwritten `app-debug.apk`) and installed it to the founder's Pixel *without running `apk_verifies_staging`*.
  - The installed APK was signed with `ayma-staging-debug.keystore` (SHA-1 `0daa766d4f17e3bb07662ee1330d3031262ff3c2`, registered on `ayma-staging`), but its native `resources.arsc` pointed to `ayma-ai`. Google Play services rejected auth because that staging SHA-1 is not registered in prod `ayma-ai`.
  - Fixes applied:
    1. `scripts/build-apk.sh`: purge entire `build/app/intermediates/` alongside `build/app/generated/res/google-services` on staging builds so AGP 8 always re-merges resources.
    2. `ops/deploy-watcher/staging-autodeploy.sh`: updated `apk_verifies_staging()` to verify `resources.arsc` directly contains `187469876115` and does NOT contain `235381544962`; enforce `apk_verifies_staging` in catch-up install path; delete `$STAGING_APK` and `$STATE/last_apk` if self-check fails.
    3. `ayma_flutter/lib/screens/sign_in_screen.dart`: enhance error handling on `signInWithGoogle` to surface `GoogleSignInException.description` and non-canceled `PlatformException`s so failures never result in a silent dead tap.
- **Part 2 — Sign-in voice introduce once, then wait:**
  - Client side (`VoiceSessionController(welcome: true)`) already sets `_userIdleTimeout = null` (no client reprompt timer).
  - The repeated prompting came from backend prompt instructions: `_WELCOME_GREETING` in `main.py` and `welcome_persona.md` instructing the model to steer back to sign-in and keep under a minute, which Gemini Live interpreted as an active loop to reprompt into silence.
  - Fixes applied:
    1. `functions/bootstrap/main.py`: updated `_WELCOME_GREETING` instructing Ayma to introduce herself once, then stay completely silent and wait for the user to reply without reprompting or nagging to log in.
    2. `functions/bootstrap/welcome_persona.md`: added strict turn-taking rule to introduce once on arrival, wait in complete silence, do not constantly ask to log in or reprompt into silence or on a timer; continue normally once the user speaks; preserved all legal/consent capabilities (Terms, Privacy, Biometrics, 18+, public web face matching, ad data).
- **Tests:**
  - `ayma_flutter`: `flutter test test/sign_in_screen_test.dart` (35 passed, including new Google error surfacing tests), `flutter test test/sign_in_voice_test.dart` (6 passed), `flutter test test/layout_matrix_test.dart` (28 passed).
  - `flutter analyze`: clean (0 issues, ran in 48.1s).
  - `deploy-watcher`: `bash ops/deploy-watcher/self-check.test.sh` (11 passed, including new hybrid APK rejection test).
  - `backend`: `pytest functions/bootstrap/tests/test_prompt.py` (56 passed), `pytest functions/bootstrap/tests/test_bootstrap.py -k welcome` (5 passed).

## INFER-CHATBUDGET — Ask-Ayma "Sorry — I couldn't finish that just now." on most starts (fix/inference-chat-max-tokens off staging, 2026-11-03) — DONE, merged to staging
- **Confirmed root cause (log-backed, verified in code):** chat on LiveKit Inference uses a *thinking* model (`google/gemini-3.7-flash`) whose `completion_tokens` also counts reasoning tokens (`inference_llm._usage`, lines 146-149). The chat call hardcoded `max_tokens=1024` (`gemini.py:253-257`); against Ask-Ayma's ~17-18k-token prompt the model spent the whole 1024-token budget on reasoning and emitted empty `content`, so `gemini.py:325-327` raised `RuntimeError("Gemini returned an empty response.")` → user-facing `_CHAT_INCOMPLETE_REPLY` (`main.py:1964`). NOT a transport error — it correctly stayed on LiveKit (bypassed `_InferenceUnavailable`). Pre-existing since c6749c14; reproduced on STAGING (chat → inference for demo-test1); prod currently routes chat to vertex so prod did not hit it.
- **Fix (backend, stays 100% on LiveKit, no provider reroute, no model change):** (1) new config knob `inference_chat_max_tokens` (`INFERENCE_CHAT_MAX_TOKENS`, default **8192**) in `config.py` — the single source; `gemini.py` reads it and **throws** if unset (no inline literal). (2) `inference_llm._parse` now captures the OpenAI `finish_reason` (was discarded) onto `InferenceResponse`; it is logged with the prompt/completion token counts on the empty-response path so a `length` truncation is distinguishable from an empty `stop`. (3) bounded single retry: if a chat response is empty AND `finish_reason == "length"`, `gemini.generate_reply` retries **once** at 2× the budget before raising as before — one retry, no loop; a non-length empty still raises immediately (honest error, no reroute).
- **Tests:** `test_config.py` — knob default 8192 + env override; `test_inference_routing.py` — chat passes the configured budget, throws when the budget is 0/unset, `_parse` captures `finish_reason`, empty+`length` retries once at 2× and succeeds, empty+`length` still-empty raises after one retry, empty+`stop` does not retry, non-empty passes straight through. Full inference/config/chat/gemini suites green (333+66 passed; `test_tokencap_finalize` skipped — pre-existing local `livekit.agents` import gap, unrelated).
- **Files:** `functions/bootstrap/config.py`, `gemini.py`, `inference_llm.py` (+ tests). No `main.py`, no client, no routing/provider change.
- **Deploy:** `ayma-api-staging` (code-only, staging env preserved, min-instances stays 0). **Prod `ayma-api` needs the same redeploy to carry the fix once chat is routed to inference on prod — NOT deployed here; founder sequences it with the routing decision.**
- **Docs:** ARCHITECTURE "Model routing" (Inference chat completion budget), FEATURE_FLAGS.md (`INFERENCE_CHAT_MAX_TOKENS`), LIVEKIT_INFERENCE_MIGRATION.md env table.

## VOICE-AUTOSCROLL — chat didn't follow the bottom while a voice reply streamed (fix/voice-mode-chat-autoscroll off staging, 2026-11-03) — DONE, merged to staging
- **Founder bug:** on a live voice call with the chat visible, Ayma's reply streams in as text but the list does NOT follow to the bottom — the newest text lands off-screen and the founder has to scroll manually. Text mode auto-scrolls fine.
- **Verify-before-fix (file:line):** the two candidate suspects were wrong — voice turns DO flow through the same `_store` as text (`chat_screen.dart:396` `_store.addListener(_scrollToBottom)`), and `TranscriptStore.applyVoiceSegment` DOES `notifyListeners()` on every segment (`transcript_store.dart:379`), so `_scrollToBottom` fires on each one. The real cause is cadence: `_scrollToBottom` (`chat_screen.dart:1566`) issues a 200ms `animateTo`; a typed reply lands in ONE `resolvePending` store write so the single animation completes, but a live voice reply streams many `applyVoiceSegment` updates a few tens of ms apart (§1.7 step 12; `chat_screen.dart:238-239` — voice finalizes turn-by-turn), each restarting the animation a frame in so it never lands → the view crawls and falls thousands of px behind. A widget repro measured ~6238px of mid-stream lag below the bottom.
- **Fix (client-only):** in `_scrollToBottom`, when the tail turn is still a streaming (non-final voice) turn, `jumpTo` the bottom instead of `animateTo` so the view keeps up with the stream; a finalized turn or a typed reply still animates smoothly (text path untouched). The jump is gated on the same at-/near-bottom test `_handleListMetrics` uses (`pixels >= _bottomBaseline - 24`, with the `infinity` pre-first-scroll bootstrap treated as at-bottom), so a user who scrolled up to read history mid-call is never yanked down.
- **Tests:** new `chat_voice_autoscroll_test.dart` — (1) streaming follows the bottom DURING the stream (worst mid-stream gap < 200px; fails pre-fix at thousands of px); (2) a user scrolled up mid-call is NOT yanked by streaming segments; (3) a typed text-mode reply still lands at the bottom. Existing `chat_scroll_to_bottom_test.dart` / pagination / popup-expand / voice-session suites stay green; all 3 layout-matrix shards + `flutter analyze` clean. No new route/feature ⇒ no app_registry, tour, or diagram change.
- **APK:** client-only ⇒ versionCode **2280**; new `--debug --staging` APK (founder builds/installs).

## VOICE-MIC-LIFECYCLE — no mic/notification during warm-up; guaranteed release on close/crash (fix/voice-mic-lifecycle off staging, 2026-11-03) — DONE, merge to staging
- **Founder privacy bug (voice):** (1) during the WARM-UP phase (e.g. "Warming up the open-weights model…", before the voice is actually live) the app already showed the persistent "Ayma — Voice call" notification AND held the mic (green status-bar indicator); (2) the mic + notification were not reliably released when the app closed/backgrounded or the session crashed/errored — the mic stayed held (privacy).
- **Verify-before-fix (file:line):** mic was enabled in `voice_session_controller.dart` `_attempt()` (old line 524) right after `connect()`, BEFORE `_setPhase(waking)` → captured through connect/waking AND the whole open-weights warm-up. The agent (`functions/bootstrap/agent.py` `_start_main_cascade_session`, ~2854-2865) joins + publishes its track (client latches `agentReady` → `live`) FIRST, then sends `open_weights_warming` — so `live` precedes warming and gating on `isListening` alone (old `call_background_service.dart:153/175/187`) lit the notification through warm-up. `_teardown()` released the mic (`transport.disconnect()`) only LAST, after several awaits that could throw and skip it; `_fail()` didn't itself tear down; no app-lifecycle hook released on detach/pause.
- **Fix (client-only):** new `isConversationReady` (`isListening && _agentRespondedOnce` — Ayma's first genuine response). Mic now published only via `_syncMicCapture()` when `isConversationReady && !muted` (never at connect/waking/warming); the OS call notification / CallKit gate on `isConversationReady` too (with a synchronous `_uiStarting` latch against a double-`start`). Guaranteed teardown: `_teardown()` wrapped so `disconnect()` (mic release) runs in `finally`; `_fail()` now always tears down (closes the `out_of_credits` path); a whole-session `AppLifecycleListener` ends the call on `onDetach` and, on `onPause`, ends any not-yet-conversation-ready session (a genuinely live call survives backgrounding = the intended FGS/CallKit background call).
- **Tests:** `voice_session_controller_test.dart` (no mic during connecting/waking; open-weights warm-up never captures the mic, first reply does; crash/drop releases mic + clears ready; mic-denied now surfaces at first response; toggle-mute only when ready; out-of-credits tears down immediately), `call_background_service_test.dart` (notification gated on conversation-ready incl. the warm-up case; no double-start), new `voice_mic_lifecycle_test.dart` (app detached → mic released + notification dismissed; paused-while-warming → torn down; paused-while-live → kept alive). `flutter analyze` clean (touched files); full voice suite green; layout matrix unaffected (no new route). versionCode 2281→2282.
- **Native (Android):** NO change — `CallForegroundService.kt`/`MainActivity.kt` untouched; the gating + teardown are all in Dart (the FGS already starts only when Dart asks, now at conversation-ready).
- **Deploy:** client-only → new **--debug --staging APK** (versionCode 2282). No backend redeploy. Branch `fix/voice-mic-lifecycle`, merge to staging (rebase).

## AUTH-WRONGPW — wrong password must NOT flip sign-in to sign-up (muse/auth-wrongpw off staging, 2026-11-02) — DONE, Astro merges
- **Founder bug (live):** wrong password on an EXISTING account (test1@gmail.com) flipped the form to create-account (confirm field, "Create account" button) — read as "this login doesn't exist". Founder: stay on sign-in and say the password is wrong; only an unknown email may offer sign-up. Reverses the first half of AUTH-UX's auto-switch.
- **Verify-before-fix:** the flip fired on `user-not-found` OR `invalid-credential` (`_onSignInError`). Enumeration-protection answer: YES, it is on for this project — staging Firebase returns byte-identical `INVALID_LOGIN_CREDENTIALS` for test1@gmail.com+wrong-password and a guaranteed-nonexistent address, and `createAuthUri` (fetchSignInMethods) returns no `signinMethods` for either. The client genuinely cannot distinguish; a server oracle is the honest route (NOT faked client-side).
- **Fix:** new unauthenticated `POST /auth/email-exists` → `{"exists": bool}` (Admin SDK `get_user_by_email`; exists-only response, 400 on malformed, 503 on transient failure, 20/minute per-IP, consent-exempt like the other pre-sign-in helpers). Client: explicit `user-not-found` flips directly and explicit `wrong-password` stays (no oracle call); ambiguous `invalid-credential` asks the oracle — exists → stay + "That password is wrong — try again.", not-exists → existing create-mode offer; oracle failure → stay + generic error, never flips on a guess. Sign-up `email-already-in-use` bounce-back kept; success path unchanged.
- **Tests:** backend `test_auth_email_exists.py` (5: true/false/400/503-no-guess/rate); client `sign_in_screen_test.dart` (both oracle branches + no-oracle direct codes + oracle-failure + bounce) and `api_client_test.dart` (emailExists true/false/throws, unauthenticated). Full backend suite 3014 passed + the 2 failures verified pre-existing on the untouched tree (app_registry categories, vendor_spend stale list); flutter auth suites + all 3 layout-matrix shards green; `flutter analyze` clean for touched files (4 pre-existing issues elsewhere). No new user surface — `app_registry.json` NOT affected (same screen, same buttons).
- **Deploy:** `ayma-api-staging` (new endpoint) AND a new APK (client branches on it). Branch `muse/auth-wrongpw`, NOT merged — Astro merges.

## GW-SCROLL: "scroll to the bottom" hung the turn (fix/gw-scroll-hang off staging, 2026-11-02) — DONE, merged to staging
- **Regression from the scroll-to-end fix (build 2248, commit 30a098a).** Asking Ayma "scroll to the bottom" from the page-context Ask-Ayma chat sheet (specimen A: a pinned profile; specimen B: Explore) hung: "Thinking…", sheet closed, page looked un-scrolled, no reply/pill/error, and `GET /chat/history` showed the user turn but no following ayma record (staging 2249 / backend 00104-k6x).
- **Verify-before-fix — the prompt's two suspects were BOTH wrong:** (1) the client command handler is NOT killed by the sheet closing — it is `AppCommandService.shared`, app-level, kept alive by `AppCommandHost`/voice; a widget test (`screen_capture_test.dart`, both specimen routes) proves `perform()` dismisses the sheet AND scrolls the revealed page AND acks "Reached the bottom." in one command. (2) the server does NOT await the ack with no timeout — `_await_ack` is bounded (`_ACK_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` = 6s), and the agent loop is a single tool round (no retry).
- **Real mechanism (new-op-specific):** the 2248 `scroll_to_bottom`/`scroll_to_top` op repeats on the client up to `kScrollToEndMaxSteps` = 50 steps (~0.45s settle each) → ~20–25s before it can ack, but commit 30a098a left the flat 6s `ui_action` ack wait untouched. So the ack always timed out → the tool returned a false `_NOT_APPLIED` at 6s while the client was still scrolling; the model's one-tool-round follow-up then produced no reply at all (empty-response path → no ayma record). Single-step `scroll_down`/`scroll_up` finish <1s and ack within 6s, so they were fine.
- **Fix (server-only; the real anti-hang):** added `_SCROLL_ACK_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` = 30s in `skills/app_control.py`; `_await_ack` takes an optional `timeout`; `skills/app_control_ui.py` passes it for the two scroll-to-end ops only (every other op keeps 6s). Still bounded → the honest real result ("Reached the bottom." / "Stopped after N scrolls…") comes back in the one turn, and if even 30s lapses the tool returns the honest `_NOT_APPLIED` — never hangs. **Did NOT fall back the op.** No client lib change (verified already correct) ⇒ no versionCode bump, no new APK.
- **Tests:** backend `test_app_control.py` — a scroll ack that lands past the 6s tap cutoff but within the scroll window COMPLETES with the real result (fails pre-fix); a scroll ack that never arrives still COMPLETES via the bounded wait with `_NOT_APPLIED` (no hang). Client `screen_capture_test.dart` — scroll_to_bottom scrolls the page revealed when the Ask-Ayma sheet is dismissed first, both specimen routes (`/explore`, `/profile/user/u1`). Backend suite green (pre-existing only); `flutter test` + all 3 layout-matrix shards + analyze clean. Docs: ARCHITECTURE §1.6b ui_action ack wait + this entry.
- **Deploy:** `ayma-api-staging` AND `ayma-agent-staging` (voice shares the same `ui_action`/`_await_ack`). No app build.
- **Variant B follow-up (Explore via chat sheet STILL failed, differently — reopened + fixed, 2026-11-02):** retest of df73422 — variant A (profile) passed (~29s, pair written), but Explore scroll-to-bottom EVAPORATED: no reply, and `GET /chat/history` had NO record at all, not even the user message.
  - **Confirmed mechanism (file:line):** the user message AND the ayma reply are both persisted only in `_finalize_chat` (`main.py:1696-1719`), and `/chat/stream` calls it only `if reply_text:` (`main.py:2114`), skipping it entirely when generation raises (`main.py:2110`). The paginated Explore scroll-to-end never settles, grinds the full 50-step cap (~0.8s/step for an image grid → >30s), overruns the 30s ack bound → false `_NOT_APPLIED` → the single-tool-round follow-up produced nothing → `RuntimeError("Gemini returned an empty response.")` (`gemini.py:327`) → finalize skipped → the whole turn (user + ayma) vanished. The server turn is already disconnection-proof (shielded `_spawn_turn`, `main.py:1933-1938`), so the client sheet-dismiss does NOT abort it — no client-abort bug.
  - **Fix — persistence (server):** `_persist_user_turn` (`main.py`) writes the user message at turn START (idempotent per `client_msg_id` via new `chat_history.user_turn_exists`), called at the top of both `/chat` and `/chat/stream` `_turn()`; `_finalize_chat` appends the user turn only if the start write didn't (never twice). Both endpoints now ALWAYS finalize with an honest reply (`_CHAT_INCOMPLETE_REPLY` on error, `_USAGE_CAPPED_REPLY` on TokenCapExceeded) — no more evaporation, no silent non-response. Finalizing capped turns guarantees no orphaned user turn is left in `chat_messages` or `raw_turns`, avoiding voice opener suppression (`agent.py:maybe_open_conversation`). Capped replies leave `client_msg_id` un-tagged so queued client retries once credits are refilled generate normally. `/chat` no longer 502s on a generation error (returns 200 + honest reply + persists). Dropped-connection recovery is unaffected (it rides the SUCCESS path, unchanged).
  - **Fix — Explore scroll bound (client):** `_scrollToEnd` (`screen_capture.dart`) now detects a paginated/growing list (`scrollExtentMax` jumping >200px as the end runs away) and stops honestly after `kScrollToEndMaxGrowths` (3) page-loads — well before the 50-step cap — so the op returns inside the ack window with "Stopped after N — more content may remain." A finite list's extent is stable, so it still runs to its real end ("Reached the bottom.") — **variant A stays passing.**
  - **Tests:** `test_chat.py` — an errored `/chat/stream` turn persists the user message + an honest ayma reply exactly once each (no evaporation); same for `/chat`; a normal turn persists exactly one user + one ayma (start-persist + finalize don't double). `screen_capture_test.dart` — a growing list stops EARLY (growth-capped, parsed step count < 50). Full backend + flutter + 3 layout shards + analyze green.
  - **APK:** client changed ⇒ versionCode **2254**; deploy `ayma-api-staging` + `ayma-agent-staging` AND a new `--debug` APK (2254).
- **Variant B op follow-up (Explore scroll returned the generic error, grid never moved — reopened + fixed, 2026-11-02, on 2255/be284ac):** persistence/hang/honest-finalize all confirmed working on retest, but Explore "scroll to the bottom" still returned the generic "Sorry — I couldn't finish that just now." (tools [], no pill) and the grid never moved; profile scroll worked.
  - **Confirmed mechanism (file:line, widget-test verified):** Scout's ~7-card Explore grid FITS on screen, so its `CustomScrollView` has zero scroll extent and exposes no `scrollUp`/`scrollDown` semantics action → `_typeOf` (`screen_capture.dart:290-293`) never tags it a `list`. A page-level scroll then resolved no target and `perform()` REFUSED "that control isn't on screen any more; read the screen again" (`screen_capture.dart:687`) → the model re-read + retried → the single-tool-round follow-up produced no reply → `RuntimeError` → the server's honest fallback. It is BOTH grid-node-finding (a non-scrollable grid isn't captured) AND short-list error-handling (refuse instead of a graceful result). Reproduced in isolation (bare GridView / CustomScrollView / grid-under-dismissing-sheet all scroll fine; only the zero-extent *fits-on-screen* grid refused).
  - **Fix (client):** `perform()` — when a scroll op finds nothing scrollable on the page, return a graceful honest result ("Reached the bottom — the whole page already fits on screen." / "Nothing to scroll — …") instead of a refusal, so the model relays a sensible answer and never retries into an error. A *named* control that is genuinely gone still refuses. A scrollable grid/list is unchanged. versionCode **2256**.
  - **Tests:** `screen_capture_test.dart` — a short Explore grid (Daniel/Sofia/Priya/…) that fits on screen → `scroll_to_bottom` returns ok + an "already fits" result, never a refusal/error; the scrollable-list, growing-list, and profile-via-sheet paths stay passing.
  - **APK:** client-only ⇒ versionCode **2256**; new `--debug` APK (2256). No backend change this round (no api/agent redeploy needed for this fix).

## Ayma registry: register agent-date/practice/avatar-chat + feature-completeness pass so Ayma never denies an existing feature (fix/ayma-registry-feature-completeness off staging, 2026-11-02) — DONE, merged to staging
- **Founder bug (voice chat, build 2246):** Ayma DENIED that core features exist because they weren't routable — "chat with Sofia's agent" → "I can't connect you to someone else's agent directly"; "talk with Sophia's avatar" → "I can't facilitate a conversation with another member's avatar"; "open an agent to agent simulation with Sophia?" → "I don't have a feature for agent-to-agent simulations." All three are core product (Practice Date + Agent Dates).
- **Verified root cause (refined):** the features were NOT absent — `/date-sim/:uid` and `/practice/:uid` sat in `app_registry.json` `non_navigable` ("opened only from a button on a profile, never on request"). So Ayma had **no tool** to open them and **no knowledge** they exist on request → she confabulated a policy refusal. Screens (`date_sim_screen.dart`, `practice_screen.dart`) and backend (`/date-sim`, `/practice/*`) already existed.
- **Fix (backend-only):** (1) moved `/date-sim/:uid` and `/practice/:uid` from `non_navigable` → `parameterized` (`via` = `open_date_sim` / `open_practice_date`); `/practice/:uid/debrief` stays non-navigable. (2) Added `open_date_sim` / `open_practice_date` in `skills/app_control_navigation.py` (mirroring `open_match_report`): resolve the named member via `_resolve_person` (fuzzy "Sophia"→"Sofia Ruiz" already covered), then queue a plain `navigate` command to the resolved path — **works on the already-shipped client** (the `navigate` case long predates this), **no new app build**. Wired into `COMMAND_SKILLS` (voice + text) and `tool_labels.py` (chip + working labels, nav-chip gated). (3) Persona completeness: `persona.md` "Driving the app" + `prompts/app_control_open_screen.md` name the two tools and mark them real features, never refuse (the "never say a page doesn't exist without trying the tool first" contract already existed).
- **connect_google / connect_instagram (reported, not changed):** both correctly sit in `unavailable` — Google-account linking is hidden in v1 (`/settings/google-account` non-navigable), Instagram is V2 behind the `meta_integrations` flag (default off; `/settings/instagram` redirects out when disabled). No N7/N8 sweep test references them in-repo. Intentional; left as-is.
- **Registration now a hard rule (coordinator addendum):** added the convention to `AGENTS.md` ("Ayma registration") and `docs/agents/CONTEXT_PACK.md`. Enforcement: `tests/test_app_registry.py` + `ayma_flutter/test/app_registry_test.dart` already fail an unregistered `GoRoute`; added `test_imperative_pushes_target_registered_routes_or_listed_subviews` to also enumerate imperative `MaterialPageRoute`/`push` screen targets (allowlist `_IMPERATIVE_SUBVIEWS` = ManualProfileEditScreen, PracticeVoiceScreen, VoiceCallScreen). Proved both enforcement tests bite on a synthetic unregistered route + synthetic imperative push, then reverted. Added resolution tests (every destination/parameterized route → a real router route + names a real tool).
- **Phrasing suite:** `test_app_control.py` — the three founder verbatim asks resolve to `open_practice_date`/`open_date_sim` with the target person and navigate (no existence-denial); not a canned `unavailable` denial; don't hijack to `connect_agent`; plus a spread of other feature phrasings. New-tool unit tests (resolve+navigate, fuzzy Sophia→Sofia, not-found, registered in command set).
- **Feature tour:** unchanged — the `date_sim` tour entry already exists and the screens/UI didn't change (only Ayma's routing did). **Diagram:** no change — two new `parameterized` destinations live inside the existing app_control → app_commands flow; no new component or edge.
- **Redeploy:** `ayma-api` (text chat tools) AND `ayma-agent` (voice reads the same `COMMAND_SKILLS`/`VOICE_SKILLS` + `persona.md`). No app build. Backend suite: green except the documented pre-existing failures (notification-categories, vendor_spend stale-list).

## Explore F1: blank photo tiles — graceful placeholder + photo-path fallback past a broken entry-0 (fix/explore-blank-tiles-f1-staging off staging, 2026-11-02) — DONE on branch, NOT merged (merge held for a window); manual adaptation of the pre-Phase-2 branch 8232cc8 onto staging
- **Gap (confirmed in staging code before the fix):** (frontend) `explore_screen.dart` `_Card.build()` invoked `_Photo(seed: card.uid, url: card.gridPhotoUrl)` with no name, and `_Photo`'s empty/failed-load fallback was a bare gradient `DecoratedBox` with no child → a blank dark tile whenever `gridPhotoUrl`/`photoUrl` was empty or failed to load. (backend) `explore.py` `_primary_photo_path` only inspected `min(entries, order)` and bailed with `None` on a broken entry-0 (empty `media_id` or a dangling `user_media` doc), ignoring later valid photos; the paginated feed's `_first_media_id`/`_first_photo_path` likewise evaluated only entry-0, so `_build_card` yielded an empty `photo_url`/`photo_url_thumb` when entry-0 was broken. (`main._primary_profile_photo_path` already iterated — used as the reference.)
- **Fix:** (frontend) pass `card.name` into `_Photo` (KEEPING staging's `gridPhotoUrl` variant-thumbnail fallback); the fallback tile now centres the name's initial (AymaType.sans 32/w600, white 0.8α) or a `person_outline` icon (white 0.6α) over the seeded gradient — the Messages-avatar look, never a blank square. (backend) `_primary_photo_path` sorts by `order` and walks entries, returning the first that resolves to a real `storage_path`; `_first_media_id` scans sorted entries and skips an empty-`media_id` entry-0 (fast batched path still recovers a later photo for the common case, no extra read); `_build_card` takes `db` and, only when the batched read resolved nothing for entry-0 yet photos exist, falls back to `_primary_photo_path` to recover a later valid photo (per-row read on the broken case only).
- **Tests:** `explore_screen_test.dart` — a card with empty `photo_url` renders `find.text('Ash')` + `find.text('A')` (placeholder initial), driven through `PagedFreshData`. `tests/test_explore.py` — `test_primary_photo_path_falls_back_when_first_has_no_storage_path` (m1 no storage_path, m2 valid → returns m2) and `test_explore_page_recovers_photo_past_a_dangling_first_entry` (drives `explore_page()`/`_build_card` with a dangling entry-0 + valid entry-1 → card `photo_url`/`photo_url_thumb` resolve to entry-1, proving the batched-path fallback). `flutter test` green incl. both layout-matrix shards (no new route → no new matrix entry); `flutter analyze` clean; backend suite green (known pre-existing failures aside).
- **Deploy when merged:** backend → **ayma-api-staging** (card photo resolution); Flutter rides the next staging APK (client placeholder). Docs: ARCHITECTURE §6.4 photo-resolution note, this entry. No feature-tour entry (bug fix). **Branch NOT merged — held for a window; lead merges.**

## J1: ChatComposer "RenderFlex was mutated" + duplicate GlobalKey on warm chat re-nav (fix/chatcomposer-renderflex-mutation off staging, 2026-11-02) — DONE, client-only, new --debug staging APK (versionCode 2243), NO backend deploy
- **Bug (device stack, --debug build 454c94c):** warm-tapping the self web-search notification navigated into chat via `appRouter.push('/')`, stacking a SECOND `MainShellScreen` over the live one. Both shells mount the same process-wide feature-tour anchor `GlobalKey` (`TourAnchors.keyFor(homeComposer)`, attached to the chat composer's root at `chat_composer.dart:292`) → "Duplicate GlobalKeys" + "A RenderFlex was mutated in _RenderLayoutBuilder.performLayout" (the `LayoutBuilder`→`Column` at `chat_screen.dart`) → tree corruption so even the hardened go(Home) recovery (454c94c) re-threw.
- **Root cause:** a deep link to the root shell (`/`, where `chat`/unrecognised `deep_link`s resolve, `notification_service.deepLinkToRoute`) was `push`ed, not `go`ne — stacking a duplicate shell duplicates EVERY shared anchor key (composer, orb, tabs); the composer just throws first. Reachable from five dispatch sites on staging: the warm push-tap (`push.listenForTaps`, `main.dart`), the two post-auth `DeepLinkQueue.drainPostAuth` drains (`main.dart` + `setupAuthDeepLinkDrain` in `app_router.dart`), the notification center (`notification_center_screen.dart`), and Ayma's registry `navigate` to `path:"/"` from a sub-route (`app_command_service.dart`, registry key `ayma`).
- **Fix:** one dispatcher `navigateToRoute` (`lib/routing/app_router.dart`) — `go` for `MainShellScreen.path` (reuse the single live shell), `push` for every id-bearing detail route (Back still returns); resolves the router from an optional `BuildContext` (so the notification center + its tests route through the navigator they live under), else the global `appRouter`. All five sites route through it. No composer/tour/deep-link-shape change; realigns code with ARCHITECTURE §11.5 (which already said `go`).
- **Tests:** `test/j1_root_shell_renav_test.dart` reproduces the exact warm re-nav (sub-route → notification re-nav to `/`) and asserts no duplicate-GlobalKey/RenderFlex throw + a single live shell; fails pre-fix (`push` stacks a 2nd shell → duplicate key), passes post-fix (`go` reuses). `flutter analyze` clean; layout matrix green (no new route → no new matrix entry).
- **Docs:** ARCHITECTURE §11.5, §11.2a tour-anchors note, §11 build-order step 4. Follow-up (not this fix): tapping the Ayma web-search notification from a non-Ayma tab now stays on that tab (`go` reuses the shell) instead of forcing tab 0 — if the founder wants it to always land on the chat tab, add tab-targeting to the root deep link.

## PERF follow-up — GET /match/{id} warm latency ~13–15s (deferred from SWEEP451-M1, 2026-11-01) — NOT STARTED
- **Context:** SWEEP451-M1 ("every match report fails to open — Couldn't load this report.") was fixed on the CLIENT (`fix/match-timeout-and-latency`, merged to staging): `BudgetHttpClient._timeoutFor` now routes `/match/` + `/report/match/` reads to the 55s long budget instead of the 8s interactive budget, so the report LOADS. But it still takes ~13–15s warm, which is bad UX.
- **Root cause of the latency (verified in code, do NOT re-diagnose blind):** `GET /match/{match_id}` (`functions/bootstrap/main.py:6089`) recomputes the taste/Qloo section LIVE on every read — `taste = await taste_match.report_section(...)` at `main.py:6146`. `report_section` (`taste_match.py:613`) → `_resolve_pair` → `_match_with` (`taste_match.py:376`) fires ~25 live Qloo HTTP calls per pair (search per entity ×2 users, insights ×3 per shared domain, places, tags ×2, demographics ×2), and `qloo_client.py` throttles them PROCESS-WIDE to 8 req/sec (`_RATE_PER_SECOND = 8`, `qloo_client.py:60-77`) with a 6s per-call timeout + up to two 1s 429 retries. So even gathered, ~25 calls pace into ~3s+ plus round-trips = the 13–15s. Also awaited on every open: `taste_backfill.backfill` ×2 (`main.py:6134-6140`).
- **Why NOT fixed as part of SWEEP451-M1 (per Scout's "defer if not a clean low-risk cache read"):** there is NO stored taste value anywhere to read instead — `report_section` does ZERO Firestore writes, the matcher `_match_job` (`main.py:5945`) persists the match doc WITHOUT a `taste` field (`main.py:5966-5983`), and `/taste/pair` also computes live + stores nothing. The only cache (`_demo_cache`, `taste_match.py:128`) is in-memory, process-local, and only for the 4 seeded demo profiles. So the assumed one-line read-swap has nothing to read.
- **Proposed fix (moderate feature, needs its own review — NOT a quick hack):** (1) compute the taste section inside `_match_job` and persist it into the match-doc payload (`main.py:5966`); (2) change the GET handler (`main.py:6146`) to read `d.get("taste")` and trigger the EXISTING background recompute (`main.py:6124`, `_match_doc_is_stale` at `:6119`) when absent/stale — but staleness logic must become taste-aware (taste depends on both users' public Taste pages, which `taste_backfill` can mutate at read time, and on the `qloo_enabled` kill switch), or it serves stale taste; (3) first open of every legacy/pre-existing match doc still has no `taste`, so a recompute fallback stays. Also migrate `/taste/pair` if it should share the cache. Cheaper interim levers (each changes behavior → own review): background the `report_section` call the way the stale recompute is already backgrounded (serve last-known / omit taste on cold, fill async), and/or raise `_RATE_PER_SECOND`/parallelism in `qloo_client.py`.
- **Deploy when built:** backend → ayma-api-staging.

## Sim report controls C/D/E — chooser continue + Stop + Start-over (feat/sim-report-controls off staging, 2026-11-01) — DONE, merged to staging
- **Founder ask (approved, exact behavior given):** three controls on the AI↔AI simulated-date report.
  - **C — "Keep the conversation going" asks HOW MANY more.** Tapping it opens a chooser sheet (`dateSimContinueChooser`) of +3/+5/+10 (with rough credit cost); the picked count flows through as `more_messages`. Backend `/date-sim/{id}/continue` already parameterized the amount — the fix was wiring the chooser (Flutter) instead of silently reusing the setup count.
  - **D — Stop mid-run.** New `POST /date-sim/{id}/stop` sets `cancel_requested`; `_generate_turns` checks it between turns (`_cancel_requested`) and halts, keeping the partial; `_finalize` scores the partial and writes `status:done, stopped:true` (screen: `dateSimStopButton` while running → `dateSimStoppedNote`). A continuation clears the flag first.
  - **E — Start over (reset).** New `DELETE /date-sim/pair/{other_uid}` → `reset_pair_sims` deletes every sim doc + `messages` for the pair; screen confirms (`dateSimResetDialog`) then returns to the setup form (`dateSimResetButton`).
- **Files:** backend `functions/bootstrap/date_sim.py` (stop/reset fns + endpoints, cancel-aware `_generate_turns`/`_finalize`); Flutter `date_sim_screen.dart` (controls + `showSimContinueChooser`/`showSimResetConfirm`), `date_sim_models.dart` (`stopped`), `services/api_client.dart` (`dateSimStop`/`dateSimReset`), `services/tour_content.dart` (`sim_controls` entry).
- **Tests:** `test_date_sim.py` (chosen-count, stop halts+partial, stop 404, continue-after-stop clears flag, reset clears pair, reset isolation) — backend suite green; `date_sim_test.dart` (C chooser, D stop, E reset + cancel) — 20 green; layout matrix gains the chooser sheet + reset dialog entries. Docs: ARCHITECTURE §18 (controls paragraph + endpoint table + flow diagram + fields), §11.2a tour list.
- **Deploys needed:** backend → **ayma-api-staging** redeploy; Flutter rides the next staging APK.

## Open-weights voice brain — selectable cascade option (feat/open-weights-voice-brain off staging, 2026-11-01) — DONE, merged to staging
- **Founder ask:** wire the Modal-hosted open-weights LLM (`https://suhailps93--ayma-open-weights-serve.us-east.modal.run/v1`, OpenAI-compatible, Llama-3.1-8B) as a SELECTABLE voice-brain cascade option, demo-able via the existing Brain (LLM) dropdown. HARD RULE: Gemini stays the production default everywhere; this is opt-in, never a default, never auto-selected.
- **Endpoint check (2026-11-01):** `curl /v1/models` returned **HTTP 000** (down / cold-starting) on two probes (120s, 90s). Per directive, wired the code anyway (don't block on a cold Modal); founder must confirm the exact served model id for `OPEN_WEIGHTS_MODEL` from a warm `/v1/models`.
- **Wiring (backend only, gated behind `OPEN_WEIGHTS_BASE_URL`):**
  - `config.py` — new `open_weights_model` ← env `OPEN_WEIGHTS_MODEL` (default empty; distinct from `meta_open_weights_model`, the Muse Spark id the TEXT provider uses — the voice endpoint serves Llama). Reuses the existing `open_weights_api_base_url` ← `OPEN_WEIGHTS_BASE_URL`.
  - `voice_catalog.py` — new `BRAIN_OPEN_WEIGHTS = "open-weights-llama-3.1-8b"` + `ROUTE_OPEN_WEIGHTS = "open-weights"`. `_brains` lists "Llama 3.1 8B (open weights)" ONLY when `OPEN_WEIGHTS_BASE_URL` is set; `brain_route` resolves it to `(open-weights, CONFIG.open_weights_model)`. Default selection unchanged (still Meta/Gemini).
  - `agent.py::_build_cascade_brain` — new `open-weights` route builds the LLM leg via `livekit-plugins-openai` (`lk_openai.LLM(base_url=CONFIG.open_weights_api_base_url, api_key=OPEN_WEIGHTS_API_KEY or "not-needed", model=…)`). Throws (→ safe Gemini Live fallback) if base URL or model unset.
  - **No Flutter change:** the Brain (LLM) dropdown (`VoiceEngineControl`) is catalog-driven and filters on `available`, so the option appears automatically. **No feature-tour entry:** a new option inside an already-toured control (`ayma_models`) is not a new anchored feature.
- **Tests:** `tests/test_voice_catalog.py` (listed only when configured; `brain_route` resolves it; never the default) + `tests/test_agent.py` (wires the endpoint/model/key; keyless sentinel; throws without model) — 6 new, green. Full backend suite: see commit.
- **Deploy needed (staging):** set `OPEN_WEIGHTS_BASE_URL` (+ `OPEN_WEIGHTS_MODEL`, + `OPEN_WEIGHTS_API_KEY` secret if the server needs auth) on **ayma-agent-staging** (cascade LLM leg) and **ayma-api-staging** (catalog listing), then redeploy both. Prod leaves the env unset → option hidden. Not an app-code change (no new APK).
- Docs: ARCHITECTURE §13.9, FEATURE_FLAGS.md, this entry.

## Open-weights voice brain — survive Modal cold start + honest status (fix/open-weights-coldstart-and-status off staging, 2026-11-02) — DONE, merged to staging
- **Founder-reported, diagnosed from ayma-agent-staging logs:** the open-weights brain was "listening but never replying." Root cause: the open-weights LLM runs on a scale-to-zero **Modal** endpoint (~100s cold start), but the LiveKit `AgentSession` LLM leg uses `DEFAULT_API_CONNECT_OPTIONS(timeout=10, max_retry=3)` — every turn `APITimeoutError`'d after 4 attempts and the UI sat on a silent "Listening…". Meta/Gemini (always-on gateway) were never affected.
- **Fix (surgical, open-weights leg only):**
  - `config.py` — new `open_weights_cold_start_timeout_s` ← env `OPEN_WEIGHTS_COLD_START_TIMEOUT_S` (default 120).
  - `agent.py::build_cascade_session` — for the `open-weights` route only, passes `conn_options.llm_conn_options = APIConnectOptions(timeout=120, max_retry=1)` (Meta/Inference legs untouched) and fires `_warm_open_weights_endpoint()` (fire-and-forget `GET {base}/models`) so the cold start begins at session build. **No persistent keep-warm** ($100 credit — warm-on-use only).
  - `agent.py` — new `voice_status` data topic + `VOICE_STATUS_OPEN_WEIGHTS_WARMING`; `_start_main_cascade_session` publishes `open_weights_warming` on session start when the open-weights brain is selected.
  - Client (`voice_session_controller.dart` / `voice_transport.dart`) — parses `voice_status`; shows **"Warming up the open-weights model — first reply can take a minute or two"** (`warmingNotice`, not a silent "Listening…"); re-arms the no-first-response guard to `_openWeightsResponseBudget` (150s); on final timeout shows **"The open-weights model is unavailable right now — try again or switch models."** instead of the generic voice-service error. Threaded into `chat_screen.dart` + `voice_call_screen.dart` status lines.
  - `agent.py` — INFO opener observability (coordinator ask): opener FIRED vs SKIPPED (+reason) and generate_reply COMPLETED vs ERRORED, tagged with brain id, in `maybe_open_conversation` / `_open_main_call`.
- **Uncaught-signal-10 note:** seen in the agent during the failing attempts; it is the open-weights timeout cascade churning the job process (10s timeout × 4 attempts × consecutive turns tripping `max_unrecoverable_errors`), not separate instability — the timeout + warm-up fix removes the churn. No dedicated process-crash fix needed.
- **Tests:** backend `tests/test_agent.py` (open-weights session cold-start timeout + warm-up fired; Meta keeps default conn options; `voice_status` warming publish) — 3 new, green (pre-existing `_read_room_seed`/`app_registry`/`vendor_spend` failures unchanged). Client `test/voice_session_controller_test.dart` (warming line + extended budget; open-weights-specific unavailable copy) — 2 new, green. `flutter analyze` clean; layout matrix green.
- **Deploy needed:** **ayma-agent-staging** redeploy (timeout + warm-up + status publish + opener logs; helps the CURRENT installed APK once deployed). New **--debug APK versionCode 2253** needed for the warming/unavailable status copy (client half). Prod unaffected (env unset → brain hidden).
- **Commit/branch:** `fix/open-weights-coldstart-and-status` → staging.

## THREE backend bugs — explore age-gate + voice-nav aliases + realtime accent (fix/explore-agegate-and-voice-nav off staging, 2026-11-01) — DONE, deployed to staging
- **Bug 1 — Explore "Everyone" returned empty ("No one matches that yet."):** the `/explore` route (`main.py`) called `explore.apply_saved_age_range` unconditionally, injecting the viewer's `qz_partner_age_min/max` into the base feed; `_passes_filters` then dropped every candidate with a missing/invalid `birthdate`. **Fix:** removed that call — the default "Everyone" feed never hard-filters by the saved partner age range (a dealbreaker is a 0% score, not a hidden person); only EXPLICIT dropdown/NL search ages (via `merge_filters`) still narrow it. Comment at `explore.py` ~608 corrected. Regression test in `tests/test_explore.py` drives the real `/explore` route (viewer WITH a saved range + no-birthdate candidates → feed non-empty; explicit age search still filters). → **ayma-api-staging**.
- **Bug 2 — voice agent couldn't open "model selection / Ayma's models":** `app_registry.json` `voice_preference` (route `/settings/voice`) IS the model/brain picker but its aliases omitted every model-picker word, so `find_destination("model selection")` → None. **Fix:** added aliases (model, models, model selection, brain, ayma's models, pick her brain, llm, model stack, …). SECONDARY same bug: widened `skills/app_control.py` `_ACK_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` 3.0 → 6.0 so a foregrounded-but-laggy app has time to ack. Regression test in `tests/test_app_registry.py`. → **ayma-agent-staging**.
- **Bug 3 — free-text voice accent ("indian") did nothing on Gemini Live native audio:** `agent._start_main_gemini_session` built the realtime model without `seed.voice_accent`; native audio ignores the prompt-text accent for pronunciation. **Fix:** derive `effective_language = seed.voice_language or voices.bcp47_hint(seed.voice_accent)` (bare "indian" → "en-IN", mapping kept in `voices.py`) and pass it as the model `language`; explicit `ayma_voice_language` still wins; prompt directive kept. Regression test in `tests/test_agent.py`. → **ayma-agent-staging**.
- **FOLLOW-UP (BUILT 2026-11-01, `muse/accent-hot-swap`, awaiting Astro merge):** mid-call accent change now hot-swaps the CURRENT Gemini Live call instead of persisting-only for the next one — `VoiceSwapper` tracks the accent (same effective-language derivation as boot + rebuilt accent block) and reconnects in-room; `bind_live` routes the `accent` param through it; a settings save mid-call arrives over the new `voice_accent_control` data topic. Text-chat and cascade/stack paths keep next-call behavior. Docs: `ARCHITECTURE.md` §13.9.
- Backend-only, staging-first. Docs: `ARCHITECTURE.md` §6.1 + §13.9, this entry. Full backend suite green except 2 PRE-EXISTING failures unrelated to this change (`test_vendor_spend` clock-based staleness; `test_app_registry::test_notification_settings_cover_every_category` `report_ready` category mismatch — both fail on untouched origin/staging too).

## STAGING Google Sign-In fix — dedicated staging keystore (fix/staging-google-keystore off staging, 2026-11-01) — DONE, lead engineer reviews + merges + rebuilds + installs
- **Root cause (pre-diagnosed):** ayma-staging had NO Android OAuth client because OAuth Android clients are globally unique per `(package, SHA-1)` and prod `ayma-ai` already globally owns `(com.ayma.ayma_flutter, 47:95:…:3F:5D)` — the shared default debug keystore SHA-1. `google-services.staging.json` had only a `client_type: 3` (web) entry, so on-device Google sign-in failed (`GoogleSignatureVerifier: package info is not set correctly` / `unknownError`).
- **Fix:** sign `--staging` builds with a DEDICATED staging debug keystore (`ayma_flutter/android/ayma-staging-debug.keystore`, alias `aymastaging`, pw `ayma-staging`; committed — dev/staging only, never a store release) whose SHA-1 is NEW, register it on ayma-staging, regenerate the config.
  - Staging SHA-1 `0D:AA:76:6D:4F:17:E3:BB:07:66:2E:E1:33:0D:30:31:26:2F:F3:C2`, SHA-256 `90:65:5F:15:65:C0:29:9C:01:8F:B1:1B:ED:D4:C2:31:DD:27:AA:11:FC:F9:E7:D8:9B:2F:54:ED:49:18:94:28`.
  - Both registered on App ID `1:187469876115:android:adb41422d11627ddcd3666`; Firebase then created the staging Android OAuth client (`…fosufjrncr5cingnvs2aa47l13pumd79…`), now present in `google-services.staging.json` as the `client_type: 1` entry (web `client_type: 3` retained).
  - `build.gradle.kts`: `stagingDebug` signing config wired to the `debug` build type ONLY when `-PaymaStagingSign` is passed (default/prod `--debug` unchanged — verified same default-debug cert). `build-apk.sh --staging` passes `-PaymaStagingSign` + keystore path/alias/pw.
- **Verified:** default `flutter build apk --debug` still signs with the default debug keystore (`16581138…10b5`); `--staging` APK signs with the staging keystore (`90655f15…189428`); native `google_app_id` still `1:187469876115` (staging).
- **⚠️ Install caveat (lead engineer):** the staging APK signature now differs from any previously-installed build, so `adb install -r` FAILS with a signature mismatch — must `adb uninstall com.ayma.ayma_flutter` first, then install (wipes local app data). See `docs/STAGING.md` "Google Sign-In: dedicated staging keystore".
- Docs: `docs/STAGING.md` (new subsection), this entry. **Branch off staging, pushed, NOT merged — lead engineer reviews + merges + rebuilds + installs (uninstall-first).**
## SIGN-IN VOICE legal/consent intents (feat/signin-voice-legal off staging, 2026-11-01) — DONE, lead engineer reviews + merges + rebuilds
- **Founder ask:** on the sign-in page's greeting voice, the user can (1) ask Ayma to "open terms and conditions / biometric information / privacy policy" → Ayma opens the in-app screen; (2) ask "is there anything critical I should be aware of?" → Ayma states the critical consent items (18+, Terms include use of data for advertising, face recognition incl. public-web matching, Privacy Policy governs data use), wording faithful to the on-screen consent copy.
- **Mechanism found:** the sign-in greeting is a backend LiveKit agent reached via `POST /welcome/token` — **no account, no uid**. The normal Ayma-driven navigation path (`AppCommandService`, watching `users/{uid}/app_commands/pending`) is therefore **dead pre-sign-in** (it early-returns with no uid), so the backend agent cannot open screens here. The app DOES receive the live transcript (`VoiceSessionController` → `TranscriptStore`), so both intents are handled **app-side off the user's finalized voice turns** — the smallest correct approach, no new backend navigation plumbing.
- **Fix (Flutter, surgical):**
  - `services/voice_session_controller.dart` — added a `transcript` getter exposing the session's `TranscriptStore` so a controller-only surface can observe turns (1 line).
  - `services/sign_in_voice_intent.dart` (new) — pure parser: `parseSignInVoiceIntent(utterance)` → open{Terms,Privacy,Biometric} (requires an "open/show/see…" cue + a document keyword; earliest-mentioned doc wins; face wording maps to biometric) or `critical` ("critical/aware/important/should I know…"); bare mentions do not navigate.
  - `legal/sign_in_critical_consent.dart` (new) — the four faithful consent points (reuses `kBiometricConsentSignInLine`) + a joined summary.
  - `screens/sign_in_critical_consent_dialog.dart` (new) — `showSignInCriticalConsent` dialog: the four points + buttons to the three legal screens (folded into the scrollable body, single "Got it" action, so it holds in short landscape/keyboard windows).
  - `screens/sign_in_screen.dart` — listens to `_voice.transcript`; on each new finalized **user voice** turn runs the parser once (identity-guarded against re-fire) and either `context.push`es the right legal screen or opens the critical-consent dialog.
  - `functions/bootstrap/welcome_persona.md` — added a "legal / consent screen" section giving Ayma the same four facts to state accurately by voice, and that the app opens those screens on request (prompt-file only; staging-safe; no code, no model name, no secret).
- **Tests:** `test/sign_in_voice_intent_test.dart` (parser + summary wording), `test/sign_in_voice_test.dart` (drives segments through the injected `voiceController` seam: each "open X" navigates to the right screen; "anything critical?" shows the dialog containing 18+, advertising, face recognition, public web, Privacy Policy; interim/chatter are no-ops). Layout matrix: added the `sign-in critical consent dialog` overlay target. `flutter analyze` clean; full `flutter test` = 1257 passed (incl. matrix); backend `pytest tests/test_prompt.py` = 52 passed.
- **Feature tour:** intentionally NOT added — the coach-mark tour is a post-sign-in, in-shell walkthrough anchored to tabs; a pre-sign-in capability has no in-shell anchor and signed-in users never see the sign-in page. Forcing an entry would mis-spotlight. Discoverable on the sign-in page itself and by voice.
- Docs: ARCHITECTURE.md §voice (sign-in greeting legal/consent intents note), this entry. **Flutter + one prompt-file change, no deploy of prod defaults. Branch off staging, pushed, NOT merged — lead engineer reviews + merges + rebuilds (the prompt change rides the next `ayma-agent`/`ayma-api` deploy).**

## Meta ledger label + server-minted image-variant URLs (feat/meta-label-and-variant-urls off staging, 2026-11-01) — DONE, lead engineer reviews + merges
Two small backend changes (`functions/bootstrap`), staging-first, branch NOT merged — lead reviews + merges. `python -m pytest tests/test_token_ledger.py tests/test_explore.py tests/test_public_profile.py tests/test_image_variants.py` = 125 passed.
- **Task A — Meta provider label (`token_ledger.py`):** `provider_for` had no Meta branch, so every Meta Muse Spark call (`muse-spark-1.3`, `meta-models/muse-spark-open-1026`) was mislabeled `google_vertex` and `token_usage.providers.meta` stayed empty. Added `PROVIDER_META = "meta"` and a `"muse-spark"` (case-insensitive) substring rule before the Vertex fallback, after the `google/`-prefix inference check. Forward-only (no retro rewrite of existing docs); reaches prod only via a future prod deploy. Tests assert both Meta ids → `meta`, gemini → `google_vertex`, `google/...` voice → `livekit_inference`.
- **Task B — server-minted variant URLs (`image_variants.py` + `public_profile.py` + `explore.py`):** the client can't derive another user's `__thumb_400`/`__medium_1080` URL (owner-only storage.rules + per-object tokens), so the backend mints them. New `image_variants.variant_object_name()` (serve-time path, no GCS call), `public_profile._variant_url()` (None when `image_variants_enabled` off ⇒ zero extra GCS calls; else token-mint via `_photo_url` on the variant object). New additive response fields: Explore card `photo_url_thumb`; public profile `photos_thumb[]` + `photos_medium[]` (1:1 with `photos[]`). Each falls back to the original when the variant is absent or the flag is off — byte-identical behaviour on prod; old clients ignore the new fields. Unblocks `docs/agents/handoffs/perf-phase2-img-display-blocked.md` step 1 (Muse: pure client field-mapping `photoUrlThumb ?? photoUrl`, `photosMedium[i] ?? photos[i]`).
- Docs: `docs/perf/image-variants.md` (new "Serving variant URLs" section + fields table), this entry, the handoff doc's "UNBLOCKED — DELIVERED" note. **Backend-only; Task A rides the next `ayma-api` deploy, Task B is staging-gated (flag off on prod). No Flutter changes (Muse's follow-up), no prod defaults, no new secret.**
## AUTH-UX — failed sign-in guides into sign-up instead of dead-ending (feat/auth-autosignup off staging, 2026-11-01) — DONE, lead engineer reviews + merges + rebuilds
- **Founder ask:** on a FAILED sign-in, guide the user into sign-up instead of dead-ending on an error toast — with a clear way back to login.
- **Fix (`sign_in_screen.dart`, surgical):**
  - **Email auto-switch** — a failed sign-in (`user-not-found` OR `invalid-credential`; newer Firebase lumps unknown-email AND wrong-password into `invalid-credential` under email-enumeration protection) now flips the form to create mode instead of only toasting: keeps the typed email + password, reveals + focuses the confirm-password field, and shows an inline nudge "New here? Confirm your password to create your account." (new `_createHint` state; new `onAuthError` seam on `_run`, passed only by the email flow — Google/Apple/phone keep the plain toast).
  - **Wrong-password robustness** — if the user then confirms and `createAccountWithEmailPassword` throws `email-already-in-use` (so the earlier failure was a mistyped password on an existing account, not a new user), the form switches BACK to sign-in mode and toasts "You already have an account — check your password." Both enumeration-hidden cases stay correct.
  - **Toggle both ways** — the existing "Already have an account? Sign in" / "Don't have an account? Sign up" switch is present in BOTH modes (always a route back to login); it now also clears the confirm field + nudge on switch.
  - **Google & Phone** — verified NO app-side gate rejects a brand-new user: `AuthService.signInWithGoogle` and `confirmPhoneCode`/`startPhoneSignIn` use Firebase `signInWithCredential`, which auto-creates the user, and the screen just navigates to Splash on success. "Sign in" and "sign up" are already one seamless flow for these providers — unchanged (no password/confirm added, correct for OAuth/passwordless).
- **Tests (`sign_in_screen_test.dart`):** replaced the now-outdated "error codes map to messages" test with: (a) a failed `invalid-credential` sign-in auto-switches to create mode (confirm field + nudge + back-to-login toggle, typed values kept, no raw code leak); `user-not-found` does the same; (b) `email-already-in-use` on the subsequent create returns to sign-in with the "check your password" nudge; a non-steering code (`too-many-requests`) still falls through to the plain toast with no mode switch. The existing mode-switch test covers (c) the toggle moving between modes.
- **Layout matrix:** added a synthetic `signin (create account)` target (new injectable `SignInScreen.startInCreateAccountMode` flag — test/matrix seam, defaults false) so the taller create variant (confirm + referral fields) is pumped at every matrix size; stays green.
- Docs: this entry. No ARCHITECTURE change (no documented sign-in error-contract to amend). **Flutter-only, no deploy — rides the next build. Branch off staging, NOT merged — lead engineer reviews + merges + rebuilds.**
