## GATE-RED — Ayma chat "red block" on a history-load failure (fix/gate-red-chat-error off staging, 2026-11-01) — DONE, lead engineer reviews + merges
- **Symptom (Scout, build 2109665, 2/2 on relaunch whenever `/chat/history` load failed):** the Ayma chat tab rendered a giant solid red `ErrorWidget` block; logs showed framework assertions `framework.dart:7243` (`_children.contains(child)` in `MultiChildRenderObjectElement.forgetChild`) and `framework.dart:2170` (`_elements.contains(element)` in `_InactiveElements.remove`) — the classic GlobalKey-in-a-multi-child reconciliation corruption.
- **Verify-before-fix outcome (honest):** on current `staging` the reported crash does **NOT** reproduce. The initial history-load failure path renders the graceful `"Couldn't load your conversation history." + Retry` state cleanly — proven by regression tests across initial-load failure, retry-that-fails-again, external scroll controller, shared pre-seeded store, `targetTimestamp`, dual-screen, and older-page failure. Every one: no `ErrorWidget`, `tester.takeException()` null. I could not construct the exact element-tree corruption in a widget test (asserts are on in test mode, so a real corruption would have fired). The closest related failing path I exercised — an *older*-page `/chat/history` fetch failing, which flips the `_loadingOlder` loader row while GlobalKey-ed turn rows are mounted — also renders cleanly.
- **Root-cause hardening applied (`chat_screen.dart`, the transcript `ListView.builder`):** the one residual latent hazard matching the assertion signature on a history-load path was that the older-page **loader row** and the **"Your conversation" divider row** were *unkeyed* while the turn rows carry `GlobalKey`s (`_turnKeys`) and queued rows carry `ValueKey`s. When `_loadingOlder` flips (older-page load start / **failure** / success) the loader row appears/disappears, shifting every keyed turn's index against the unkeyed siblings and forcing Flutter to *move* each GlobalKey-ed child mid-rebuild — exactly the `forgetChild` / inactive-element churn those assertions guard. Fix: gave the loader row `ValueKey('chat-older-loader')` and the divider `ValueKey('chat-conversation-divider')` so every row is keyed and the loader is a clean keyed insert/remove with no turn moves. Zero behaviour/visual change; success path untouched.
- **Durable guard added (lead engineer, `main.dart` + `lib/widgets/graceful_error.dart`):** because the exact relaunch crash could not be reproduced on identical code, the keying above is hardening, not a proven-load-bearing fix. To make the founder-facing symptom impossible regardless of the untracked trigger, the app now installs a custom `ErrorWidget.builder` (`gracefulErrorWidget`) so ANY build-phase exception renders a calm neutral fallback instead of Flutter's full-screen red block. Reporting is unchanged — `FlutterError.onError` (loud log + Crashlytics) fires independently, so the fleet still sees every failure. Applied in all modes on purpose (the founder/testers run debug builds and must never see the red screen; developers still get the console stack).
- **Tests:** new `ayma_flutter/test/graceful_error_test.dart` (the override turns a thrown build into the graceful fallback — no `ErrorWidget` — while the framework still records the exception); new `ayma_flutter/test/chat_history_load_error_test.dart` — (1) initial `/chat/history` failure (injected via the screen's `apiClient` seam) shows `"Couldn't load your conversation history."` + Retry, no `ErrorWidget`, `takeException` null, and a Retry-that-fails-again stays graceful; (2) an older-page failure flips the loader row with the transcript list intact, no `ErrorWidget`, no framework exception. `flutter analyze` clean; full `flutter test` green incl. the layout matrix (chat error state is an in-screen state, not a new route ⇒ no new matrix entry).
- Docs: this entry. No ARCHITECTURE change (no documented chat error-handling contract to amend; the generic-report "Couldn't load that report." + Retry pattern at §-generic-reports is the sibling this matches). **Flutter-only, no deploy — rides the next build. Branch off staging, NOT merged — lead engineer reviews + merges (verify the root cause first).**

## R2-H6 — a completed AI Date-Sim must be RE-OPENABLE (fix/h6-sim-reopenable off staging, 2026-11-01) — DONE, lead engineer reviews + merges
- **Bug:** a finished sim could not be re-opened. The server already persisted every sim at `users/{uid}/date_sims/{sim_id}` (status:done + verdict) and `DateSimScreen` already supported reopening via a `simId` param, but (1) completion fired NO notification, (2) nothing surfaced a completed sim in any list, and (3) the router never passed `simId`, so re-entering via ✨ always started a fresh setup.
- **Fix (smallest complete slice, no new flag):**
  - **Backend (`date_sim.py`):** persist `thread_id` on create (`other_uid` was already there); `_finalize` now stamps `completed_at` (renamed from the unused `finished_at`); on `status:done`, `run_simulation`/`continue_simulation` fire `notifications_triggers.notify_date_sim_done` — a `skill_result`-type push with a score-bearing preview + deep link `date_sim/{other_uid}/{sim_id}`, deduped per sim (`date_sim_{sim_id}`), best-effort (never fails the sim).
  - **Flutter:** the date-sim route now reads `sim_id` (query param or `extra['sim_id']`) → `DateSimScreen(simId:)`; `deepLinkToRoute` maps `date_sim/{uid}/{sim_id}` → `/date-sim/{uid}?sim_id=…`; `DateSimService.latestCompletedSim(otherUid)` (two equality filters, newest-by-`completed_at` client-side, no composite index, null on empty/error); the ✨ profile entry reopens the last completed sim with that person when one exists, else opens fresh.
  - Feature tour `date_sim` entry updated (tap ✨ again to reopen the last result).
- **Tests:** `test_date_sim.py` (`test_finalize_writes_completed_at_other_uid_thread_id_and_notifies`, `test_date_sim_notification_deep_link_reopens_the_sim`); `date_sim_test.dart` (✨-reopen carries sim_id; the existing `simId` render test); `notification_center_test.dart` (date_sim deep link). Layout matrix already covers `DateSimScreen` setup + reopened states (no new route) ⇒ stays green.
- Docs: ARCHITECTURE §18 (re-openability paragraph + updated doc-fields + flow diagram). **Backend (`date_sim.py`, `notifications_triggers.py`) needs an `ayma-api` redeploy; Flutter rides the next build. Branch off staging, NOT merged — lead engineer reviews + merges.**

## R2 profile/onboarding bugs — Astro QA against 1.30.0(2242) (fix/profile-onboarding-bugs-r2 off meta-hackathon, 2026-10-31) — DONE, founder integrates (Flutter-only, NO deploy)
Three Astro-filed bugs. **Branch off meta-hackathon, NOT merged — founder integrates; no store release.** Verified live on emulator-5554 (fresh email signup → onboarding → You). `flutter test` incl. the layout matrix stays green; `flutter analyze` clean; feature tour unaffected (no new routes/features).
- **R2-C1 [S3] — DOB free-text parser read MM/DD/YYYY as DD/MM.** `preboarding_flow_screen.dart` `_parseDob`: numeric date-first input (`mm/dd/yyyy`, `mmddyyyy`, `m/d/yy`, sep or all-digit) was day-first, so typing `03/05/1993` (US, the placeholder's own example) echoed "3 May 1993". **Fix:** read **month-first (US MM/DD)**, fall back to day-first only when month-first is impossible (first group >12). `yyyy-mm-dd` and month-name forms unchanged. Realigned the placeholder + error-hint example dates so all read as the same date under MM/DD. **Live-reproduced** before the fix (echo showed "→ 3 May 1993"). Regression: `test/preboarding_dob_parser_test.dart` — types `03/05/1993` into the real DOB field, asserts the echo "→ 5 March 1993" (never "3 May 1993"), plus the all-digit and day-first-fallback cases.
- **R2-C3 [S3] — Edit profile didn't bind a stored DOB.** Root cause in the read model, not the form: `profile_service.dart` `ProfileData.fromMap` parsed `birthdate` only when it was a Firestore `Timestamp`. The server writes an **ISO-8601 String** (`"1990-01-01"` — `set_profile_field` voice tool + seeded accounts, `functions/.../mcp_tools.py`/`completeness.py`), so a server-written DOB came back null and `ProfileSettingsScreen` ("Profile details"/"Edit profile") showed "Pick a date" even while the public profile showed the age. **Fix:** `_parseBirthdate` accepts `Timestamp | ISO String | DateTime | epoch-millis int`, null for unparseable. One-point fix at the model layer (also fixes `watchProfile`, preboarding autofill). Regression: `test/profile_data_birthdate_test.dart` (fromMap parses ISO/Timestamp/int, null-safe) + a `profile_settings_screen_test.dart` widget test asserting the form binds a server ISO-string DOB into the field (shows `1990-01-01`, not "Pick a date").
- **R2-C7 [S2] — new-account "My public profile" two overlapping layers: NOT REPRODUCIBLE on 1.30.0(2242).** Traced definitively: the "story-header" strings in the report ("10 TOPICS / 3 PHOTOS / 0 WEB FINDS", "Ayma keeps learning about you as you talk.", "01 PHOTOS — A few glimpses into my world") exist ONLY in the standalone **My story** screen (`story_editor_screen.dart` `_StoryCover`/`_PhotosSection`); `MyPublicProfileScreen`/`ProfileBody` never composes them (it renders `ProfilePhotoHeader` + `LivingReportView`, no cover). The SWR report cache is correctly keyed by `uid:reportType:reportId` (no story_private↔story_public bleed). **Live-verified clean, zero overlap:** fresh account "My public profile" (hero photo, "Alex · 33", "San", photo strip, "IDENTITY — Alex."), fresh account "My story", re-entry after viewing My story (incl. the transition frame), and an established full-story account. No code path overlays the two layers. **No behavioural change made** (changing working code would violate surgical-change rules). Added a guard/regression test anyway — `my_public_profile_screen_test.dart`: new-account public profile renders the clean single layer (ProfilePhotoHeader + real IDENTITY section), scrolls, and shows NONE of the story-cover strings. **Ask Astro to confirm the exact failing state** (account, device/orientation, build) — the gallery screenshots that look like the overlap are old (May/Jun) single-layer "My story" captures.
- Delegation: `gemini_code`/`deepseek_code`/`codex_code` not used — fixes were precise, context-critical, surgical edits authored directly (not a paid fallback).

## R2-C6 / R2-C7 ghost-header SYSTEMIC fix (fleet/ghost-header-systemic off staging, 2026-11-08) — DONE on branch, staging-lineage, NOT merged (MAIN FROZEN)
The surface fix (`c1bb923`, `story_editor_screen.dart` `_userChanged`/`_loadedForUid`) only guarded the Story screen. This closes the **navigation-stack + in-memory-singleton root cause** for the whole app (privacy-critical: on sign-out / account-switch Account A's story, DMs, reports, credits, chat transcript, prefetched tabs, live voice and dev-panel unlock could linger into Account B). Implements ghost-header spec §6 Files 1–5. `flutter analyze` = 0 issues; full `flutter test` green incl. all three layout-matrix shards (no routes added — the "every route has a layout-matrix entry" guard still passes).
- **New `AuthSessionState`** (`lib/services/auth_session_state.dart`) — pure `ChangeNotifier`, no `firebase_auth` import, merged into `appRouter.refreshListenable` so `redirect` re-runs on every auth transition; `redirect` reads it first with `FirebaseAuth.currentUser` fallback.
- **New `teardownSession({isSwitch})`** (`lib/core/session_teardown.dart`) — stops voice + dismisses the Ayma popup, purges every cross-account singleton, routes to SignIn (sign-out) / Splash (switch), bumps `appRecoveryEpoch` (rebuilds the whole router/navigator subtree, destroying cached shell tabs + pushed routes), then clears `AuthSessionState`. Cold-boot safe (fires only when `lastKnownUid != null`). Theme/accent/localization/nav keys deliberately survive (spec §5.2).
- **`main.dart` auth listener** calls `teardownSession` on sign-out and on real account switch; preserves all sign-in work (`notifyAuthSessionRestored`, RevenueCat `logIn`, `push.syncToken`, `DeepLinkQueue.drainPostAuth`).
- **StoryEditor surface fix KEPT** as defense-in-depth.
- **Drift adaptations vs the 6-day-old spec:** no `rootNavigatorKey` exists → popup dismissed via `screenPopupTracker.navigator`; popup flag is `isAymaSheetOpen` (not `isPageContextAymaPopupOpen`); `DeepLinkQueue` had no public clear → added `clearPendingIntent()`; `TabPrefetchCoordinator.clear()` and `MatchReportCache.clearAll()` added as public (the existing `debugReset()`/`reset()` are `@visibleForTesting` — illegal to call from prod under the analyze gate); `PushService()` eagerly builds an `ApiClient` (→`FirebaseAuth`), so the three external-transport calls are each try/catch-wrapped.
- Regression: `test/session_teardown_test.dart` (AuthSessionState pure-ChangeNotifier contract; `teardownSession` isSwitch false/true purges every singleton + bumps epoch). Scout to run spec §7 QA cases 1–5 on the installed staging APK.

## DM media v1: GIFs + images "at par with WhatsApp" (feat/dm-media-gif-image off meta-hackathon, 2026-10-30) — DONE, founder integrates (NO deploy — ships after Qloo submission)
- **Founder ask:** make DM messaging support media at par with WhatsApp — v1 = GIFs + images (send, store, display). The composer's keyboard GIF/image button showed "This app does not support images here".
- **Finding:** the image/file media pipeline (attach picker gallery/camera/file → Firebase Storage upload → backend `dm_ops.post_message` attachment fields → inline bubble render + full-screen viewer) was **already built** on `meta-hackathon`. The genuine gaps were (a) keyboard rich-content insertion (the toast) and (b) an explicit `gif` type. Extended, did not rebuild.
- **Built:** (1) **Keyboard rich content** — `ContentInsertionConfiguration` on the DM composer `TextField` (`dm_thread_screen.dart`); `_onContentInserted` uploads the inserted bytes via a new `DmService.uploadInsertedAttachment` (Storage `putData`, no temp file / no new dep) and sends immediately as a `gif`/`image` message (WhatsApp-style). (2) **`gif` message type** — `dm_schema.MESSAGE_GIF` added to `MESSAGE_TYPES` + `ATTACHMENT_MESSAGE_TYPES`; `main.DmMessageRequest.type` Literal accepts `gif`; `DmMessage.hasGif`; bubble renders gif inline (same `Image.network` path, which animates GIFs) + `📷 GIF` label + `[gif]` assist placeholder. Gallery image path unchanged. (3) **Feature tour** — `dm_media` entry in `tour_content.dart` (anchored to the Messages tab; the DM thread isn't a shell tab so no thread-level anchor is spotlightable).
- **Decisions:** kept `Image.network` for the thumbnail (already animates GIFs; satisfies the ask; switching to `cached_network_image` risked the existing viewer + widget test — surgical). Inserted media sends immediately rather than into the pending-preview (keyboard hands bytes, not a File).
- **FOLLOW-UP — needs founder input: in-app GIF SEARCH (Tenor/Giphy) needs an API key.** A WhatsApp-style in-app GIF search picker requires a Tenor or Giphy API key; none available, none invented/hardcoded. v1 ships keyboard-GIF + gallery-image; the dedicated GIF-search picker is deferred until the founder provides a Tenor/Giphy key.
- **FOLLOW-UP — media NSFW moderation.** The text `dm_moderation` path is untouched and still gates text. Image/GIF NSFW moderation (a vision classifier on the uploaded bytes) is **not built** — documented follow-up; no existing gate was removed.
- **Tests:** backend `test_dm_ops.py` — gif is a known attachment type; `post_message` stores a gif attachment (url+type); `/dm` endpoint accepts `gif` and forwards the attachment. Flutter `dm_thread_screen_test.dart` — a `gif` message renders inline + taps to the viewer; the composer declares `contentInsertionConfiguration` and inserting a GIF uploads+sends it as a `gif` message. Existing image/file render tests unchanged.
- **Code-delegate MCPs were down** (gemini 503, deepseek premature-close, codex down) so the `.dart`/`.py` edits were authored directly from fully-specified drafts — re-delegate if desired.
- Docs: ARCHITECTURE §6.6 (data model: types + attachment fields + GIF/media paths + media-moderation follow-up) and §6.10 (storage map row). Layout matrix: no new route (DM route already in `layout_targets.dart`) ⇒ stays green. **Backend (`dm_schema.py`, `main.py`) needs an `ayma-api` redeploy; Flutter rides the next build. Branch off meta-hackathon, NOT merged, NOT deployed (ships after Qloo submission) — founder integrates.**

## DM coach: stop leaking long rambles + raw reasoning/JSON into the composer hint (fix/dm-coach-long-text off meta-hackathon, 2026-10-30) — DONE, founder integrates + deploys
- **Bug (founder, screenshots):** the DM coach hint under the composer dumped LONG rambling text and even the model's raw chain-of-thought + raw JSON into the UI (e.g. a tip rambling "…smoothly naturally quietly comfortably easily…" for a paragraph; another leaking "Wait, what about safety? … Guardrails: Base every flag on something `Them` actually said … JSON shape: {\"chips\":[…],\"tip\":\"…\",\"safety\":{…}}"). Supposed to be a SHORT one-sentence tip + chips + a safety flag parsed from structured output.
- **Root cause:** the coach (`dm_assist.py` + `prompts/dm_coach.md`, surface `dm_coach`) defaults to **Meta Muse Spark** (`llm_provider_dm_coach=meta`), a reasoning model. The recent §6.9.1 **safety red-flag** addition (commit `1fd14ba`, Oct 27) made the prompt reasoning-heavy (long SAFETY/GUARDRAILS block — the leaked text quotes it verbatim), so Muse over-produced chain-of-thought and stuffed it into the JSON string fields. The parser had **no brevity or leak guard**: `_clean_tip` returned any string verbatim (no length/sentence cap), and `MetaProvider.generate_json` did a bare `json.loads` with no extraction, so a reasoning-wrapped reply either fell back to Gemini or (when the thought landed inside a valid field) surfaced raw. Reasoning is already pinned `minimal` in `config.py`; the leak is a parser-hygiene defect, not a routing one — kept on Meta per the standing "everything on Muse" rule.
- **Fix (root cause, two layers):** (1) `llm_provider._coerce_json_text` — extracts just the first balanced JSON document (strips `<think>…</think>`, ```` ```json ```` fences, surrounding prose; brace-scan that skips string literals) before `json.loads`, so reasoning never reaches a parsed field and a malformed reply still raises → Gemini fallback. Benefits every Meta JSON surface. (2) `dm_assist._clean_tip` — enforces the `tip` is ONE short sentence (first-sentence split, ≤180 chars) and **drops** it if it carries leaked-JSON/reasoning markers (`{`,`}`,```` ``` ````,`<think`, schema keys, "JSON shape"); the safety `reason` fails quiet on the same markers. UI shows nothing rather than a ramble or raw reasoning. Prompt + credit/safety behavior untouched.
- **Regression tests:** `test_dm_assist.py` — short clean tip passes; rambling tip dropped (chips kept); the exact leaked reasoning+JSON tip dropped; tip trimmed to first sentence; safety reason with leaked JSON fails quiet. `test_llm_provider.py` — `_coerce_json_text` passes plain JSON, strips fence, strips think-block+prose, ignores braces inside strings; `generate_json` extracts JSON from a reasoning wrapper.
- Backend-only (`functions/bootstrap`): `llm_provider.py`, `dm_assist.py`, tests. No Flutter change (`dm_thread_screen.dart` just renders the backend `tip` string → no layout-matrix run needed). All three code-delegate MCPs were down (gemini 503, deepseek premature-close, codex down) so the edits were authored directly — re-delegate if desired. Docs: ARCHITECTURE §6.9 (new "Output hygiene" paragraph), this entry. **Needs `ayma-api` redeploy; the voice `ayma-agent` does NOT use the coach, so no agent redeploy. Branch off meta-hackathon, NOT merged (founder integrates).**
## Clean-language guardrail: Ayma's OWN output stays non-crude in every conversation (fix/ayma-clean-language-guardrail off meta-hackathon, 2026-10-30) — DONE, founder integrates + deploys
- **Founder content-safety rule.** In ANY conversation — human↔Ayma (voice + chat) AND any Ayma-controlled/AI-generated conversation (date sim, practice, avatar, suggested DM replies) — Ayma must NEVER use obscene, vulgar, or crude sexual language (crude anatomical slang like "boobs", explicit vulgarity, degrading sexual terms), and must NEVER mirror or adopt the user's crude wording even if they use it repeatedly. Stays respectful/tasteful even in flirty/adult (18+) contexts; redirects warmly without repeating/escalating the crude term. About crudeness only — ordinary mature/flirty themes still fine.
- **Where added (prompts are FILES, never inline — AGENTS.md):** a **"Clean language (hard rule)"** block in `persona.md` (base chat/voice/welcome prompt → every `compose_system_prompt(variant="text"/"voice")` + `compose_welcome_prompt()`), `prompts/date_sim.md` (AI↔AI date/avatar sim, both sides), `prompts/practice_persona.md` (human↔AI practice, text + the appended `practice_persona_voice.md` voice framing), `prompts/dm_coach.md` (opener/coach suggested-reply chips + tip), `prompts/persona_snapshot.md` (per-user avatar brief, so crudeness never bakes into the avatar seed). Also reconciled the auto-managed `prompts/lessons.md` register bullet ("match their *energy*, not their vocabulary") so the appended-lessons layer no longer reads as licence to mirror crude wording.
- **Did NOT touch** the inbound safety reviewers (`dm_moderation` / `profile_text_moderation` / `ai_assessment`) — separate concern (they flag *user* content; this rule governs Ayma's *own* output).
- **Regression test:** `functions/bootstrap/tests/test_clean_language_guardrail.py` (7 tests, drafted by `gemini_code`) — asserts the guardrail text is present in each composed/loaded prompt (chat + voice system prompt, date_sim, practice text+voice, dm_coach, persona_snapshot), and still present when a crude user turn is in context. All 7 green.
- Docs: ARCHITECTURE §13.2a (new). Backend-only (`functions/bootstrap` markdown prompts + one test). **Needs `ayma-api` + `ayma-agent` redeploy (persona lives in both).** **Branch off meta-hackathon, NOT merged — founder integrates + deploys.**

## Voice agent: real match score in context + fuzzy name open + demo persona (fix/voice-match-context-and-persona off meta-hackathon, 2026-10-29) — DONE, founder integrates
- Three founder-reported live voice-call bugs:
  - **BUG 1 — voice agent read a 99% score as "low" / "one out of one hundred".** Root cause: the `matches.score` field is stored on the **0–1** scale (e.g. `0.99` for a 99% match; `report_adapters` multiplies by 100 for the UI), but `public_profile._viewing_profile_context` — the private match-read briefing fed to the voice agent via `open_profile` / `current_screen` — formatted it as `round(score)/100`, so `0.99` became **"1/100 compatibility"**. The model faithfully called that low. **Fix (surgical):** compute `pct = round(float(score) * 100)` → "99/100 compatibility", and add that it's a 0–100 scale (100 = best) so the model never calls a high score low. An existing test had masked the bug by feeding a 0–100 value (`73.4`); corrected to `0.734` and a 0.99→"99/100" regression added (`test_public_profile.py`).
  - **BUG 2 — "open Sophia" hit the reject path instead of opening "Sofia".** Root cause: `skills/app_control._resolve_person` (used by `open_profile` / `open_match_report`) matched only exact full name / exact first name / substring — a near-identical spelling scored 0 and was rejected. **Fix:** added a `difflib` fuzzy tier (ratio ≥ `_FUZZY_NAME_THRESHOLD` = 0.72 on first names / full strings, ranked strictly below every exact/substring tier so it only decides when nothing better matches and the closest candidate wins) — "Sophia" now opens "Sofia" directly; unrelated names still find nobody. Mirrors the close-spelling fallback `open_message_thread` already had. Regression tests in `test_app_control.py`.
  - **BUG 3 — demo persona.** Added a **"Being shown off (demos)"** section to `persona.md` (a carve-out from the dating-only scope): when the user says they're about to demo Ayma (e.g. a hackathon), Ayma responds enthusiastically ("Yeah, let's do it! Let's show off what I can do") and offers to run a real capability on the spot — never dismissively ("hackathons aren't really my thing").
- Backend-only (`functions/bootstrap`): `public_profile.py`, `skills/app_control.py`, `persona.md`. The two `.py` edits were drafted by `gemini_code`; the `public_profile` test edits were authored directly only because both code agents were unavailable at the time (gemini 429 daily-quota, deepseek premature-close) — re-delegate if desired. Docs: ARCHITECTURE §13.4 (persona demo carve-out), §13.5-adjacent `_viewing_profile_context` paragraph (score scale) + the app-control navigation-tools bullet (`_resolve_person` fuzzy). Needs `ayma-agent` + `ayma-api` redeploy (persona + context live in both). **Branch off meta-hackathon, NOT merged (founder integrates).**
## Match report: revert "Compatibility" → "Match report", fix Qloo-card scroll skip, de-cram match-request header (branch `fix/match-report-ui-scroll-terminology` off meta-hackathon, 2026-10-30) — DONE, founder integrates
Three founder-reported Flutter bugs on the match report / match-request screens. **Branch off meta-hackathon, NOT merged — founder integrates into the stable build; no deploy.**
- **BUG 1 — terminology revert.** The founder disliked the `fix/report-ux` split that titled a non-mutual pair "Compatibility" vs "Match". Reverted to a single consistent wording: the report screen's AppBar always reads **"Match report"** (`match_report_screen.dart`), and the report body always labels its top section **"Match"**, its score **"Match score"**, and its title **"Match with X"** (`report_adapters.match_blocks` no longer branches on `status`; removed the now-unused `matching_schema` import). The `mutual` data flag is **kept** (`GET /match` still returns it; Flutter `MatchReport.mutual` still parses it) for future callers — it just no longer swaps any label. Conversational/grounding phrasing ("compatibility report" in chat/push/profile grounding) left untouched — not what the founder flagged. Backend tests updated (`test_report_adapters.py`: default + mutual case now both assert "Match").
- **BUG 2 — Qloo taste card skipped on scroll.** Root cause: `_Report` wrapped the avatar header, the shrink-wrapped `LivingReportView`, and the trailing "Taste match · powered by Qloo" card in a **lazy `ListView`**; the lazy sliver mis-estimated the scroll extent of the variable-height embedded report, so a bottom-to-top fling overshot and could skip the taste card (the demo hero). Fix: replaced the outer lazy `ListView` with a `SingleChildScrollView` + `Column(crossAxisAlignment: stretch)` — every child is fully laid out, exact extent, smooth both directions, taste card always reachable.
- **BUG 3 — cramped match-request header.** On `MatchDetailScreen` ("Ayma found someone for you", `/match-request/:matchId`), the "N% match / Since you want…" case card lived in the `ChromeColumn` `header:` slot, capped at 30% of screen height and crammed against the profile photo. Fix: added an optional `ProfileBody.leading` slot and moved the card there, so it rides inside the profile's own scroll above the photo with full width + room (margin `20,12,20,16`, score line bumped to w600, 10px gap). Header now holds only the title bar.
- Regression tests (real path): `match_report_screen_test.dart` — AppBar title reads "Match report"; Qloo taste card present & reachable via `scrollUntilVisible`. `matches_screen_test.dart` — match-request card with a long explanation lays out with no overflow at 320×568.
- Tests: backend `test_report_adapters.py` + `test_report_hub.py` green (108). Flutter `match_report_screen_test` / `matches_screen_test` / `taste_match_report_section_test` / `public_profile_screen_test` green; layout matrix green. Docs: ARCHITECTURE §5.8 (terminology paragraph rewritten), this entry. No new route ⇒ no feature-tour change. **Flutter rides the next build; backend (`report_adapters.py`) needs an `ayma-api` redeploy.**

## Explore: hide seeded TEST FIXTURES from real users (keep curated demo set) (fix/hide-test-fixtures-from-explore off meta-hackathon, 2026-10-29) — DONE, founder deploys + coordinates e2e
- **Bug (founder):** the `demo-edge-*` corner-case fixtures (Mara/Elena/Hana/Nadia/Robin/Teresa — seeded by `scripts/seed_demo_edge_accounts.py` to exercise the dealbreaker gates) were showing in real users' Explore, worsened by the "dealbreakers SHOW in Explore" fix above. Founder product call: **keep a curated demo set visible, hide only the test fixtures.**
- **Flag:** `exclude_from_explore: true` written on each fixture's top-level `users/{uid}` doc. Set by `seed_demo_edge_accounts.py` (added alongside the existing `demo_edge: true`); the curated set `demo-test1/2/3` (Daniel/Sofia/Priya) does NOT get it and stays visible. Practice personas (`demo_practice_*`) already excluded — unchanged.
- **Filter:** `functions/bootstrap/explore.py::_is_test_fixture` drops any user with the flag (authoritative) or a `demo-edge-` uid prefix (secondary safety net), in the same stream-loop spot as the practice-persona exclusion. Reads the flag off the `users` stream snapshot — no extra read.
- **E2E bypass:** `config.explore_fixture_bypass_uids` (env `EXPLORE_FIXTURE_BYPASS_UIDS`, default `demo-test1,demo-test2,demo-test3`) — when the *viewer's* uid is in the set, fixture-hiding is disabled for that viewer only, so `e2e_edge_test.dart` (signed in as Daniel=demo-test1) still sees all `demo-edge-*`. Real users never match. Contract for the GATES test documented in `docs/TESTING_E2E.md`.
- **Backfill:** `scripts/backfill_fixture_exclude_flag.py` (idempotent; `--revert` untags). **RAN against live Firestore (project ayma-ai) — all 6 `demo-edge-*` docs now carry `exclude_from_explore: true`; demo-test1/2/3 untouched.** Founder's current Explore is clean once `ayma-api` redeploys.
- Tests: new `functions/bootstrap/tests/test_explore_hides_fixtures.py` (regression: a normal viewer sees NO `demo-edge-*` and NO practice persona but DOES see `demo-test1/2/3`; a bypass viewer sees the fixtures). Full `functions/bootstrap` pytest green (3 known pre-existing failures aside). Docs: ARCHITECTURE §6.1 + matching diagram, TESTING_E2E (bypass contract + GATES bullet), this line. **`seed_demo_edge_accounts.py` + `docs/TESTING_E2E.md` live only on `test/e2e-integration-harness`; they were pulled onto this branch to edit — main reconciles with the e2e agent on integration.** Backend — needs `ayma-api` redeploy; no Flutter change. **Branch `fix/hide-test-fixtures-from-explore` off meta-hackathon, NOT merged (founder integrates).**

## Dealbreakers: SHOW in Explore (don't hide) + "Why this is 0%" report + cache input-staleness (fix/dealbreakers-show-not-hide off meta-hackathon, 2026-10-29) — DONE, founder integrates
- **Product correction (founder, 2026-10-29) — REVERSES the "Explore drops dealbreakers" half of the fix/match-dealbreakers item below.** A profile that violates the viewer's gender/age/location preferences must APPEAR in Explore again; the dealbreaker surfaces as a 0% report, not a hidden person. Showing the person + explaining the zero beats silently dropping them.
  - **Explore revert (surgical):** removed `explore._hard_dealbreaker` and its two now-unused imports (`match_deterministic`, `matching`); the hard-dealbreaker drop and its docstring/comment are gone. **Practice-persona exclusion (`practice_seed.is_practice_persona` / `demo_practice_*`) STAYS** — that's the separate, correct Maya-no-photos fix (BUG E). `test_explore.py`: reverted the 3 dealbreaker tests, kept the practice test.
  - **Score still 0% on hard dealbreakers (unchanged, verified):** `HARD_DEALBREAKER_GATES` / `is_hard_dealbreaker` force `score` → 0.0 in `score_structured` + `matching_skill.score_pair`.
  - **"Why this is 0%" report section:** `report_adapters.match_blocks` now titles the gate section **"Why this is 0%"** with a lead sentence + a bulleted reason per failed hard gate (real values, e.g. "Outside a stated partner age range: one wants 28–38, the other is 42." — enriched `match_deterministic._gate_age_range`), at the TOP of the report before the dimension breakdown. Soft gate failures keep the "Dealbreakers" heading. `gate_failures` already flows backend → `GET /match` → Flutter model; the match report renders it via the shared `LivingReportView` (no bespoke widget, no new route → no layout-matrix/tour entry needed).
  - **Cache input-staleness (bug F staleness, coordinator add-on):** a complete `matches/{id}` doc is still served instantly, but `main._match_doc_is_stale` now treats it as a cache miss when either party's inputs changed after it was scored (`_match_inputs_mtime` = newest `updated_at` across each person's `profile/main` + `questionnaire/*`). Stale → serve cached THIS open, silent background recompute (`_match_job(notify=False)`); next open is correct. Fresh → served as-is (F unchanged). Manual Recalculate (`force=true`) still the force path. So narrowing your age range (adding a dealbreaker) no longer leaves a stale 65% — it refreshes to 0%.
- Tests: `test_explore.py`, `test_match_deterministic.py`, `test_matching_skill.py`, `test_report_adapters.py` (+ new "Why this is 0%" test), `test_match_report_cache.py` (+ new `_latest_ts`/`_match_inputs_mtime`/`_match_doc_is_stale` + stale-doc endpoint tests). Docs: ARCHITECTURE §5.4 / §5.4a / §6.1 + matching diagram. **Branch `fix/dealbreakers-show-not-hide` off meta-hackathon, NOT merged (founder integrates); needs `ayma-api` redeploy; Flutter rides next build (no Flutter change).**

## Taste match: stop surfacing INFERRED tastes as stated / mislabeled overlap (bug G) (fix/taste-grounding off meta-hackathon, 2026-10-29) — DONE
- **Bug G:** the "Taste match" card presented Qloo cross-domain *predictions* as stated/shared tastes and mislabeled per-domain overlap. Real evidence (founder × "Sofia Marino"): the card showed "Books: 25" as the STRONGEST overlap and a narrative "tastes meet around One World Trade Center, The Anthropocene Reviewed, How to Be Perfect…" — books the founder never listed (he listed only Music/Film/Food) — while Music and Film (both stated) showed 0, and the header read "Different tastes" while the narrative claimed an overlap.
- **Root causes (`functions/bootstrap/taste_match.py::_match_with`):** (1) domains were scored when *either* person listed them (`d in a_taste or d in b_taste`) → a domain only one side filled (books) got a Qloo-prediction score and could be the strongest; (2) shared Qloo *recommendation* names were appended to `shared` → predicted entities surfaced as stated/shared tastes; (3) the "Strongest overlap: …" line was appended even in the "Different tastes" band → header/narrative contradiction.
- **Fix (surgical, same Qloo calls/endpoints — presentation + scoring semantics only):** (1) score only domains BOTH people stated (`and`), so a one-sided domain is never scored/shown/strongest; (2) `shared` chips = ONLY entities both people listed outright — Qloo predictions drive the per-domain score but are never surfaced as a stated taste; (3) each person's per-domain set now = stated entities **plus** recs (a shared favourite always counts; a stated domain never reads a spurious 0); (4) summary phrases a shared taste only from names both listed ("X and Y both listed …") and appends the strongest-overlap domain only above the "Different tastes" band, so header and narrative agree. Flutter: a small muted "You both listed" label now sits above the `shared` chips, visibly separating them from the inferred "Why you match" descriptors.
- Tests: backend `test_taste_match.py` **32 passed** (new `test_domain_only_one_side_stated_is_never_an_overlap`: one-sided domain never scored/strongest/shared, stated overlap preserved); `flutter test` green incl. layout matrix (date-sim taste match card entry). Docs: ARCHITECTURE §19 (prose + diagram), QLOO_TASTE scoring section, this line. No new route ⇒ `qloo_taste` tour entry unchanged. **Branch `fix/taste-grounding` off meta-hackathon, NOT merged (founder integrates); Flutter rides the next build, no deploy.**

## Bug C — Web research screen: distinguish "never run" from "load failed" (branch `fix/web-research-empty-state` off meta-hackathon, 2026-10-29) — DONE, founder integrates
- The "Web research" screen (`deep_dive_report_screen.dart`, rendered via `LivingReportView`) showed **"Couldn't load that report." + Retry** in BOTH cases: (1) a dive was genuinely never run for the subject, and (2) a real fetch failure of an existing report. "Couldn't load" wrongly implied an error when nothing had ever been generated.
- **No backend change needed:** `GET /report/deep_dive/{id}` (`report_hub.get_report`) already returns a **clean 404** (`KeyError` from `deep_dive.read_dive` → no stored dive) for "never run", distinct from a 5xx / connection-class error. The conflation was purely client-side.
- **Fix (Flutter only):**
  1. `living_report_view.dart` — new `_neverRun` state set when the fetch throws an `ApiException` with `statusCode == 404` (vs `_loadFailed` for anything else); new optional `emptyBuilder` param rendered in place of the retry error when `_neverRun`. No `emptyBuilder` ⇒ a 404 still shows the retry error (unchanged for every other report type).
  2. `deep_dive_report_screen.dart` — passes an `emptyBuilder` rendering a **"No web research yet" + "Run web search"** empty state (`_NoResearchYet`, `CenteredScroll`). "Run web search" derives the subject from the report id (`uid_<subject>` → that uid, server infers `is_self`; `name_<slug>` → that name) and calls the shared `_startRun` (same `POST /deep-dive` force-run as the AppBar "Re-run"), then the in-progress banner + `DeepDiveActivity` swap the result in. A ran-but-found-nothing dive is NOT a 404 (adapter returns a "Nothing found yet." block) → renders normally.
- Tests: `deep_dive_report_screen_test.dart` +3 (404 → empty state not error; "Run web search" on `uid_bob` POSTs `target_uid: bob, force: true`; 500 → the "Couldn't load" retry still, not the empty state). Layout matrix: new synthetic target `web research (never run)` (`layout_targets.dart` + `layoutApi404()` in `layout_fakes.dart`) — green at 320x568→Fold, landscape, 300px split, textScale 2.0, keyboard, RTL, light/dark. Full `flutter test` + `functions/bootstrap` pytest green. No new route and the surface/anchor is unchanged ⇒ no feature-tour change. No data-flow change ⇒ architecture diagram unchanged. Docs: ARCHITECTURE.md (generic-reports §), this line. **Flutter-only — rides the next build, no deploy. Branch off meta-hackathon, NOT merged (founder integrates).**
- **Founder fast-follow (branch `fix/web-research-run-action` off meta-hackathon, 2026-10-29):** the empty state already had a working button (`_NoResearchYet`'s `FilledButton` → `_startRun(firstRun: true)` = same `POST /deep-dive` as "Re-run"); the founder's refinement was purely the **label**, renamed **"Run report" → "Run web search"** (button + body copy + the code comments in `deep_dive_report_screen.dart` / `living_report_view.dart` + 3 test assertions + `layout_targets.dart` comment + ARCHITECTURE.md). No logic change. `flutter test` green (`deep_dive_report_screen_test.dart` drives the exact 404 → "Run web search" tap → `POST /deep-dive {target_uid, force:true}` path; layout matrix `web research (never run)` target green across all configs); `flutter analyze` clean. Flutter-only, rides next build.
- **Founder UX bug: profile web-research icon auto-started the search (branch `fix/web-research-icon-opens-report` off meta-hackathon, 2026-10-29) — DONE, founder integrates.** On another user's profile the action-rail web-research icon (magnifier+sparkle) with **no report yet** called `_runWebSearch` → `POST /deep-dive` directly and jumped straight to the "Searching the web…" spinner. Wrong: tapping the icon should only **open** the deep-dive screen (which already shows the existing report, or the "No web research yet" + "Run web search" empty state from Bug C/`fix/web-research-run-action`), and the search should start only on the explicit "Run web search" tap. **Fix (Flutter only, `public_profile_screen.dart`):** the rail button's `onPressed` now always just navigates — `/deep-dive/<deep_dive_id>` when a report exists, else `/deep-dive/uid_<uid>` (the never-run empty state). Deleted the now-dead `_runWebSearch` method, the `_startingSearch` field, the `_Actions` `searching`/`onWebSearch` params + fields, and the now-unused `dart:async` + `deep_dive_activity.dart` imports. **The deep-dive screen already does NOT auto-run on open** (`initState`/`_loadMeta` only resume a run the *server* reports `running`); the only auto-start was the profile icon. Tests: `public_profile_screen_test.dart` — the entry-point regression now asserts the icon tap fires **no** `POST` and pushes `/deep-dive/uid_her` (replacing the old test that locked in the auto-run; removed the stale icon out-of-credits test); `deep_dive_report_screen_test.dart` — 404 empty-state test now also asserts **no** `POST` on open, plus a new capped "Run web search" out-of-credits test. `flutter analyze` clean; `flutter test` green incl. layout matrix (existing `web research (never run)` target, no new route). Docs: ARCHITECTURE.md generic-reports §. No surface/anchor change ⇒ no feature-tour change; no data-flow change ⇒ diagram unchanged. **Branch off meta-hackathon, NOT merged (founder integrates). Flutter-only, rides next build, no deploy.**
## Matching/Explore: hard dealbreakers + practice-persona leak + cached reports (fix/match-dealbreakers, 2026-10-29) — DONE
- Four backend bugs (branch `fix/match-dealbreakers` off `meta-hackathon`, NOT merged — founder integrates):
  - **BUG B — gender/age/location are HARD dealbreakers (exclude + 0%).** `interested_in`/`gender` (mutual `matching.orientation_match`), partner age range (`qz_partner_age_min`/`max`, **strict — `_AGE_TOLERANCE_YEARS=0`**) and location/relocation (`qz_relocation_radius`/`location`, a **one-sided** refusal to move is enough) now (1) **exclude** a candidate from Explore (`explore._hard_dealbreaker`) and (2) drive a computed report to **exactly 0.0** (`match_deterministic.HARD_DEALBREAKER_GATES`/`is_hard_dealbreaker`, applied in `score_structured` + `matching_skill.score_pair`) — not the old 10–25 soft `_gate_cap` floor (which still applies to the other, soft gates). New `_gate_orientation` gate. **[PARTIALLY REVERSED 2026-10-29 — see the "Dealbreakers: SHOW in Explore" item at the top of this file: the Explore-exclusion half (`explore._hard_dealbreaker`) was removed; the 0% report half stays.]**
  - **BUG E — practice personas leaked into Explore.** `demo_practice_*` personas (`practice_seed.is_practice_persona`) excluded from `explore()` and `matching.source_and_score`. Practice Date feature itself untouched.
  - **BUG F — don't recompute an existing report.** `POST /check-compatibility` serves a COMPLETE cached report and only recomputes on `force=true`; `GET /match/{id}` reads the persisted doc. `match_id` is deterministic (`sorted_pair_key`).
  - **BUG H — stale "lite" demo-seed reports.** `main._match_doc_is_complete` treats a score-only doc (no `dimensions`/`structured_score`/`method`) as a cache MISS → full recompute (`check-compatibility` re-scores; `GET /match` queues a silent `_match_job(..., notify=False)`). `scripts/seed_demo_accounts.py::persist_match` now writes the FULL report payload (idempotent).
- **Reverses** the explore.py "no orientation/community gating" product decision of 2026-08-30 — only the **orientation** half (founder explicitly asked, 2026-10-29); **community** is still NOT gated. Docstrings + ARCHITECTURE §5.1/§5.4/§5.4a/§6.1 + matching diagram updated.
- Tests: `test_match_deterministic.py` (orientation gate, strict age, one-sided relocation, hard-vs-soft), `test_matching_skill.py` (orientation → 0.0), `test_matching.py` (practice exclusion), `test_explore.py` (practice + gender/age/location exclusion), new `test_match_report_cache.py` (lite→recompute, complete→cache hit). Full `functions/bootstrap` pytest green. **Backend — needs an `ayma-api` redeploy; re-run the demo seed to refresh lite match docs.**
## Match/Compatibility report UX: terminology + Instagram share picker + report cache (branch `fix/report-ux` off meta-hackathon, 2026-10-29) — DONE, founder integrates
- Three UX fixes on the shareable report screen (`match_report_screen.dart`). **Branch off meta-hackathon, NOT merged — founder integrates.**
- **BUG D — "Match" vs "Compatibility".** A *match* = a genuine **mutual like** (`matches/{id}.status == "mutual_interest"`); a scored-but-not-mutual pair is only a compatibility preview. `GET /match/{id}` now returns `mutual` (from `status`), read by Flutter `MatchReport.mutual` for the AppBar title (`Match` only when mutual, else `Compatibility`). `report_adapters.match_blocks` reads the same `status` → score block label, section header and report title read "Compatibility" / "Match" accordingly. AI-conversation/grounding text fixed to say "compatibility report", not "your match": `_match_chat_text`, `_match_label`, the match-done push (`main.py`), and `public_profile._viewing_profile_context`. Tests updated + a mutual-case test added. **Out of scope (flagged, tracked below):** preview-labelled-"match" copy on OTHER surfaces — Explore "% match" badge + "Match run" filters (`explore_screen.dart`), scored-match digest push (`notifications_triggers.notify_new_scored_matches` / `notification_service` channel label), Matches list (`matches_screen.dart`), tour card "Matched on taste" (`tour_content.dart` `qloo_taste`), and `taste_match_card.dart` / `taste_match.py` (owned by another agent). The Practice/Date-sim prompts (`prompts/practice_*.md`, `date_sim.md`) call the subject "the match" but are **ambiguous** (launchable from Explore = non-mutual, or a real match) — left as-is per "flag, don't guess"; `persona.md` already carries the canonical rule.
- **BUG P — Instagram-DM share picker.** `RecipientPicker` (`share_report_sheet.dart`) rebuilt: each row = profile photo (`AymaPhoto`) + name + per-row **Send** button; tapping the avatar opens that profile (`/profile/user/:uid`); a search box filters by name; **mutual matches first** (badged "Matched"), then other threads. Same data source (DM threads + mutual matches, `/profiles/mini` for names/photos). Picker made public so the layout matrix can pump it; new matrix entry "send-to-user recipient picker" (+ `/profiles/mini` fake).
- **BUG F — cache the opened report.** New session in-memory `MatchReportCache` (`match_report_cache.dart`): open does a cache-aware `GET /match/{id}` (no forced recompute) and re-opening the same report paints instantly (quiet background GET refresh); heavy loading only when genuinely uncached. The report *body* already caches via `ReportCache` (SWR). Recalculate button still forces `POST /check-compatibility force:true`. (Backend GET/force caching is being made cache-aware separately.)
- Tests: backend `test_report_adapters.py` (compat default + new mutual case) / `test_matching_endpoints.py` / `test_public_profile.py` green; Flutter `match_report_screen_test.dart` + `share_report_test.dart` green; layout matrix updated + green. Docs: ARCHITECTURE §5.8. **Flutter changes ride the next build; backend (`main.py`, `report_adapters.py`, `public_profile.py`) needs an `ayma-api` redeploy.**

## Voice agent: cascade proactive-greeting HTTP 400 on inference LLM (meta-hackathon, 2026-10-29) — DONE
- Sentry (`ayma-agent`, only still-firing live issue, 37 events, first seen Oct 26): LiveKit Inference LLM returned `HTTP 400: "messages must contain at least one message with role user or tool"` from `inference/llm.py::_run` via `voice/generation.py::_llm_inference_task`.
- **Root cause:** on a **cascade** session (text chat LLM over the OpenAI-compatible protocol — LiveKit `inference.LLM`, or Meta Muse via `lk_openai.LLM`) an **agent-initiated** opener/greeting with no prior user turn built a **system-only** message list. `session.generate_reply(instructions=…)` adds the instruction as a *system* message, and LiveKit's OpenAI provider format (`_provider_format/openai.py::to_chat_ctx`) accepts but never applies `inject_dummy_user_message` (the google/anthropic/aws formats do), so the request reached the gateway with zero user/tool messages → 400. Realtime stacks (Gemini Live / Inference `RealtimeModel`) don't hit that request shape, which is why only the cascade/inference path fired.
- **Fix (`agent.py` + `prompt.py` + `prompts/voice_session_seed.md`):** every cascade proactive opener/greeting seeds one benign synthetic user turn via `generate_reply(user_input=prompt.load_voice_session_seed())`. Threaded as `seed_user_turn` through `_open_main_call` (welcome greeting + silent-caller opener), `maybe_open_conversation`, and the Practice Date greeting (`meta_cascade` only); `_start_main_cascade_session` passes `seed_user_turn=True`. Realtime paths leave it False → byte-identical opener/greeting. Seed text is a system cue the persona is told not to read aloud.
- Tests: `test_agent.py` +5 regression (cascade opener seeds a user turn and the composed OpenAI messages carry ≥1 user/tool message; welcome greeting seeded on cascade / unseeded on realtime; practice greeting seeded on cascade / unseeded on realtime; cascade cold-start test now asserts `seed_user_turn=True`). Full `functions/bootstrap` suite green. Docs: ARCHITECTURE §13.9 (opener section). **Backend/agent — needs an `ayma-agent` redeploy to stop the live errors (see commit/report for the revision).**

## Feature tour: root overlay + nav-aware controller (persist across pages) (meta-hackathon, 2026-10-29) — DONE [[todo-tour-root-overlay]]
- Founder (2nd attempt) wanted the tour to be a floating dialogue that **stays open as it walks across pages** — the old overlay lived inside `MainShellScreen`'s `Stack`, so pushing a sub-page (My story / Appearance / Ayma's settings) buried it and the callout vanished.
- **Root overlay (Bug B).** New `TourHost` (`lib/widgets/tour_host.dart`) mounts **one** `CoachMarkOverlay` in `MaterialApp.router`'s `builder` **above the Navigator**, driven by a new `TourController` (`ChangeNotifier` singleton, `lib/services/tour_controller.dart`). It survives every route push. The shell now only *decides* to start (`_maybeShowFeatureTour`/`_consumeTourRequest` → `TourController.start`); it no longer holds overlay state or mounts the overlay. Back-to-skip moved to a root `BackButtonListener` in `TourHost`; the shell's `PopScope` only walks tab history now.
- **Nav-aware per step.** `onSwitchTab(int)` → `onPrepareStep(TourFeature)`: before each step the host pops any pushed sub-page back to the shell (`appRouter.go('/')`) so the shell's keyed widgets are live, then switches to the step's `tab` via `TabRequest`, then the engine re-acquires the anchor on the new page. Measure retry raised to `maxMeasureAttempts` frames (default 40 ≈ 0.6s) to ride a route-pop transition; tests inject a small cap.
- **Orb anchor (Bug A).** Confirmed (again) the `home_orb` key is wired semantically to the real centre orb (`AymaTabBar` tab 0), NOT the Messages/envelope icon — the earlier on-device "envelope/bell" report was a stale pre-fix build. Locked in with a regression test (`home_orb` resolves to an `AymaOrb`, the Messages key does not, and the orb rect sits right of the Messages rect).
- **Centering (Bug C).** Callout is explicitly centred horizontally (centred `Align` + `maxWidth: 400`) and scrolls internally (`SingleChildScrollView`) so it never overflows at text scale 2.0.
- Tests: `coach_mark_overlay_test.dart` +3 (orb-anchor, persists-across-route-push, no-overflow@2.0); full `flutter test` + layout matrix green; `flutter analyze` clean. On-device verified on emulator-5554 (see HACKATHON screenshots). Docs: ARCHITECTURE §11.2 diagram + §11.2a, AGENT_HANDOFF. **Flutter-only — rides the next build, no deploy.**

## AI date-sim: Continue + anti-hallucination grounding (meta-hackathon, 2026-10-29) — DONE
- **Continue the conversation.** New `POST /date-sim/{sim_id}/continue {more_messages}` → `date_sim.continue_simulation`: reads the already-streamed transcript, rebuilds both personas from the same snapshots/grounding, resumes the alternation at the next absolute `seq` (CONTINUES, never restarts), streams the extra turns into the same Firestore doc, and re-scores over the FULL transcript. Shared `_generate_turns`/`_finalize` with `run_simulation`; same metering, hang guards, opt-out re-check and mid-run out-of-credits handling. Flutter: "Keep the conversation going" button (`dateSimContinueButton`) on the result screen beside the primary "Message `<name>` for real"; `ApiClient.dateSimContinue`; the live doc flips `done→running→done` so the typing row + refreshed score/Taste appear in place. "Simulated — not sent" framing intact, nothing sent to the match.
- **Anti-hallucination grounding.** `prompts/date_sim.md` now forbids *inventing* unknown facts (job/employer/industry/title/school/hometown/location/history) — distinct from the existing disclosure filter — and deflects vaguely for unknowns. Readiness-conditioned: `compose_sim_prompt` injects a `{{CONTEXT_DEPTH}}` fragment from the speaker's avatar-readiness (`persona_snapshot.readiness_for_context`) — thin profile → stricter `date_sim_depth_thin.md`, rich → `date_sim_depth_full.md`. Fixes sparse profiles fabricating a confident invented life. Existing hard non-disclosure of PRIVATE details unchanged.
- Tests: backend `test_date_sim.py` 31 green (new: continue append+rescore, continue opt-out, missing-sim, anti-fabrication prompt, thin-vs-rich conservatism); Flutter `date_sim_test.dart` 15 green (continue append+rescore, continue 402 sheet); `flutter analyze` clean; layout matrix green (date-sim live done footer unchanged shape). Docs: ARCHITECTURE §18 (prose + §18 diagram + endpoint table) + this line + HACKATHON_STATUS.
- Flutter rides APK 2235 (no APK built here per congestion). Backend deployed (see HACKATHON_STATUS for the ayma-api revision).

## Feature tour: press-through + Profile sub-steps + centering fix (meta-hackathon, 2026-10-29) — DONE [[todo-tour-pressthrough-fix]]
- Founder on-device reported three tour bugs: spotlight blocked taps (not interactive); the Profile step just pointed at the Profile tab; summary cards weren't centered. (Also audited: the voice step correctly spotlights the center orb — confirmed in isolation + on device; the earlier "highlights the bell" report was from a stale build.)
- **Press-through:** `CoachMarkOverlay` now lets the cutout pass taps through to the real widget (`_HoleGate` `RenderProxyBox` reports no hit inside the cutout) while a translucent `Listener` observes the tap and advances the tour — tapping the highlighted anchor does its real action AND advances. The dimmed scrim still absorbs taps; Skip/Next/Done still advance without tapping the anchor.
- **Profile walkthrough:** new `profile_hub` step (tap the Profile tab → opens Profile + advances) then sub-steps on tab 3 spotlight the real rows — `your_story`→My story, `appearance_report`→Appearance, `ayma_models`→Ayma's settings — each scrolled into view (`Scrollable.ensureVisible`). New anchors in `TourAnchors` + `ProfileTabScreen._Item.anchorId`.
- **Centering:** non-anchored summary callouts center on screen; anchored callouts sit in the opposite half (`Align` + shrink-wrapping scroll-safe `ListView`) so they never cover the cutout and never overflow at small widths.
- **Replay fix:** the shell now listens to `TourRequest` (`_consumeTourRequest`), so Settings "How to use Ayma" / Dev "Reset & preview" replay the full tour even when the shell is already mounted (`go('/')` had been popping back without rebuilding, so the gate never re-ran).
- Tests: `coach_mark_overlay_test.dart` +2 (press-through advances + scrim absorbed); gate/seen-state/shell suites green; **layout matrix green (73 across 3 shards)**; `flutter analyze` clean. On-device verified on emulator-5554 (auto-show, Profile tab press-through opens Profile, callouts centered). Docs: ARCHITECTURE §11.2a, HACKATHON_STATUS. **Flutter-only — rides the next build, no deploy.**

## DM translate offered "Translate" on messages already in the reader's language (meta-hackathon, 2026-10-29) — DONE
- Founder (on-device screenshot): short English DMs ("hello", "hey sexy", "nice buttt") each showed a "Translate" link while a longer English message correctly showed "Already in your language" and a Malayalam message auto-translated. Root cause: `_TranslatableText` offered "Translate" as the resting default for every incoming text bubble with no language awareness; short/ambiguous Latin text was never confidently resolved to the reader's language, so it defaulted to offering Translate.
- Fix (two layers). Backend (`functions/bootstrap/dm_translate.py`, prompt `prompts/dm_translate.md`): the model now also returns a detection `confidence`; `translate()` only surfaces a translation when the message is confidently in a *different* language — `_should_surface_translation` + `_dominant_script`/`_script_of_lang`: different writing system → always translate (reliable), same script (Latin↔Latin) → translate only when `confidence >= 0.75` and detected != target, else return the original. Client (`ayma_flutter/lib/utils/dm_language.dart` + `dm_thread_screen.dart`): script detection decides whether to even offer — different-script → offer "Translate" immediately; same-script → quiet one-shot backend detect (offer only when confidently foreign, translation cached + revealed on tap; the reader's own language shows nothing); emoji/number noise → never offered.
- Tests: `functions/bootstrap/tests/test_dm_translate.py` (17, incl. same-language short+long → no translate, different-script short → translate, high-confidence Latin foreign → translate, low-confidence short Latin → no translate); `ayma_flutter/test/dm_language_test.dart`; widget cases in `dm_translate_test.dart` (English → no Translate; short Malayalam → Translate + translates on tap); `flutter test` incl. `layout_matrix_test.dart` green. Docs: ARCHITECTURE §6.12. **CODE+TESTS+PUSH + deploy ayma-api (backend changed); APK carries the Flutter half on the next build.**
## Conversations/DM: list threads + open-last/fuzzy + chip-only-on-success (meta-hackathon, 2026-10-29) — DONE
- Founder (on-device): Ayma couldn't say who had messaged them, couldn't "open the last conversation", and showed a false "Opened that conversation" chip when the open failed ("Sahil not found").
- **Fix 1 — `list_conversations` (new tool, `skills/app_control.py`):** reads the signed-in user's own `dm_threads` (`participants array_contains uid`, drops `hidden_for` self), returns each other-person name + recency + unread flag, most-recent first; never message bodies. Registered in BOTH text (`COMMAND_SKILLS` → `make_session_tool_fns`) and voice (`VOICE_SKILLS` → `make_livekit_tools`). Answers "who has messaged me?" / "who's in my conversations?".
- **Fix 2 — open-last/fuzzy (`open_message_thread`):** accepts "last"/"most recent"/"latest" (opens the most recently active thread) and a partial/fuzzy name (best-match + close-spelling fallback); asks which when >1 is equally plausible instead of silently failing. Resolves to a concrete uid server-side, so the existing client `open_dm_thread` path is unchanged.
- **Fix 3 — chip only on real navigation (`tool_labels.py`):** nav tools (`open_message_thread`/`open_screen`/`open_profile`/`open_match_report`/`search_explore`) earn their "Opened …" chip only when the result starts with `navigated:`; otherwise `outcome_label` returns `NO_CHIP` → text drops it from `tools_used` (gemini.py), voice sends an empty-label end `tool_used` event that clears the "…ing" indicator and queues no chip (agent.py). No more false success chip.
- Tests: `test_app_control.py` +8 (list ordering/own-user-only/hidden/none, open-last, fuzzy, ambiguous-asks-which, name-not-found no-chip), new `test_tool_labels.py` (nav-chip gating, list labels); existing open-thread + registry tests updated. Backend suite green except known base failures (`test_vendor_spend`, `test_agent_keepalive`, `test_app_registry`).
- **Backend-only — no APK** (client already handles `open_dm_thread`; chip is server-driven). **Deploy: ayma-api (text) + ayma-agent (voice tools changed).** Docs: ARCHITECTURE (screen-context block) + HACKATHON_STATUS + this line. **CODE+TESTS+PUSH+DEPLOY.**

## Taste backfill from existing context + all-three-domains priority (meta-hackathon, 2026-10-29) — DONE
- Founder (with screenshot): the in-app "Taste match · powered by Qloo" card showed his OWN taste with Music full (Billie Eilish, Bruno Mars, Russ, AC/DC) but **Film and Food EMPTY**, and the matched person had no taste page. Directive: "pull a LOT of context for the taste match — from the story, from other conversations — and fill this up fully. Ask ENOUGH questions about taste."
- **New third capture path — `taste_backfill.py`:** mines the user's ALREADY-EXISTING content — whole Story (all `wiki_pages` via `read_wiki_context`) **and** past voice + text conversation history (`chat_history._collect` over `chat_messages` + `raw_turns`) — with a dedicated Gemini extraction (`prompts/taste_extract.md`, model from `config.py`) and folds the result through the existing `merge_taste_mentions` (proper-name validation, dedup, cap 5, never clobbering a direct answer). Idempotent + cheap: a no-op (3 Firestore reads, no LLM) once all three domains have an entry; only a thin signal (any domain empty) runs the LLM. Best-effort, never blocks/fails a surface. Metered under new `token_ledger.FEATURE_TASTE_BACKFILL`, exempt from the credit gate like `wiki_ingest`.
- **Triggers:** fire-and-forget on `GET /completeness` + `GET /questionnaire` (`taste_backfill.schedule`); awaited for BOTH people inside `GET /match/{id}` just before `report_section` so the card fills same-render. Privacy: reads only each user's OWN private content; writes only names that user themselves stated, onto their OWN public Taste page — no cross-user private read reaches Qloo or another viewer.
- **Stronger ongoing extraction:** `prompts/wiki_ingest.md` taste rule made explicit about capturing ALL THREE domains (film/food are the ones most often missed; passing mentions count), so Film/Food fill from normal conversation going forward.
- **Ask enough taste questions (`question_tree.json`, tree version 4→5):** `taste_film`/`taste_food` weight 4→5 (now equal to `taste_music`), so answering one domain never crowds the other two out of `next_questions` — a user with Music filled but Film/Food empty gets Film + Food at the very top of `next`. Still the normal questionnaire flow (no separate form).
- Tests: `tests/test_taste_backfill.py` +8 (fills empty film/food from story+transcript, cheap no-op when full, force, no-source no-op, never-raises, thin-check, schedule), `tests/test_completeness.py` +1 (music-answered still surfaces film+food high). Full bootstrap suite + layout matrix green. Docs: QLOO_TASTE + ARCHITECTURE (§19 + diagram) + this line. **CODE+TESTS+PUSH + deploy ayma-api; no APK (card rendering unchanged).**

## Taste routing fix: film/food land on TASTE (not Interests) + one-sided match card (meta-hackathon, 2026-10-28) — DONE
- Founder (from on-device screenshots): films he discussed (Iron Man, I Robot, Terminator, WALL-E, Marvel, Friends) went into the **Interests** story, not the TASTE `Film:` line; Food never captured; Music worked. And the "Dillu" match card dead-ended ("Dillu hasn't added a public Taste page yet…").
- **Root cause (Issue 1):** two capture paths — direct `record_answer(taste_*)` → `_sync_taste_page`, and the greedy `wiki_ingest` wiki pass. `wiki_ingest` folded named films/food into the `interests` page (its description literally said "Hobbies, passions, **tastes**…"), and Ayma didn't reliably call `record_answer` for the deep-tier `taste_film`/`taste_food` nodes, so the structured Taste page's Film:/Food: stayed empty.
- **Fix (Issue 1):** `wiki_ingest` now returns a structured `taste: {music, film, food}` block of named favourites (prompt `prompts/wiki_ingest.md` routes them there, keeps `interests` for hobbies/activities only, and a narrative `taste` page op is dropped); `skills/questionnaire.py::merge_taste_mentions` appends them to the taste nodes (dedup, cap 5, never clobbering a direct answer) and recomposes the public Taste page. `wiki_schema.json` `interests` description tightened. So Film:/Food: fill from the user's film/cuisine mentions during the taste flow OR free chat; no duplication into Interests.
- **Fix (Issue 2):** `taste_match._resolve_pair` gives the one-sided empty state tailored, server-driven copy — when the VIEWER has taste but the match doesn't, it surfaces the viewer's own Music/Film/Food and "the moment {name} adds theirs, your shared tastes and date ideas appear here"; viewer-missing prompts them to add. Full dual-sided rendering unchanged. **Server-driven — no APK.**
- **Demo data:** all four demo profiles (maya/leo/aisha/noah) already carry FULL Music/Film/Food on BOTH sides — confirmed, no change needed.
- Tests: `test_questionnaire_skill.py` +3 (merge fills film/food, caps/dedup/drops prose, no-op), `test_wiki_ingest.py` +1 (routes named taste to the taste page, not interests; drops narrative taste op), `test_taste_match.py` updated +1 (one-sided surfaces viewer tastes). Full bootstrap suite green. Docs: QLOO_TASTE + ARCHITECTURE + HACKATHON_STATUS. **CODE+TESTS+PUSH + deploy ayma-api; no APK.**
## Appearance per-axis SINGLE-select + report loading state (meta-hackathon, 2026-10-29) — DONE [[todo-appearance-single-select]]
- Repeat-report bug, correctly root-caused to the CLIENT: `AppearancePreRunSheet`'s per-axis photo picker was MULTI-select while the backend (`appearance._pick`) reads exactly ONE photo per axis (the first selected in grid order). The sticky `auto_select` default stayed selected next to a newly-tapped photo and re-won, so every "Run analysis" re-analysed the same photo → identical report.
- Fix (Flutter, `skills_screen.dart`): per-axis **single-select** — tapping a photo makes it THE selected one, replacing any prior pick; added photos become the sole pick; first load defaults to the first `auto_select`; exactly one `media_id`/`image_path` per axis sent; Skip leaves a section out. Dead multi-select toggle removed.
- Founder fast-follow (same commit): **loading state** on `AppearanceReportScreen` — Re-run control spins (disabled) + "Updating…" banner while a run is in flight (`ReportEvents.started`/`stopped`/`isRunning`), clearing to the fresh report on completion.
- Backend unchanged (one selected id → deterministic `_pick`); added a focused test `test_single_explicit_selection_analyses_exactly_that_photo`.
- Tests: pre-run single-select assertions, report-screen loading-state test, backend test; `flutter analyze` clean, `flutter test` incl layout matrix green; `test_appearance.py` (52) green. **E2E PROVEN on emulator-5554** (Play-reviewer account): face selection A→B changed `face.source_photos`+`overall_score` between runs; loading state visible. Docs: ARCHITECTURE, HACKATHON_STATUS. **CLIENT CODE+TESTS+E2E+PUSH; rides next APK; no backend deploy.**
- Follow-up noted (NOT in scope here): an added-only axis selection (no grid photo chosen, the photo rides as a private `image_path`) is not analysed for face/body on a grid-less account — `_pick` with a non-None empty `only_media_ids` returns None and never falls back to `extra`. The intended selection path is grid photos (`face_media_ids`/`body_media_ids`); worth a later backend tweak so a single explicitly-added `image_path` can drive its own axis read.

## Taste questions asked early + fillable from completeness (meta-hackathon, 2026-10-28) — DONE
- Founder: let users add tastes from the profile-completeness page, and bump the taste questions' priority so they're asked EARLY (not buried) — so taste data gets populated and the Qloo "Taste match" stops showing empty. **No new form** — reuse the existing questionnaire/completeness path end-to-end.
- **Priority bump (`question_tree.json`, tree version 3→4):** `taste_music` weight 3→5 (now top tier), `taste_film`/`taste_food` 3→4.
- **Promoted section (`completeness.py`, `_EARLY_PAGE_TYPES = {"taste"}`):** `_section_gating` makes `taste` always unlocked and transparent to the sequential core-first gate — it no longer waits behind every earlier section's core and never locks the sections after it. Net effect: `taste_music` surfaces at ~#2 in `next` (right after the routing node; identity basics come from preboarding and aren't re-asked), so `next_questions` hands it to Ayma early, and the **"Tastes (music, film & food)"** section shows as an open, fillable topic on the server-driven complete-profile screen. Anti-repeat cooldown unchanged.
- Answering any taste node still writes the PUBLIC `wiki_pages/taste` page via `_sync_taste_page` in the `Music:/Film:/Food:` shape `taste_match.parse_taste` reads (own-profile only, public by design). `taste_film`/`taste_food` unlock once `taste_music` is answered.
- **No Flutter change** — `complete_profile_screen.dart` is fully server-driven from `questionnaireTree()`. Label clarified to "Tastes (music, film & food)" in `question_tree.PAGE_TYPE_LABELS`.
- Tests: `test_completeness.py` +4 (promoted/early + transparent-gate + film/food unlock), `test_questionnaire_skill.py` +1 (`next_questions` offers taste early). Full questionnaire/completeness/taste suites green; layout matrix green. Docs: ARCHITECTURE + QLOO_TASTE. **CODE+TESTS+PUSH + deploy ayma-api; no APK.**

## Public-profile proposal dedup/suppress/apply-current + Story live-refresh (meta-hackathon, 2026-10-28) — DONE [[todo-proposal-dedup-story-refresh]]
- **BUG A (backend, `functions/bootstrap/profile_manual.py`):** a surgical story edit left the "Public profile updates" screen showing a duplicate card for one line (correct new text + previous wrong text), and "Apply all" wrote the stale one. Root cause: the one-shot `suppress_next_proposal` guard was consumed by the first ingest write (even a no-op) so a later lagging re-ingest re-proposed/reverted the user's edit; and Apply rebuilt the public text from the proposal's frozen `before`/`after` regardless of the live text.
  - `route_ai_update`: guard is spent only on the first rewrite that actually *diverges* from the user's text (never on a no-op/private/first-write pass). A newer rewrite already replaces the section's prior card → one card per line, latest wins.
  - `list_proposals`: drops a proposal whose frozen base no longer matches the live public text (`_live_matches_decisions`) → stale/duplicate cards self-heal on read.
  - `_load_proposal`: refuses + clears a stale proposal on Apply/Apply-all/dismiss (404) → never regresses a line to an older proposed value.
- **BUG B (Flutter, rides consolidation APK 2233):** the open Story page didn't refresh after a chat/voice story edit. `TranscriptStore` (shared chat `resolvePending` / voice `applyVoiceSegment` tool-completion seam) now calls `report_events.dart` `signalStoryEditFromTools`, bumping `story_private`/`story_ai_notes`/`story_public` so `LivingReportView` re-fetches live.
- **Privacy/security:** own-profile only; applying targets the live text (can't leak or regress another user's data); firing a story report event only triggers a same-report re-fetch.
- Tests: `test_profile_manual.py` +4; `transcript_store_test.dart` +4; backend suites green except known base failures (`test_vendor_spend`, `test_agent_keepalive`); Flutter layout matrix green. **Deploy: ayma-api only (BUG A); no APK, versionCode unchanged.** Docs: ARCHITECTURE (§534-adjacent proposal flow, §11.6c report refresh, §13.6 edit_story_entry), HACKATHON_STATUS, this line.

## Appearance/body report completion notification + freshness stamp + standardized loader (meta-hackathon, 2026-10-28) — DONE [[todo-report-ready-notify]]

- **Backend (ayma-api):** `appearance.run_appearance` → `notifications_triggers.notify_report_ready` fires an in-app `report_ready` notification on a user-initiated run (`source != "auto"`) that persists; deep-links `appearance/{report_id}`, one rolling dedup entry, neutral "ready" preview (body-only → "Your body analysis is ready"). Fired at the persist point so it reaches a user who left the screen. Not for the silent background reconcile (`source=="auto"`) or an unchanged-photos rerun.

If the app is KILLED mid-request the run may not finish, so no report/notification is produced. A **fully durable server-side background job** (enqueue the run, survive client death, notify on completion) is a larger follow-up — do when prioritized. [[todo-durable-report-background-run]]

PUBLIC profile data only, both people — no private/cross-user private reads. Tests: backend `test_taste_match.py` 16 green, Flutter `taste_match_report_section_test.dart` 5 green. CODE+TESTS+PUSH only (no APK/deploy/versionCode bump — final integration does that).
- **DONE (meta-hackathon, 2026-10-28) — collect taste in the questionnaire [[todo-onboarding-taste-questions]]:** founder ("this should be part of our questionnaire"). Three `page_type: "taste"` nodes are now in the question tree (`question_tree.json` v3, branch `all`: `taste_music` core, `taste_film` / `taste_food` deep), asked by Ayma in the normal flow. On any taste answer `skills/questionnaire.py::_sync_taste_page` composes the user's PUBLIC `wiki_pages/taste` page in the `Music: … / Film: … / Food: …` shape `parse_taste` reads (written straight to `ai_body`, public by design like the seeded profiles). So a pair who both answered get a real taste score instead of the `no_taste` empty state — previously only the 4 seeded demo profiles + anyone who hand-added a Taste page had taste data. No Flutter change (complete-profile screen is server-driven); backend-only deploy. Tests: `test_taste_match.py` end-to-end (two fresh users → real pairing), `test_questionnaire_skill.py` (compose + parse, visibility preserved). Docs: ARCHITECTURE §19 + diagram, QLOO_TASTE.md § "Where the Taste page comes from".

**DONE (meta-hackathon, 2026-10-28) — report share: one native "Share", per-app WhatsApp/Instagram buttons removed [[todo-report-share-native-only]]:** founder (repeatedly): the "Share via WhatsApp"/"Share via Instagram" **direct** buttons didn't work while the native OS share sheet (reached via "More…") shared to those apps fine. Fix: deleted the per-app buttons and their broken intent/channel code; the Appearance report card's **single "Share" button now opens the native OS share sheet directly** (`report_card_share.dart` → `SharePlus.share` with the card PNG + `justabot.info/get` smart-link caption). Removed: the intermediate `report_card_share_sheet.dart`, the WhatsApp/Instagram/Save-image targets, `CardShareTarget`/`ShareTargetNotInstalled`, the `ayma/share` `sendToApp`/`instagramStory` method channel + native `MainActivity.kt` helpers, `ShareService.shareToInstagramStory` and `Env.facebookAppId`. The web-research/Match report sheet (`share_report_sheet.dart`) drops "Instagram Story" and renames "More…" → "Share" (keeps in-app "Share to Ayma"/"Send to a user"). Gallery "Save image" (image viewer, `media_save` channel) kept. Tests green (`appearance_report_card_test`, `share_report_test`, `layout_matrix_test`); `flutter analyze` clean. CODE+TESTS+PUSH only — no APK, versionCode unchanged (rides the next consolidation build). Docs: ARCHITECTURE report-card note, HACKATHON_STATUS.

**IN PROGRESS / FLAGGED (meta-hackathon, 2026-10-28) — move the voice agent to LiveKit Cloud [[todo-v2-move-agent-to-livekit-cloud]]:** `ayma-agent` can now run on **Cloud Run (default)** OR **LiveKit Cloud Agents**, chosen per user by the `voice_agent_cloud` preference (`cloudrun` default | `livekit`), default OFF so production voice is unchanged. `/bootstrap` reads the pref and routes the room by **explicit agent dispatch** to `ayma-lk` when `livekit` (`RoomConfiguration`/`RoomAgentDispatch`); the Cloud Run worker keeps **automatic** dispatch and declines `livekit`-flagged rooms via a `request_fnc` guard (fail-open) so the two never contend. Same `agent.py`, switched by `AGENT_RUNTIME`; the LiveKit worker loads Firestore from a least-privilege SA key (`LK_AGENT_FIRESTORE_SA` in Secret Manager — a durable credential; SA `lk-agent-firestore@ayma-ai`, role `datastore.user`). Dev toggle: Developer screen → "Voice agent cloud" (`GET/POST /voice/agent-cloud`). **LiveKit Cloud deploy SUCCEEDED** 2026-10-28: agent `CA_bJMNunTLpVqk` (region us-east, project `aimma-wt34nu3l`), worker **registered as `ayma-lk`**, status Running. Artifacts: `Dockerfile.livekit`, `livekit.toml`, `scripts/deploy-agent-livekit.sh`. Runbook + flip-the-default plan: `docs/LIVEKIT_AGENT_MIGRATION.md`. Backend-only deploy (ayma-api for the endpoint/dispatch; Cloud Run ayma-agent redeploy for the guard before anyone opts in). No APK/versionCode change (dev toggle ships in appearance-fix APK 2230). Tests: `test_voice_agent_cloud.py` (flag resolution, endpoint, explicit-dispatch routing), bootstrap/parity green, layout matrix green.

**DONE (meta-hackathon, 2026-10-28) — story edit direct, no passage popup [[todo-story-edit-direct-no-popup]]:** `edit_story_entry` / `remove_story_entry` (`skills/story_manage.py`) are now wired into the VOICE tool set too (`make_livekit_tools`), not just text, so Ayma can change/soft-remove a section of the user's own story on a call. A "change / fix / reword / remove / delete … my story" intent routes to the direct tool *before* `open_screen` can navigate (new `story_edit_actions` in `app_registry.json` + `find_story_edit_action`), so she edits in place instead of navigating into the Story editor's "Ask Ayma" passage popup (founder bug: "it shouldn't open that pop-up, it should just update it directly"). Persona + `prompts/voice_tool_use.md` + `open_screen` description strengthened. Own-story-only (bound on `(db, uid)`). Backend + agent change; needs ayma-api + ayma-agent deploy together, no APK change.

Open design points for later: auth and scopes (OAuth/MCP server), per-user consent and data-sharing controls, rate limits and cost, moderation and safety of agent-to-agent chat, 18+ and biometric-consent gates still apply, disclosure when a conversation is agent-driven.
